int threadFunctionId1 = 0; 
int main (int argc, char *argv[])
{
	int error = 0; 
	int threadFunctionId1 = 0; 
	if (InitCVIRTE (0, argv, 0) == 0)
		return -1;	/* out of memory */
	if ((panelHandle = LoadPanel (0, "Pianoid_QM.uir", PANEL)) < 0)
		return -1;
	
	GetPanelHandleFromTabPage (panelHandle,PANEL_TAB, 0,&tabPanelHandle); 
	if(comport){CloseCom (comport);} 
	errChk (CreateUDPChannel(5555, &writerChannel));   
	
//	CmtScheduleThreadPoolFunction (DEFAULT_THREAD_POOL_HANDLE,ThreadFunction1, NULL,&threadFunctionId1);
	DisplayPanel (panelHandle);
	
	Init();
	
		//Initialize MIDI
	midErr = midiInOpen(&(hMIDhnd),nDev,(DWORD)&midProc,dwUserParam,CALLBACK_FUNCTION);
	midErr = midiInStart(hMIDhnd);	
	
	
	
	RunUserInterface ();
	CmtWaitForThreadPoolFunctionCompletion (DEFAULT_THREAD_POOL_HANDLE,
                                            threadFunctionId1,
                                            OPT_TP_PROCESS_EVENTS_WHILE_WAITING);
	 CmtReleaseThreadPoolFunctionID (DEFAULT_THREAD_POOL_HANDLE, threadFunctionId1); 
	 
	DiscardPanel (panelHandle);
	
	Error:
    if (writerChannel)
        DisposeUDPChannel(writerChannel);
    if (error < 0)
        MessagePopup("Error", GetGeneralErrorString(error));

	return 0;
}

void StopServer(void)
{
	QMX_Stop(SD, QMX_STOP_ALL);			// Остановим систему
	QMX_MOD_SetStartMode(SD, 0xFF, QMX_START_MODE_MANUAL); 									

	QMX_CircBufDetach(SD);              // Освободим виртуальный слот USB
										// Остановим сервер
	LaunchExecutableEx ("qmbox_ds.exe -s0", LE_SHOWNORMAL, &ServerHandle); 		
}
void CheckErr(void)
{
	char tmp_str[1024];
	
	if (QMX_GetLastError(SD, tmp_str, sizeof(tmp_str))==QMX_OK){ return;}
	else  
	{	
		printf("%s\n", tmp_str);
		QuitUserInterface (0);
			

	}
}	


void send_pitch(void)
{
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0};    
	int i,j,k,c,res;
	int temp[1056];
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	
			
			
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{
						for(j = 0; j < 3; j++)
						{
							for(k = 0;k < 4; k++)
							{    
								
								temp[12*i + 4*j +k] = Pitch[i][j][k];

							}
						}
				
					}
					;
			
					for(c = 0; c < 5;c++)
					{
			
						sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
						sendBuff[3]	 = CMD_recieve_ttn; 
						sendBuff[4]	 = c;
	
						k = 0;
						
							for(j = 0; j < 256; j ++)
							{
								
								if((j + c*256)==1056)break;
								res = temp[j + c*256]; 
								
								
							//	res = (int)val;
			
									lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
									mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 
									
									
								sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
								sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
								sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
								sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
								k+=4;
							}
							
		
						Length232 = k;
						sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
						sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
						sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
				//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
						UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 

					}

}

void Send_block_param(int mod_adr_shift,double data0[88])
{
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0};    
	int j,k,res;
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
			
						sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
						sendBuff[3]	 = CMD_recieve_ttn; 
						sendBuff[4]	 = 8 + mod_adr_shift;
	
						k = 0;
						
							for(j = 0; j < 88; j ++)
							{
								
							
							
								
								res = (int)data0[j];
			
									lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
									mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 
									
									
								sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
								sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
								sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
								sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
								k+=4;
							}
							
		
						Length232 = k;
						sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
						sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
						sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
				//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
						UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 

	
	
}


void send_mass(void)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j;
	int coef;
	int res;
								
	double decimat_100 =  100.0;
	double decimat_28 = 268435456.0;									   
	double decimat_31 =	2147483648.0;
	double decimat_30 = 4294967296.0 ;
	double decimat_24 = 16777216.0; 
	//double decimat_30 = 1073741824; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result; 
	double val;
	double ratio;
	double temp[128];
	
	

			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_Ci_out; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 202;
	
			k = 0;
		
			for(i = 0; i < 256 ; i ++) 
			{
				
					res = (int)(Mass[i]*decimat_31/(omega_coef[i]*omega_coef[i]));	
				//	res = (int)(Mass[i]*decimat_31/(omega_coef[i]));	
			
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  
	
		//	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
		//	FlushOutQ (comport);
			Delay(0.001);
			

}
void construct_molot(int not_num)
{
	double tt[1000];
		
	double tt_sdv[256];
	double ww;
	double ddel;
	double ddd,dx;
	double a,b,c, D;
	double X[1000],Y[1000], x_val;
	double secondDerivatives[1000];
	int i,j,k,colour;
	int val;
	int length,start_point;
	int numberOfElements;
	double xmax,xmin,ceil;
	int imax,imin;
	int mute,shift;
	int m0,m1,m2;
	
			ww = width[not_num];
			ddel = del[not_num];
			numberOfElements  = 1000;
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_102, &ceil);		
				for (i = 0; i < numberOfElements; i++)
				{
					
					c = 0.5 - (double)i/(double)numberOfElements;
					
					a = c*c - 2*ddel*c + ddel*ddel + ww*ww - 1;
					
					D = 4*ww*ww - 4*a;
					if(D < 0){D =0;}
					tt[i] = (2*ww + sqrt(D))/2;
					if(tt[i] < 0) tt[i] = 0;
					   
					
				}
			
			
		
		MaxMin1D(tt, numberOfElements, &xmax,&imax,&xmin,&imin); 
			
			if(xmax < -1.0*xmin){b = -1.0/xmin;}else{ b = 1.0/xmax; } 
			
						for(i = 0; i < numberOfElements; i ++)
						{
							tt[i] = tt[i]*b; 
						}
						
						for(i = 0; i < numberOfElements; i ++)
						{
							if(tt[i] > ceil) tt[i] = ceil;
						}
	
	   ////////////////////////////////////
		for(k = 0; k < 3; k++)
		{
		
			length =  Pitch[nota][k][0];   //length mashinka k
			
			if(length < 0){break;}
			
			start_point = Pitch[nota][k][3]; 
			m0 =  Pitch[nota][k][1];  
		
			 dx = (double)numberOfElements/(double)length;
		  
			  for(i = 0; i < length; i++)
			  {
				  out_array[i] = tt[(int)((double)i*dx)];
	  
			  }
			  for(i = 0; i < length; i++)
			  {
				  shape_512[m0][start_point + i] = out_array[i];
	  
			  }
			  
			Send_shape_512(m0,shape_512[m0]);
			
			if(Flag_Upload == 0)
			{
			
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0); 
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,(double)length); 
			
			  DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
		  		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, out_array,  length, VAL_DOUBLE,  
			     VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
				if(k == 0)
				{
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,(double)length); 
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
		  		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, shape_512[m0],  512, VAL_DOUBLE,  
			     VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW); 
				}
				if(k == 1)
				{
				
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_4, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,(double)length); 
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
		  		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, shape_512[m0],  512, VAL_DOUBLE,  
			     VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
				}
				if(k == 2)
				{
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_3, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,(double)length); 
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
		  		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, shape_512[m0],  512, VAL_DOUBLE,  
			     VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN); 
				}
			}
		}
		;					
	
}
void Send_shape_512(int mashinka,double shape[512])
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j;
	int cc,c;
	int coef;
	int res;
	int temp[1024];
	
	int all_single;
	double decimat_100 =  100;
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result; 
	double val; 
	int test_l;
	int limit;
	double fb_mult;
	
	
	
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_8, &all_single);
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_74, &fb_mult);
			
//			  DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
//		  PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, shape,  512, VAL_DOUBLE,  
//			     VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		  
		  		 fb_mult = 100000.0;
			  
						 	for(i = 0; i < 1024; i++){temp[i] = 0;}
							
							sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
							sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_shape; 
							sendBuff[4]	 = mashinka;
					  
								elements = 256;
					
								for(i = 0; i < elements; i++)
								{
									temp[i] = (int)(fb_mult*shape[i]);	
					  				shape_to_flash[mashinka][i] = temp[i];
								} 
	
							k = 0; 
					
							for(i = 0; i < elements ; i ++)
							{
				    
									res =   temp[i];    
					
									lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
									mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

								sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
								sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
								sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
								sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
								k+=4;

							}
		
							Length232 = k;
							sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
							sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
							sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
				
							UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  	    
		
							Delay(0.005);
							
							//////////////second part of 512
						 	for(i = 0; i < 1024; i++){temp[i] = 0;}
							sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
							sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_shape; 
							sendBuff[4]	 = mashinka + 101;
					  
								elements = 256;
					
								for(i = 0; i < elements; i++)
								{
									temp[i] = (int)(fb_mult*shape[i + 256]);	
					  				shape_to_flash[mashinka][i + 256] = temp[i]; 
								} 
	
							k = 0; 
					
							for(i = 0; i < elements ; i ++)
							{
				    
									res =   temp[i];    
					
									lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
									mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

								sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
								sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
								sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
								sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
								k+=4;

							}
		
							Length232 = k;
							sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
							sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
							sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
							UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  	    
							Delay(0.005);
							
				
					}
void Pitch_2_mashinka(void)

{
int i,j,k;
int temp[88];

for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
{
	temp[i] = 0;
	for(k = 0; k < 3; k++)
	{   if(Pitch[i][k][1]>=0)
		{
			if(Pitch[i][k][0] > 0)
			{
				
				mashinka_Pitch[Pitch[i][k][1]][0] = i;
			}
			else { mashinka_Pitch[Pitch[i][k][1]][temp[i]] = -1;} 
		}
	temp[i]++;
			
	}
	;
}
	
}
void send_shteg(int nota, int shteg_pos)
{
char sendBuff[20000] = {0}; 

int i,k;
int index;
int mashinka,num_str;
int c0,dat,cc;
unsigned int lo,mid,hi,hi_hi;
int addr0,addr1,addr2;
int val,length,start_point;	
	
	k = 7;
	for(i = 0; i < 3;i++)
	{
			mashinka = Pitch[nota][i][1];
			num_str  = Pitch[nota][i][2];
			length  =  Pitch[nota][i][0];
			start_point = Pitch[nota][i][3];
			mashinka2prog_command(mashinka);
			
			if(length > 0)
			{
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_str_tripple; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = Prog_Command;
			
			Addr232 = mode_addr_shift*4 + num_str;
			
				lo =  Addr232& 0x00FF;
				mid =  Addr232 >>8& 0xFF;
				sendBuff[k] = lo;
				sendBuff[k+1] = mid;
				
				
				val = start_point + length - shteg_pos;
				
				lo    = (val)      & 0x000000ff;
				mid   = (val >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
				hi    = (val>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
			//	hi_hi = (val>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 
				sendBuff[k+2] = lo;
				sendBuff[k+3] = mid;
				sendBuff[k+4] = hi;
			//	sendBuff[k+5] = hi_hi;

			Length232 = 5; 
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);				
			Delay(0.005);
			}
	}
				
}
void mashinka2prog_command(int mashinka)
{ 
	switch(mashinka)
	{
		case 0:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_0;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_0;
		break;
		case 1:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_1;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_1;
		break;
		case 2:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_2;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_2;
		break;
		case 3:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_3;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_3;
		break;
		case 4:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_4;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_4;
		break;
		case 5:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_5;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_5;
		break;
		case 6:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_6;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_6;
		break;
		case 7:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_7;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_7;
		break;
		case 8:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_8;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_8;
		break;
		case 9:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_9;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_9;
		break;
		case 10:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_10;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_10;
		break;
		
		case 11:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_11;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_11;
		break;
		case 12:
			Prog_Command =  CMD_param_12;
			Shape_Command = CMD_shape_12;
		break;
		case 13:

void Send_param_pack4(int nota, double data0,double data1,double data2)
{
	
char sendBuff[20000] = {0}; 

int i,k;
int index;
int mashinka,num_str,length;
int c0,dat,cc;
unsigned int lo,mid,hi,hi_hi;
int addr0,addr1,addr2;
int val;

			k = 7;
			mashinka = Pitch[nota][0][1];
			num_str  = Pitch[nota][0][2];
			if(mode_addr_shift == 0)
			{
					num_str = num_str - 1;
					if( num_str <0){num_str = 3;}
				
			
			}
			////////////Kostyl////////////
				
			
			length =   Pitch[nota][0][0];
			
			mashinka2prog_command(mashinka);   
			if (length > 0)
			{
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_str_tripple; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = Prog_Command;
			
			Addr232 = mode_addr_shift*4 + num_str;
			
				lo =  Addr232& 0x00FF;
				mid =  Addr232 >>8& 0xFF;
				sendBuff[k] = lo;
				sendBuff[k+1] = mid;
				
				
				val = (int)data0;
				
				lo    = (val)      & 0x000000ff;
				mid   = (val >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
				hi    = (val>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
			//	hi_hi = (val>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 
				sendBuff[k+2] = lo;
				sendBuff[k+3] = mid;
				sendBuff[k+4] = hi;
			//	sendBuff[k+5] = hi_hi;

			Length232 = 5; 
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);				
			Delay(0.01);
			
			}	
		
		mashinka =	Pitch[nota][1][1];
		length =   Pitch[nota][1][0];  
		if(length >= 0)
		{
			num_str  = Pitch[nota][1][2];
			if(mode_addr_shift == 0)
			{
				num_str = num_str - 1;
				if( num_str <0){num_str = 3;}
			}
			mashinka2prog_command(mashinka);
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_str_tripple; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = Prog_Command;
			
			Addr232 = mode_addr_shift*4 + num_str;
			
				lo =  Addr232& 0x00FF;
				mid =  Addr232 >>8& 0xFF;
				sendBuff[k] = lo;
				sendBuff[k+1] = mid;
				
				
				val = (int)data1;
				
				lo    = (val)      & 0x000000ff;
				mid   = (val >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
				hi    = (val>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
			//	hi_hi = (val>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 
				sendBuff[k+2] = lo;
				sendBuff[k+3] = mid;
				sendBuff[k+4] = hi;
			//	sendBuff[k+5] = hi_hi;

			Length232 = 5; 
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);				
			Delay(0.01);
		}
		   	mashinka =	Pitch[nota][2][1];
			length =   Pitch[nota][2][0];  
		if(length >= 0)
			{
			num_str  = Pitch[nota][2][2];
			if(mode_addr_shift == 0)
			{
				num_str = num_str - 1;
				if( num_str <0){num_str = 3;}
			}
			mashinka2prog_command(mashinka);
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_str_tripple; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = Prog_Command;
			
			Addr232 = mode_addr_shift*4 + num_str;
			
				lo =  Addr232& 0x00FF;
				mid =  Addr232 >>8& 0xFF;
				sendBuff[k] = lo;
				sendBuff[k+1] = mid;
				
				
				val = (int)data2;
				
				lo    = (val)      & 0x000000ff;
				mid   = (val >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
				hi    = (val>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
			//	hi_hi = (val>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 
				sendBuff[k+2] = lo;
				sendBuff[k+3] = mid;
				sendBuff[k+4] = hi;
			//	sendBuff[k+5] = hi_hi;

			Length232 = 5; 
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);				
			Delay(0.01);
			}
}
void send_stacatto(int nota,int vol,double delay)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0}; 
	
				
				sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
				sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
				sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
				sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
				sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
				sendBuff[7]	 = nota; 
				sendBuff[8]	 = vol; 
			
		
		
				sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
		//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
				UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
				
				Delay(delay);
					
					sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
					sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
					sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
					sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
					sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
					sendBuff[7]	 = nota; 
					sendBuff[8]	 = 0; 
			
		
		
					sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
			//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);
					UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);	
	
}

void Send_udp_single(int res, int command,int command_1)
{
char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = command; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = command_1; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  	
		
			Delay(0.001); 	
}
void write_sector(int sector_num)
{
	int i,j,cc,k;
	int res;
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi;
			for(cc = 0; cc < 4; cc++)
			{
						sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
						sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_write; 
						sendBuff[4]	 = cc;
	
						k = 0;
						
							for(j = 0; j < 256; j ++)
							{
								
								
								res = array_to_send[cc*256 +j]; 
								
							//	res = (int)val;
			
									lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
									mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 
									
									
								sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
								sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
								sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
								sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
								k+=4;
							}

		
						Length232 = k;
						sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
						sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
						sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
				//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
						UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 
						
						Delay(0.02); 
			}
			
			
			   Delay(0.1); 
				sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
				sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_sdvig_in;					
				sendBuff[4]	 = sector_num;			 
		
				
				sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
				sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
				sendBuff[7]	 = 0; 
				sendBuff[8]	 = 0; 
		
				sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
				UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10); 
				Delay(0.1); 
				
	
	
}


void send_udp_256(int command, int offset, int temp[256])
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j,res;
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = command; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = offset;
	
			k = 0;
			for(i = 0; i < harm_num ; i ++)
			{
				res = temp[i];
		
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;

			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  

			Delay(0.005);
	
}

void send_udp_88(int command, int offset, int temp[88])
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j,res;
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = command; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = offset;
	
			k = 0;
			for(i = 0; i < 88 ; i ++)
			{
				res = temp[i];
		
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}

			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;

			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  

			Delay(0.005);
	
}

		 

void scroll_wheel(int event,int st_num)
{
	double val;
	
	
	switch( st_num)
	{
	case 0://e
		scroll_step = step_e;
	break;
	case 1://d
		scroll_step = step_d;
	break;
	case 2://a
		scroll_step = step_a;
	break;
	}

		
		if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			if(event ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				scroll_step = scroll_step*10;	
			}
			if(event ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				scroll_step = scroll_step*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, active_cntrl,scroll_step);  
			 
		
		
			switch( st_num)
			{
			case 0://e
				step_e = scroll_step;
			break;
			case 1://d
				step_d = scroll_step;
			break;
			case 2://a
				step_a = scroll_step;
			break;
			}
		
	 } 	////
		
	else
	{
		
		if(event ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
		{
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,active_cntrl,&val);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, active_cntrl,val + scroll_step); 
		}
	
		if(event ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
		{
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,active_cntrl,&val);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, active_cntrl,val - scroll_step); 
		}
	
	}
	
	
}
void prepare_FIR(void)
{
	
	double integral_R,integral_L,multiplier;
	int i;
				
				integral_R = 0; 
				integral_L = 0;
				for(i = 0; i <  Fir_length*3;i++)
				{
					integral_R =  integral_R + impulse_resp_Right[i]*impulse_resp_Right[i];
					
					integral_L =  integral_L + impulse_resp_Left[i]*impulse_resp_Left[i];
				}
				
				multiplier = sqrt(integral_R/integral_L);
				
				for(i = 0; i <  Fir_length*3;i++)
				{
				
					impulse_resp_Left[i] = impulse_resp_Left[i]*multiplier;	
				}
				
				
				
			  				
				for(i = 0; i < Fir_length; i++)
				{
					
					impulse_resp_R[i] = impulse_resp_Right[i]	;
					
					impulse_resp_R1[i] = impulse_resp_Right[i + Fir_length - 21]	;
					
					impulse_resp_R2[i] = impulse_resp_Right[i + 2*(Fir_length - 21)]	; 
					
	//				impulse_resp_R3[i] = impulse_resp_Right[i + 3*(Fir_length - 21)]	;
					
					
					impulse_resp_L[i] = impulse_resp_Left[i]	;
					
					impulse_resp_L1[i] = impulse_resp_Left[i + Fir_length - 21]	;
					
					impulse_resp_L2[i] = impulse_resp_Left[i + 2*(Fir_length - 21)]	; 
					
	//				impulse_resp_L3[i] = impulse_resp_Left[i + 3*(Fir_length - 21)]	;
					
					
				}

				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,128.0); 
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,128.0); 
				
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
				
				PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_GRAPH,impulse_resp_Left,3*Fir_length, VAL_DOUBLE,
	    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);
				
				PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_GRAPH,impulse_resp_Right,3*Fir_length, VAL_DOUBLE,
	    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
				
	
	
}
void send_ttn(void)
{
	int nnn;
	int i;
	
			for( nnn = lo_nota_limit; nnn < hi_nota_limit; nnn++)
			{
				i = nnn;
				
				mode_addr_shift = 0;
				Send_param_i(i,ttn[i],ttn[i],ttn[i]);
				   if(nnn >= start_dupple_note)
				   {
					mode_addr_shift =  1;
					Send_param_i(i,ttn[i] + dt[i],ttn[i] + dt[i],ttn[i] + dt[i]);
						if(nnn >= start_tripple_note)
						{
						
							mode_addr_shift =  5;
							Send_param_i(i,(ttn[i] - dt[i]),(ttn[i] - dt[i]),(ttn[i] - dt[i]));
							
						}
				
				   }
			}

}
void send_ttn_Q(void)
{
	int nnn;
	int i;
	
			for( nnn = lo_nota_limit; nnn < hi_nota_limit; nnn++)
			{
				i = nnn;
				
				mode_addr_shift = 0;
				Send_param_i(i,ttn_Q[i],ttn_Q[i],ttn_Q[i]);
			
				   if(nnn >= start_dupple_note)
				   {
					mode_addr_shift =  1;
					Send_param_i(i,ttn_Q[i] + dt[i],ttn_Q[i] + dt[i],ttn_Q[i] + dt[i]);
				
						if(nnn >= start_tripple_note)
						{
						
							mode_addr_shift =  5;
							Send_param_i(i,(ttn_Q[i] - dt[i]),(ttn_Q[i] - dt[i]),(ttn_Q[i] - dt[i]));
						 
						}
				
				   }
			}

}

void update_tab(void)
{
	int xc,yc,voice_n,param_n; 
	
	
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,&voice_n);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_FLAG,&param_n);
	
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DXI,&xc);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DYI,&yc);
			
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_VOICE_0,voice_vol[0][xc][yc]);
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_VOICE_1,voice_vol[1][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_VOICE_2,voice_vol[2][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_VOICE_3,voice_vol[3][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_VOICE_4,voice_vol[4][xc][yc]); 
				
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_V0,voice_nota[0][xc][yc]);
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_V1,voice_nota[1][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_V2,voice_nota[2][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_V3,voice_nota[3][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_V4,voice_nota[4][xc][yc]);
				
				
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_GLISS_0,voice_gliss[0][xc][yc]);
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_GLISS_1,voice_gliss[1][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_GLISS_2,voice_gliss[2][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_GLISS_3,voice_gliss[3][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_GLISS_4,voice_gliss[4][xc][yc]);
				
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NOISE_0,voice_noise[0][xc][yc]);
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NOISE_1,voice_noise[1][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NOISE_2,voice_noise[2][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NOISE_3,voice_noise[3][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NOISE_4,voice_noise[4][xc][yc]); 
			
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_SINGLE_0,voice_single[0][xc][yc]);
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_SINGLE_1,voice_single[1][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_SINGLE_2,voice_single[2][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_SINGLE_3,voice_single[3][xc][yc]); 
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_SINGLE_4,voice_single[4][xc][yc]); 
			
			
			
	
	
}

void code_surface(void)
{
	unsigned int a,b,c,d,e,f;
	unsigned int temp0, temp1, temp2, temp3,result;
	int i,j,k;
	
	for(k = 0; k < Max_voice_num; k++)
	{
		for(j = 0; j < Max_Cell_x; j++)
		{
			for(i = 0; i < Max_Cell_y; i++)
			{
				 voice[k][i][j].flag = voice_reset_tn[k][i][j]<<1|voice_single[k][i][j]<<5|voice_gliss[k][i][j]<<6|voice_noise[k][i][j]<<7; 
				 voice_flag[k][i][j] = voice[k][i][j].flag;
				
				temp0 = voice_nota[k][i][j]&0x000000FF;
				temp1 = voice_vol[k][i][j]&0x000000FF;
				temp2 = voice_point[k][i][j]&0x000000FF;
				temp3 = voice_flag[k][i][j]&0x000000FF;
				
				
				result = (temp0 << 8)   | temp1;
				result = (result << 8) | temp2;
				result = (result << 8) | temp3;
				
				
				 surface_int[k][i][j] =  result ;
			
			}
			
		}
		
	}
	
	
}



void send_sell(int x, int y)
{
	int voice_n,Xi_c,Yi_c,i; 
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0};   
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi;
	
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI,&Xi_c);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI,&Yi_c);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,&voice_n);
			
			   			
						
			for(i = 0; i < Max_voice_num; i++)
			
			{
				////////////////////////////////////////////////			
							sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
							sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_edit_voice; 
							sendBuff[4]	 = i; 
							sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
							sendBuff[6]	 = 0x08; 
							
							lo    = (Xi_c*32)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (Xi_c*32 >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (Yi_c*32)      & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (Yi_c*32 >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
							
			

							hi_hi   = (surface_int[i][Xi_c][Yi_c])      & 0x000000ff;
							hi 		= (surface_int[i][Xi_c][Yi_c] >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
						 	mid    	= (surface_int[i][Xi_c][Yi_c]>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							lo 		= (surface_int[i][Xi_c][Yi_c]>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[11] = lo;
						sendBuff[12] = mid;
						sendBuff[13] = hi;
						sendBuff[14] = hi_hi;
						sendBuff[15]  = 0x23;
			//	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 12);
						
						UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 16);
						Delay(0.005);
	/////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
						
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_flash_prog_on_off ; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
							lo    = (Xi_c*32)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (Xi_c*32 >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (Yi_c*32)      & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (Yi_c*32 >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
						sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
			//			ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 12);
						UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  
						
			//			Delay(0.01);
						
				
						

	}
	
}

void code_sell(void)
{
	int voice_n,Xi_c,Yi_c; 
	unsigned int a,b,c,d,e,f;
	unsigned int temp0, temp1, temp2, temp3,result;
	int i,j,k;
	
	
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI,&Xi_c);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI,&Yi_c);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,&voice_n);
			
			a = voice_reset_tn[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
			b =voice_single[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
			c = voice_gliss[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
			d = voice_noise[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
	
	
				 voice[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c].flag = a<<1|b<<5|c<<6|d<<7; 
				 
				 voice_flag[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = a<<1|b<<5|c<<6|d<<7; 
				
				
				temp0 = voice_nota[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c]&0x000000FF;
				temp1 = voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c]&0x000000FF;
				temp2 = voice_point[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c]&0x000000FF;
				temp3 = voice_flag[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c]&0x000000FF;
				
				
				result = (temp0 << 8)   | temp1;
				result = (result << 8) | temp2;
				result = (result << 8) | temp3;
				
				
				 surface_int[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] =  result ;
	
	
}

void code_cell(int Cell_x,int Cell_y)
{
	unsigned int a,b,c,d,e,f;
	unsigned int temp0, temp1, temp2, temp3,result;
	int i,j,k;
	
	for(k = 0; k < Max_voice_num; k++)
	{
		j = Cell_x; 
		
		i = Cell_y;
		
				 voice[k][i][j].flag = voice_reset_tn[k][i][j]<<1|voice_single[k][i][j]<<5|voice_gliss[k][i][j]<<6|voice_noise[k][i][j]<<7; 
				 voice_flag[k][i][j] = voice[k][i][j].flag;
				
				temp0 = voice_nota[k][i][j]&0x000000FF;
				temp1 = voice_vol[k][i][j]&0x000000FF;
				temp2 = voice_point[k][i][j]&0x000000FF;
				temp3 = voice_flag[k][i][j]&0x000000FF;
				
				
				result = (temp0 << 8)   | temp1;
				result = (result << 8) | temp2;
				result = (result << 8) | temp3;
				
				
				 surface_int[k][i][j] =  result ;
		
	}
}


void decode_surface(void)
{
	unsigned int a,b,c,d,e,f;
	int i,j,k;
	
	for(k = 0; k < Max_voice_num; k++)
	{
		for(j = 0; j < Max_Cell_x; j++)
		{
			for(i = 0; i < Max_Cell_y; i++)
			{
				 voice_nota[k][i][j] = (surface_int[k][i][j]&0xFF000000) >> 24 ;
				 voice_vol[k][i][j]  = (surface_int[k][i][j]&0x00FF0000) >> 16 ; 
				 voice_point[k][i][j]= (surface_int[k][i][j]&0x0000FF00) >> 8 ;
				 voice_flag[k][i][j] = (surface_int[k][i][j]&0x000000FF)  ;
				 
				 
				 voice[k][i][j].nota = voice_nota[k][i][j];
				 voice[k][i][j].vol = voice_vol[k][i][j];
				 voice[k][i][j].point = voice_point[k][i][j];
				 voice[k][i][j].flag = voice_flag[k][i][j];
				 
				 
				 if(voice[k][i][j].flag & (1 << 1)) //reset_ttn 
				 { voice_reset_tn[k][i][j] = 1;}
				 else{voice_reset_tn[k][i][j] = 0;} 
				 
				 if(voice[k][i][j].flag & (1 << 7)) //molot_noise 
				 { voice_noise[k][i][j] = 1;}
				 else{voice_noise[k][i][j] = 0;} 
				 
				 if(voice[k][i][j].flag & (1 << 6)) //glissando 
				 { voice_gliss[k][i][j] = 1;}
				 else{voice_gliss[k][i][j] = 0;} 
				 
				 if(voice[k][i][j].flag & (1 << 5)) //nota_single 
				 { voice_single[k][i][j] = 1;}
				 else{voice_single[k][i][j] = 0;}  
				 
				 

			
			}
			
		}
		
	}
	
	
}


void Send_Force_vozb(int dynamic, int okt_num)
{
///	
char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j;
	double temp_double[harm_num];   
	int temp[harm_num];
	int decimat_23 = 4194304;
	int offs;
	
	
	switch(dynamic)
	{
		
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_55,&pp); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_56,&p); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_57,&mf); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_58,&f); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_68,&ff);  
		
		case 0:
			
			for(i = 0; i < harm_num; i ++)
			{
				temp_double[i] =pp*Force_vozb[dynamic][okt_num][i];
				temp[i]  =  (int)(decimat_23*temp_double[i]);
	
			}
		
			temp[0] = 0; 
			temp[255] = 0;
						
			offs = 0;
			send_udp_256( Transmit_Command, offs, temp); 
			
		break;
		
		case 1:
			
			for(i = 0; i < harm_num; i ++)
			{
				temp_double[i] =p*Force_vozb[dynamic][okt_num][i];
				temp[i]  =  (int)(decimat_23*temp_double[i]);
	
			}
		
			temp[0] = 0; 
			temp[255] = 0;
						
			offs = 2;
			send_udp_256( Transmit_Command, offs, temp); 
			
		break;
		
		case 2:
			
			for(i = 0; i < harm_num; i ++)
			{
				temp_double[i] =mf*Force_vozb[dynamic][okt_num][i];
				temp[i]  =  (int)(decimat_23*temp_double[i]);
	
			}
		
			temp[0] = 0; 
			temp[255] = 0;
						
			offs = 4 + okt_num;
			send_udp_256( Transmit_Command, offs, temp); 
			
		break;
		case 3:
			
			for(i = 0; i < harm_num; i ++)
			{
				temp_double[i] = f*Force_vozb[dynamic][okt_num][i];
				temp[i]  =  (int)(decimat_23*temp_double[i]);
	
			}
		
			temp[0] = 0; 
			temp[255] = 0;
						
			offs = 12 + okt_num;
			send_udp_256( Transmit_Command, offs, temp); 
			
		break;
		
		case 4:
			
			for(i = 0; i < harm_num; i ++)
			{
				temp_double[i] = ff*Force_vozb[dynamic][okt_num][i];
				temp[i]  =  (int)(decimat_23*temp_double[i]);
	
			}
		
			temp[0] = 0; 
			temp[255] = 0;
						
			offs = 20 + okt_num;
			send_udp_256( Transmit_Command, offs, temp); 
			
		break;
		
		
		
		
	}
	
	
	

	
	
	
	
}

void make_ci_spline(double x0,double x1,double x2,double y0,double y1,double y2,int num_elements,int interpType)
{

	int i;
	double Second_deriv[128];
	double 	x_val;
	double yError;
	double interp_val;
	
	
					X[0] = x0;
					X[1] = x1;
					X[2] = x2;
					
					Y[0] = y0;
					Y[1] = y1;
					Y[2] = y2;
					
					Spline (X, Y, 3, Y[0], Y[2], Second_deriv); 
					
						for(i = 0; i<  128; i ++)                                                    			
	                                                                                           			
							{	                                                                               			
								x_val = X[0] + i*(X[2] - X[0])/num_elements;                          			
	                                                                                           			
								if (interpType==0)                                                                 			
				                    PolyInterp (X, Y, 3, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);              			
				                if (interpType==1)                                                             			
				                    RatInterp (X, Y, 3, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);               			
				                if (interpType==2)                                                             			
				                    SpInterp (X, Y, Second_deriv, 3 , x_val , &interp_val);        			
	                                                                                           			
								CI_left_spline[i] = interp_val; 
                                                                                 			
							 }		
}

void constr_ci_spline(int str, 	double A_0, double A_nechet, double A_chet)
{
	int i,j,k,c;
	int ind;
	double a0,a1,a2,a3,a4,a5,a6,a7,a8,a9,a10,a11;
	int Harm;  
//	double A[12];
//	double B[12]; 
	
	double val_at_index[harm_num]; 
	double integral;
//	double A_0,A_nechet,A_chet;

	double xmax,xmin;
	double first_frq;
	int imax,imin;
	int first_harm;

	///
	int interpType, pointCount;
	double Second_deriv[128];
	double 	x_val;
	double yError;
	double interp_val;
	double plotbuff[128]; 
	
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RING,&interpType); 
			

	//		omega_coef[i] = 27.5*pow(2.0,(double)i/24.0); 
		
				for( j = 0; j <  NumString; j++)
					{	
							xmin = (omega_coef[str*2] - Notes_freqs[j])/Notes_freqs[j];
							if(xmin < 0){ xmin = -1.0*xmin;}
						    if(xmin < 0.01)
							{
								first_harm = 2*j ; 
							//	break;
							} 
					}
				
					first_frq = omega_coef[first_harm];
					
					
					
					    k = 1;
					   
						for( c = 0; c <  harm_num; c++)
						{
							frq_array[c] = ((double)k)*first_frq - omega_coef[c];
							if(frq_array[c] < 0){ frq_array[c] = -1.0*frq_array[c];} 
 
						}
						 MaxMin1D(frq_array, harm_num, &xmax,&imax,&xmin,&imin); 
						 
						 index[k -1] = imin;
						 
							 
							 	Ci_coeff_cos[str][imin] = A_0/omega_coef[imin]; 
							
								 val_at_index[k] = Ci_coeff_cos[str][imin];
						 
						 
					
			
					for( k = 2; k < 256;k++)
					{
			
						for( c = 0; c <  harm_num; c++)
						{
							frq_array[c] = ((double)k)*first_frq - omega_coef[c];
							if(frq_array[c] < 0){ frq_array[c] = -1.0*frq_array[c];} 
 
						}
						 MaxMin1D(frq_array, harm_num, &xmax,&imax,&xmin,&imin); 
						 
						 index[k-1] = imin;
						 
						 
							 	Ci_coeff_cos[str][imin] = A_0/omega_coef[imin]; 
							
						 
						 val_at_index[k-1] = Ci_coeff_cos[str][imin];
			
					 }
					
					
					for( c = imin; c < harm_num; c++)
					 {
							Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] = Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] + A_0/omega_coef[c]; 	 
					 }

					//////////////////////////
		
						if(index[0] > 0)
						{
							make_ci_spline(	
											0,
											(double)((index[0])/2),
											(double)(index[0]),
										
											 A[str][0],
											 B[str][0],
											 A[str][1]/A[str][0],
										
											128,
											interpType
										);
						
					
						for( c = 0; c < index[0]; c++)  	
					
							  {
								  ind = (int)(128*c/(index[0]));
							  
								  Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] = Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] + A[str][0]*CI_left_spline[ind]*Ci_coeff_cos[str][index[1]];  
							  }
					
						}
						
						
						make_ci_spline(	
										(double)(index[0]),
										(double)((index[0] + index[1])/2),
										(double)(index[1]),
										
										 A[str][1],
										 B[str][1],
										 A[str][2]/A[str][1],
										
										128,
										interpType
									);
					
					for( c = index[0]+1; c < index[1]; c++)  	
					
						  {
							  ind = (int)(128*(c - index[0]-1)/(index[1] - index[0]));
							  
							  Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] = Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] + A[str][1]*CI_left_spline[ind]*Ci_coeff_cos[str][index[1]];  
						  }
					
					
						make_ci_spline(	
										(double)(index[1]),
										(double)((index[1] + index[2])/2),
										(double)(index[2]),
										
										 A[str][2],
										 B[str][2],
										 A[str][3]/A[str][2],
										
										128,
										interpType
									);
					
					for( c = index[1]+1; c < index[2]; c++)  	
					
						  {
							  ind = (int)(128*(c - index[1]-1)/(index[2] - index[1]));
							  
							  Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] = Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] + A[str][2]*CI_left_spline[ind]*Ci_coeff_cos[str][index[1]];  
						  }
					
					
						make_ci_spline(	
										(double)(index[2]),
										(double)((index[2] + index[3])/2),
										(double)(index[3]),
										
										 A[str][3],
										 B[str][3],
										 A[str][4]/A[str][3],
										
										128,
										interpType
									);
					
					for( c = index[2]+1; c < index[3]; c++)  	
					
						  {
							  ind = (int)(128*(c - index[2]-1)/(index[3] - index[2]));
							  
							  Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] = Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] + A[str][3]*CI_left_spline[ind]*Ci_coeff_cos[str][index[1]];  
						  }
					
						make_ci_spline(	
										(double)(index[3]),
										(double)((index[3] + index[4])/2),
										(double)(index[4]),
										
										 A[str][4],
										 B[str][4],
										 A[str][5]/A[str][4],
										
										128,
										interpType
									);
					
					for( c = index[3]+1; c < index[4]; c++)  	
					
						  {
							  ind = (int)(128*(c - index[3]-1)/(index[4] - index[3]));
							  
							  Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] = Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] + A[str][4]*CI_left_spline[ind]*Ci_coeff_cos[str][index[1]];  
						  }
					

					
						make_ci_spline(	
										(double)(index[4]),
										(double)((index[4] + index[5])/2),
										(double)(index[5]),
										
										 A[str][5],
										 B[str][5],
										 A[str][6]/A[str][5],
										
										128,
										interpType
									);
					
					for( c = index[4]+1; c < index[5]; c++)  	
					
						  {
							  if((index[5] - index[4]) > 3)
							  {
							  ind = (int)(128*(c - index[4]-1)/(index[5] - index[4]));
							  
							  Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] = Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] + A[str][5]*CI_left_spline[ind]*Ci_coeff_cos[str][index[1]]; 
							  }
							  else
							  {
								break;	  
							  }
						  }	
					
					make_ci_spline(	
										(double)(index[5]),
										(double)((index[5] + index[6])/2),
										(double)(index[6]),
										
										 A[str][6],
										 B[str][6],
										 A[str][7]/A[str][6],
										
										128,
										interpType
									);
					
					for( c = index[5]+1; c < index[6]; c++)  	
					
						  {
						  	if((index[6] - index[5]) > 3)
							  {
							  ind = (int)(128*(c - index[5]-1)/(index[6] - index[5]));
							  
							  Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] = Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] + A[str][6]*CI_left_spline[ind]*Ci_coeff_cos[str][index[1]];
								}
								else
								{
								 break;
								}
						  }					
					
					
					

											make_ci_spline(	
															(double)(index[6]),
															(double)((index[6] + index[7])/2),
															(double)(index[7]),
										
															 A[str][7],
															 B[str][7],
															 A[str][8]/A[str][7],
										
															128,
															interpType
														);
					
					for( c = index[6]+1; c < index[7]; c++)  	
					
						  {
							  if((index[7] - index[6]) > 3)
							  {
							  ind = (int)(128*(c - index[6]-1)/(index[7] - index[6]));
							  
							  Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] = Ci_coeff_cos[str][c] + A[str][7]*CI_left_spline[ind]*Ci_coeff_cos[str][index[1]];  
							  }
							  else
							  {
								break;	  
							  }
						  }					
															
	/////////////////////end of make        
	
					
		/////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
					
					
					for( k = 1; k < 256;k++)
					{
			
						for( c = 0; c <  harm_num; c++)
						{
							frq_array[c] = ((double)k)*first_frq - omega_coef[c];
							if(frq_array[c] < 0){ frq_array[c] = -1.0*frq_array[c];} 
 
						}
						 MaxMin1D(frq_array, harm_num, &xmax,&imax,&xmin,&imin); 
						 
						 index[k-1] = imin;
						 
							 if((fmod((double)(k - 1),2.0)) > 0)
							 {
						 
							 	Ci_coeff_cos[str][imin] = A_chet/omega_coef[imin]; 
							 }
							 else
							 {
								Ci_coeff_cos[str][imin] = A_nechet/omega_coef[imin];  	 
							 }
						 
						
					 }
					
					
					
					integral = 0;
					for( j = 0; j < harm_num; j++) 
					{
						integral = integral + Ci_coeff_cos[str][j]*omega_coef[j]*omega_coef[j];  	
					}
					
					for( j = 0; j < harm_num; j++) 
					{
						if(str< 16)
						{
							Ci_coeff_cos[str][j] = 10000000.0*Ci_coeff_cos[str][j]/integral; 
						}
						else
						{
							if(str < 48)
							{
								Ci_coeff_cos[str][j] =10000000.0* Ci_coeff_cos[str][j]/integral; 	
							}
							else
							{
								if(str < 72)
								{
									Ci_coeff_cos[str][j] = 40000000.0*Ci_coeff_cos[str][j]/integral; 	
								}
								else
								{
									Ci_coeff_cos[str][j] = 80000000.0*Ci_coeff_cos[str][j]/integral; 
								}
								
							}
						}
					}
  			
	
}

void Draw_Shape(void)
{   
	int i;
	int str_num,elements; 
	double temp_array[256];    
	str_num = nota;
	
				
						elements = 256;
					   	SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,255.0); 
					
					
							   
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, shape_256[str_num],  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_FAT_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
						
						
	return;
				
}

void send_FIR_subr (void)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,j,k,cc;
	float val;
	int res;
//	double decimat_32 = 21048575;
	double decimat_32 =  1; //8388607; 
	double decimat_24 = 8388607; 
	
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;
	
	int temp[Fir_length];
	
			  GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_8,&val);
			  
			  
			  ///////////////////start R ///////////
			
			for(cc = 0; cc < 32; cc++)
			{
				
						sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
						sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_fir; 
						sendBuff[4]	 = cc;
	
						k = 0;
						
							for(j = 0; j < 256; j ++)
							{
								
								Float_Result.fl_val  = val*(float)(impulse_resp_R[cc*256 +j]);
								res = Float_Result.int_val; 
								
							//	res = (int)val;
			
									lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
									mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 
									
									
								sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
								sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
								sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
								sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
								k+=4;
							}

		
						Length232 = k;
						sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
						sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
						sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
				//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
						UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 
						
						Delay(0.02); ; 

			}
			
			
 
			
				sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
				sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_fir_on_off;					
				sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00;			 //R
		
				
				sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
				sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
				sendBuff[7]	 = 0; 
				sendBuff[8]	 = 0; 
			
		
		
				sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
			//	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
				UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10); 
				
				Delay(0.02); ;
				
				
				
			
			for(cc = 0; cc < 32; cc++)
			{
				
						sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
						sendBuff[3]	 = Transmit_Command; 
						sendBuff[4]	 = cc;
	
						k = 0;
						
							for(j = 0; j < 256; j ++)
							{
								
								Float_Result.fl_val  = val*(float)(impulse_resp_R1[cc*256 +j]);
								res = Float_Result.int_val; 
								
							//	res = (int)val;
			
									lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
									mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff;

								sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
								sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
								k+=4;
							}

						sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
						sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
						sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;

void Send_param(double data0,double data1,double data2)
{
	
char sendBuff[20000] = {0}; 

int i,k;
int index;
int c0,dat;
unsigned int lo,mid,hi,hi_hi;
int addr0,addr1,addr2;
int val;

			k = 7;
			Com232_0 = cmd0_str_tripple;   
	  		nota_to_addr(nota); 
			
			
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_str_tripple; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = Prog_Command;
			
				lo =  Addr232& 0x00FF;
				mid =  Addr232 >>8& 0xFF;
				sendBuff[k] = lo;
				sendBuff[k+1] = mid;
				
				
				val = (int)data0;
				
				lo    = (val)      & 0x000000ff;
				mid   = (val >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
				hi    = (val>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
			//	hi_hi = (val>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 
				sendBuff[k+2] = lo;
				sendBuff[k+3] = mid;
				sendBuff[k+4] = hi;
			//	sendBuff[k+5] = hi_hi;

			Length232 = 5; 
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);				
			Delay(0.005);
			
			
	
 return ; 	
}

void Send_param_i(int i,double data0,double data1,double data2)
{
	
char sendBuff[20000] = {0}; 

int k;
int index;
int c0,dat;
unsigned int lo,mid,hi,hi_hi;
int addr0,addr1,addr2;
int val;

			k = 7;
			Com232_0 = cmd0_str_tripple;   
	  		nota_to_addr(i); 
			
			
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_str_tripple; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = Prog_Command;
			
				lo =  Addr232& 0x00FF;
				mid =  Addr232 >>8& 0xFF;
				sendBuff[k] = lo;
				sendBuff[k+1] = mid;
				
				
				val = (int)data0;
				
				lo    = (val)      & 0x000000ff;
				mid   = (val >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
				hi    = (val>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
			//	hi_hi = (val>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 
				sendBuff[k+2] = lo;
				sendBuff[k+3] = mid;
				sendBuff[k+4] = hi;
			//	sendBuff[k+5] = hi_hi;

			Length232 = 5; 
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);   
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);				
			Delay(0.005);
			
			
	
 return ; 	
}
void update_modes_amp(double plotbuff[128],int moda,int switch_mode)
{
	int i,nn,ni;
	int val,mod;


	double mult;
	double max,min;
	int imax,imin;
	
			if(switch_mode == 0)
			{
			
			
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{
				
						nn = (int)(i/2);
						ni = i - nn*2;
						
						plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][moda + ni*128];
				//		plotbuff_1[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][mod + ni*128]; 
						

					}
				
				MaxMin1D (plotbuff, 88,&max, &imax, &min, &imin);
				
				if( max < -1.0*min)
				{
					modes_amps[moda] = -1.0*min;
					mult = -1.0/min;
				}
				else
				{
					modes_amps[moda] = max;
					mult = 1.0/max;
				}
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
					modes_extr[moda][i] = mult*plotbuff[i]; 
					
				}
			}
			else
			{
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{
				
						nn = (int)(i/2);
						ni = i - nn*2;
						
						plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][moda + ni*128];
				//		plotbuff_1[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][mod + ni*128]; 
						

					}				
				MaxMin1D (plotbuff, 88,&max, &imax, &min, &imin);
				
				if( max < -1.0*min)
				{
					modes_amps_str[moda] = -1.0*min;
					mult = -1.0/min; 
				}
				else
				{
					modes_amps_str[moda] = max;
					mult = 1.0/max; 
				}
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
					modes_extr_str[moda][i] = mult*plotbuff[i]; 
					
				}
				
			}
			
	
	
}

void Init(void)
{
	
int i,j;
	for (i = 0; i < 12; i++)
	{
	  step[i] = 1;
	}
	 step[4] = 0.0001; 
	 step[3] = 0.00001;  
	 step[12] = 0.00001;
	 step[13] = 0.01; 
	 step[8] = 0.0001;
	 step[14] = 0.01; 
	 step[15] = 0.01; 
	 step[16] = 0.01; 
	 step[17] = 0.1;
	 step[18] = 100; 
	 step[19] = 0.0000001;
	 step[20] = 1000;
	 step[21] = 1000;
	 step[22] = 1000;
	 
	 flag_velocity = 0;
	 
	 step_e = 0.1;
	 step_d = 0.001;
	 step_a = 0.01; 
	 MUTE_mode = 0;
	 xint_prev = 0;
	 for (i = 0; i < 256; i++)
		{
		  mute_amps[i] = 1;
		}
	 
	for(i = 0; i < 16; i++)
		{
		tr_led[i] = 1;
		}
	
		for(i = 0; i < 14;i++)
		{
				GetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle,btn_list_exibition[i],ATTR_LEFT,&btn_list_attr[0][i]);
				GetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle,btn_list_exibition[i],ATTR_TOP,&btn_list_attr[1][i]);
		
		}
		
	
	 
}

void send_cmd_midi(int note,int volume)
{
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi;
	sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
					sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
					sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
					sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
					sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
					sendBuff[7]	 = note; 
					sendBuff[8]	 = volume; 
			
		
		
					sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
			//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);
					UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
	
}
static void CALLBACK midProc(HMIDIIN hMidiIn,UINT wMsg,DWORD dwUserParm,DWORD dwParam1,DWORD dwParam2)
{	
	
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;  
	char buf[16];
	static char *label;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
	
	int p1 = (float)( (dwParam1>>24)&0xFF ); 
	int p2 = (float)( (dwParam1>>16)&0xFF );
	int p3 = (dwParam1>>8)&0xFF;
	int p4 = (dwParam1)&0xFF;
	int p5 = (dwParam1)&0xFFF;
	
	int p10 = (float)( (dwParam1>>24)&0xFF ); 
	int p11 = (float)( (dwParam1>>16)&0xFF );
	int p12 = (dwParam1>>8)&0xFF;
	int p13 = (dwParam1)&0xFF;
	int led_force;
	
	float noise_vol;
	
	
	float place = 5;
	int time_milliseconds = dwParam2;
	int pv;
	int pc;
	int okt;
	int i,j,k;
	int prev_p2 = 0;
	unsigned int a,d;
	int    data,command; 
	int ni,nn;
	double plotbuff[256];
	double plotbuff_1[256]; 
	
	double harm_buff[256]; 
	
	double harm_s_buff[256];  
	int switch_mode;
	int harm_index[10];
	
	double ind_disp, vol_disp,tail_disp;
	
	double a1,b1,x,y;
	int val;
	int xc,yc;
	
	int count;
	
	
	int disp_on_off,display,mode_number;  
	
	int res;
	
	if( p4==254 ) // Timer
	return;

if (1)
	{
	 //   SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_2, p2); 
		
	 if( dwParam1!=0 )
	 {
		 
/*		 
		label = StrDup ("");
		Fmt (buf, "%s<%i", dwParam1); 
		AppendString(&label, buf, -1); 
		AppendString(&label, " p3", -1);
		Fmt (buf, "%s<%i", p3);
		AppendString(&label, buf, -1); 
		AppendString(&label, " p4", -1);

						switch(res)

							case 0:
								ind_disp = ind_mult_0[nota];
								vol_disp = ind_vol_0[nota];
								tail_disp = ind_tail_0[nota];
							break;
							case 1:
								ind_disp = ind_mult_1[nota];
								vol_disp = ind_vol_1[nota];
								tail_disp = ind_tail_1[nota]; 
							break;
							
							case 2:
								ind_disp = ind_mult_2[nota];
								vol_disp = ind_vol_2[nota];
								tail_disp = ind_tail_2[nota]; 
							break;
							
							case 3:
								ind_disp = ind_mult_3[nota];
								vol_disp = ind_vol_3[nota];
								tail_disp = ind_tail_3[nota]; 
							break;
							
							case 4:
								ind_disp = ind_mult_4[nota];
								vol_disp = ind_vol_4[nota];
								tail_disp = ind_tail_4[nota]; 
							break;
						}
						
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ, ind_disp);
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_2, vol_disp); 
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_3,tail_disp);  
						ind_disp = 242000.0*1/(ind_disp*65000.0);
			
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC, ind_disp);
				//		Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
						
				
					}								   
		
		////end disp on	
			}
			
			
			/////////////
			
			//////////////////
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_NOISE,&val);
			if(val > 0)
			{
				if(split_on_off > 0)
				{
					if(split_order <1)
					{
			
						if(nota < split_nota){p2 = p2 + val*128; }
					}
					else
					{
						if(nota > split_nota){p2 = p2 + val*128; }  	
					}
				}
				else { p2 = p2 + val*128;}
			
			}
			
			flag_nota_on[nota] = p2; 
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TUNE_MODE,&val); 
			
//			send_single_molot(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_90, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
			
				if(forbid_notes_send == 0)
				{
			
					sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
					sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
					sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
					sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
					sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
					sendBuff[7]	 = nota; 
					sendBuff[8]	 = p2; 
			
		
		
					sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
			//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);
					UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
					
	
	 				if(val == 1)
					{
						Delay(0.001);
				
						sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
						sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
						sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
						sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
						sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
						sendBuff[7]	 = nota + 12; 
						sendBuff[8]	 = p2;
						
						sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
						
						UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10); 
						
								
						
					}
					else
					{
	
							if(val == 2)
							{
							
								Delay(0.001);
				
								sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
								sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
								sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
								sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
								sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
								sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
								sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
								sendBuff[7]	 = nota + 24; 
								sendBuff[8]	 = p2;
						
								sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
						
								UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10); 						
						
							}
							else
							{
								
								if(val == 3)
								{
									Delay(0.001);
				
									sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
									sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
									sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
									sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
									sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
									sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
									sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
									sendBuff[7]	 = nota - 12; 
									sendBuff[8]	 = p2;
						
									sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
						
									UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10); 
						
									
								}
		
								
								
							}
							
						
					}
		
				}
			
//		FlushOutQ (comport);	
	
		}
	
	if( p4 == 128) 
		{ 
			
			if(forbid_notes_send == 0)
				{
					GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TUNE_MODE,&val);
			
					p3=p3-21;  //-40    
					nota = p3;
			
					nota = nota + shift_nota_val;

					if(nota < 0){ return;}
					if(nota >87){ return;} 
			

					if(p3 < 0){ return;}
					if(p3 >87){ return;}
			
					sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
					sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
					sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
					sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
					sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
					sendBuff[7]	 = nota; 
					sendBuff[8]	 = 0; 
			
		
		
					sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
		//			ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
					UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
					flag_nota_on[nota] = 0;
					
					if(val == 1)
					{
					
						sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
						sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
						sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
						sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
						sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
						sendBuff[7]	 = nota + 12; 
						sendBuff[8]	 = 0; 
			
		
		
						sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
			
						UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);					
						
					}
					
					else
					{
						if(val == 2)
						{
							sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
							sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
							sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
							sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
							sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
							sendBuff[7]	 = nota + 12; 
							sendBuff[8]	 = 0; 
			
		
		
							sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
				//			ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
							UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
					
							sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
							sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
							sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
							sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
							sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
							sendBuff[7]	 = nota + 24; 
							sendBuff[8]	 = 0; 
			
		
		
							sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
				//			ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
							UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);					
						
						}
			
						else
						{
						if(val == 3)
						{
								sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
								sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
								sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
								sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
								sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
								sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
								sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
								sendBuff[7]	 = nota + 12;

								sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
					//			ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
								UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
					
								sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
								sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
								sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
								sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
								sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
								sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
								sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
								sendBuff[7]	 = nota  - 12; 
								sendBuff[8]	 = 0; 
			
		
		
								sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
					//			ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
								UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);					
						
						}	
						}
					}
				
			
	
			
		}
	 
		}
	

		return;

	return;
	}
}

				}

					mask ^= (0 << i);	
				}
			}

	mode_num = 0;
	
 		while(1)

	 	{
	
		
        	if(KeyHit())
  			{
			letter = GetKey();
			if(letter == 768)
			{
				flag_audio = 0;
				break;  // esc
			}
			
 			/*
			if(letter == 1536)		// ^
			{
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_DISPLAY_MODE,&int_val);
				frq = frq + step;

			if(letter == 1792)  // -^

				frq = frq - step;
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_FRQ,frq);

				omega_coef[int_val] = frq;
				
			}
			
			if(letter == 2048)  // <
			{
				vol = vol - step_A;
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_VOL,vol);
				out_vol_out(panelHandle,PANEL_VOL,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			}
			if(letter == 2304)   // >
			{
				vol = vol + step_A;
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_VOL,vol);
				out_vol_out(panelHandle,PANEL_VOL,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			}
			
			if(letter == 256)  // delete

			delete_peak(panelHandle,PANEL_DELETE_PEAK,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  	
			}
			
			if(letter == 32)  // spase
			{
				
			mode_num++;
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_NUM,mode_num);
				
				
			}
			if(letter == 120)  // x
			{
				
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_NUM,&num);

				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_NUM,num);

			}
			
			if(letter == 122)  // z

				Disp_mode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_NUM,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				
			}
			
			if(letter == 97)  // a

				if ((!CurrentPtr)&&(!DataPtr))break;

				QMS_data_0[ch] = Data[CurrentPtr];
				QMS_data[ch] = Data[CurrentPtr + 1];
									
				
				cc++;
		
				result[ch][elements] = ((double)(QMS_data[ch] * 4 + QMS_data[ch +1]))* CC_Adc.Scale + CC_Adc.Offset; 
			
			   if(ch == (num_ch*2-1))elements++;

			}
			
 		if(elements !=0)
		{ 
			

			for(j =0; j< num_ch; j++)

				for(i = 0; i< elements; i++)

					result_d[j][i] = 1.0*result[2*j][i];
				
				}

				    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
				   
				   elements = elements - imax - 100;
				//   elements = 300;   
				   for(i = 0; i < elements;i++)
				   {
					   plotbuff[i] = result_d[0][imax - 100 +i];
				   
				   }
				   
						PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_MODE_2,plotbuff,elements, VAL_DOUBLE,
			    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
				   
				   
				   	 for(i = 0; i < elements;i++)
					 {
						plotbuff[i] = xmax*exp(-1.0*Q_coeff[mode_num]*(double)i*dt); 
		 
					 }

			
			 	
			 
						PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_MODE_2,plotbuff,elements, VAL_DOUBLE,
			    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
			   }
			   		
			}
			 
			 
	//		 Q_measure_flag = 0;
	//		 SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_Q_MEASURE,Q_measure_flag);
	//		 SetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_ELEMENTS,elements); 
		}
		
	 }
		 
	StopServer();
	GetKey(); 
	Sleep(500);
	
	
	return 0;
}

	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		flag_audio = 1;

	rms = 0;

	num_ach_freq = 0;
