int CVICALLBACK Reset (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
	int err;
	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON, &val);
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_reset; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x03; 
						   
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x1;
		
			sendBuff[7]	 = val;
		
			sendBuff[8] = 0x23;
	
      //  	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 9);	
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 9);

			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}




int CVICALLBACK Timer_start (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_2,&val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_TIMER,ATTR_ENABLED,val);

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Button_Tn (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char *char_val;
	double percents;
	int detune;
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("Tn");
			param_num = 0;
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TN,ttn[nota]);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_TN,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ttn,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
		
		
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_TN,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);  
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_23, &detune); 
		
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				ttn[nota] = ttn[nota] + eventData2*step[0];
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				ttn[nota] = ttn[nota] - eventData2*step[0];		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TN,ttn[nota]);
			
			mode_addr_shift = 0;
			if(detune == 1)
					{
					Send_param_pack4(nota,ttn[nota],ttn[nota],ttn[nota]);    	
					}
					else
					{
						Send_param_pack4(nota,(ttn[nota]),(ttn[nota] + dt[nota]),(ttn[nota] - dt[nota]));
					}
			
			Delay(0.005);
 		
			percents =  (100.0/ttn[nota])*dt[nota];
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_PERCENTS,percents);
		 }
		 
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[0] = step[0]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[0] = step[0]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TN,step[0]);  
			 
		 }
		 
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
		 PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ttn,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
		 VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_TN,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_TN,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Button_D_Close (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char *char_val;
	
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("Decr close");
			param_num = 2;
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_D_CLOSE,decr_cl[nota]); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_D_CLOSE,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, decr_cl,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
			
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_D_CLOSE,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
	   GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);  
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				decr_cl[nota] = decr_cl[nota] + eventData2*step[1];
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				decr_cl[nota] = decr_cl[nota] - eventData2*step[1];		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_D_CLOSE,decr_cl[nota]);
			
			mode_addr_shift =  3;
			
			Send_param_pack4(nota,decr_cl[nota],decr_cl[nota],decr_cl[nota]);
		 }
		 
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[1] = step[1]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[1] = step[1]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_D_CLOSE,step[1]);  
			 
		 }
		 
		  DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, decr_cl,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_D_CLOSE,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_D_CLOSE,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Button_D_Open (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char *char_val;
	
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("Decr open");
			param_num = 1;
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_D_OPEN,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, decr_op,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
		
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_D_OPEN,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);  
	
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				decr_op[nota] = decr_op[nota] + eventData2*step[2];
				if( decr_op[nota] < 0){ decr_op[nota] = 0;}
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				decr_op[nota] = decr_op[nota] - eventData2*step[2];
				if( decr_op[nota] < 0){ decr_op[nota] = 0;} 
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_D_OPEN,decr_op[nota]);
			mode_addr_shift =  2;
			
			Send_param_pack4(nota,decr_op[nota],decr_op[nota],decr_op[nota]);
		 }
		 
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, decr_op,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[2] = step[2]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[2] = step[2]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_D_OPEN,step[2]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_D_OPEN,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_D_OPEN,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Button_Width (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char *char_val;
	
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE, 3);
			construct_molot(nota); 
				
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("Width");
			param_num = 3;
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_WIDTH,width[nota]); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_WIDTH,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0);
			 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, width,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_WIDTH,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);  
		
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				width[nota] = width[nota] + eventData2*step[3];
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				width[nota] = width[nota] - eventData2*step[3];		
			}
			
			if(width[nota] < -1.0) {width[nota] = -1.0;}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_WIDTH,width[nota]);
			construct_molot(nota); 
			

		 }
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, width,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[3] = step[3]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[3] = step[3]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_WIDTH,step[3]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_WIDTH,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_WIDTH,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Button_DEL (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
char *char_val;
	
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE, 3);
			//	display_m(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
		
				
		//		gen_test_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_91, EVENT_COMMIT, 0, 0, 0);
//		GenShape_4(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_87, EVENT_COMMIT, 0, 0, 0); 
							
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0);	
					
				
	//			Draw_Shape(); 
				
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("Del");
			param_num = 4;
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_DEL,del[nota]); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_DEL,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_DEL,del[nota]);
			construct_molot(nota);
			
			
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0);
			 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, del,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_DEL,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
		
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				del[nota] = del[nota] + eventData2*step[4];
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				del[nota] = del[nota] - eventData2*step[4];		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_DEL,del[nota]);
			construct_molot(nota);
	
		 }
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, del,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[4] = step[4]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[4] = step[4]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_DEL,step[4]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_DEL,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_DEL,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Button_D_tn (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char *char_val;
	double percents;
	
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("D_tn");
			param_num = 5;
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TN,dt[nota]); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_D_TN,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, dt,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
				
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_D_TN,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);  
		
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				dt[nota] = dt[nota] + eventData2*step[5];
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				dt[nota] = dt[nota] - eventData2*step[5];		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_D_TN,dt[nota]);
			
			mode_addr_shift = 0;
			
			Send_param_pack4(nota,ttn[nota],ttn[nota]+dt[nota],ttn[nota]-dt[nota]);
			Delay(0.005);
			
 			
			
			percents =  (100.0/ttn[nota])*dt[nota];
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_PERCENTS,percents);
			
		 }
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, dt,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[5] = step[5]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[5] = step[5]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_D_TN,step[5]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_D_TN,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_D_TN,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Button_DISP (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
char *char_val;
	
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("Disp");
			param_num = 6;
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_DISP,disp[nota]); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_DISP,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, disp,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
		
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_DISP,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);  
	
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				disp[nota] = disp[nota] + eventData2*step[6];
				if( disp[nota] < 0){ disp[nota] = 0;} 
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				disp[nota] = disp[nota] - eventData2*step[6];
				if( disp[nota] < 0){ disp[nota] = 0;}
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_DISP,disp[nota]);
			
			mode_addr_shift =  6;
			Send_param_pack4(nota,disp[nota],disp[nota],disp[nota]);
		 }
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, disp,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[6] = step[6]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[6] = step[6]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_DISP,step[6]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_DISP,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_DISP,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK draw_params (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double x,y;                     	
	static int xint; 
	double plotbuff[Fir_length];
	int i,led,pan,led_force;
	int nn,ni;
	int val;
	int ringslide2;
	int num_elements_to_display;
	int colour;
	 int activeCursorNumber;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED,&led);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_5,&led_force);  
			GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,1,&x,&y);
			
			if (led > 0)
			{
				if(param_num != 300)
				{

				xint=(int)x;                                                                   
				if(xint<0) xint=0;  
				
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_LOCK,-1.0,1.0);
				}
				else
				{
					
				}
			}
			break;
			
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK_UP:
				if(param_num == 570)
				{
					send_mass();
				}
				
	//		send_single_molot(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_90, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			break;
			
			
			
			
			
		case EVENT_VAL_CHANGED:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED,&led); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_5,&led_force); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_103,&event_counter);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_4,&val);     
			if (led == 0)
			{	
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,1,&x,&y);
			
				xint=(int)x;                                                                   
				if(xint<0) xint=0; 
				  
				
				switch (param_num)
				{
					case 560: // pedal curve
						num_elements_to_display =  128;
						colour = VAL_CYAN;
						if(xint > 127) xint= 127;
						pedal_out[xint] = (int)y;
						for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = (double)pedal_out[i];                                             			
						}
						
						
						
						
					break;
					case 0:
						if(xint > 87) xint= 87;
						ttn[xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = ttn[i];                                             			
						}
						
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_CYAN;
						
					break;
					
					case 1:
						if(xint > 87) xint= 87; 
						decr_op[xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = decr_op[i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_CYAN; 
						
					break;
					
					case 2:
						if(xint > 87) xint= 87; 
						decr_cl[xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = decr_cl[i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_CYAN; 
						
					break;
					
					case 3:
						if(xint > 87) xint= 87; 
						width[xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = width[i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_CYAN; 
						
					break;
					
					case 4:
						 if(xint > 87) xint= 87; 
						del[xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = del[i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_CYAN; 
						
					break;
					
					case 5:
						if(xint > 87) xint= 87; 
						dt[xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = dt[i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_CYAN; 
						
					break;
					
					case 6:
						 if(xint > 87) xint= 87; 
						disp[xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = disp[i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_CYAN; 
						
					break;
					
					case 7:
						 if(xint > 87) xint= 87;

						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = decr_disp[i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_CYAN; 
						
					break;
					
				  case 8:
							
							GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&ringslide2); 
							
							switch( ringslide2)
							{
								case 0:
									ind_mult_0[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_mult_0[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  88;
									colour = VAL_CYAN; 
								break;
								case 1:
									ind_mult_1[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_mult_1[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  88;
									colour = VAL_CYAN;
								break;
								case 2:
									ind_mult_2[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_mult_2[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  88;
									colour = VAL_CYAN;
								break;
								case 3:
									ind_mult_3[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_mult_3[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  88;
									colour = VAL_CYAN; 
								break;
								case 4:
									ind_mult_4[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_mult_4[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  88;
									colour = VAL_CYAN; 
								break;
							
							}
						
						
						
					break;

					case 10:
						
						GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&ringslide2); 
						
						switch( ringslide2)
							{
								case 0:
						
									ind_vol_0[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_0[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
									colour = VAL_GREEN;
							 	break;
								case 1:

									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_1[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
									colour = VAL_GREEN;
							 	break;
								case 2:
						
									ind_vol_2[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_2[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
									colour = VAL_BLUE;
							 	break;
								case 3:
						
									ind_vol_3[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_3[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
									colour = VAL_CYAN;
							 	break;
								case 4:

							 	break;

					break;

					break;
					
					case 12:
						
						
					break;

						  plotbuff[i] = NL_disp[i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_CYAN;
						
						
					break;
					case 20:
						GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36,&val); 
						
						nn = (int)(xint/2);
						ni = xint - nn*2;
						
						Ci_coeff_cos[nn][val + ni*128] = y;
						Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][val + ni*128] = y;

							nn = (int)(i/2);

						  plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][val + ni*128];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_MAGENTA;
						
						
					break;
					
					case 21:  //velocity
						
						velocity[xint]= y; 
						velocity[0]= 0;
						velocity[127]= 127;
						
						for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = velocity[i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  128;
						colour = VAL_CYAN;
						
					break;
					
					case 400:
						
						equal_loud[xint]= y;
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = equal_loud[i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  88;
						colour = VAL_CYAN;
						
						
						
					break;
					case 300: 
						
						fir_curve[xint]= y;
						
						
						
						for(i = 0; i < spectrum_window; i++) 
						{
							plotbuff[i] = fir_curve[i]*amplitudeSpectrum[0][i];	
						}
						
						
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
				
				                                                                                     						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  spectrum_window, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);  
						
						
						for(i = 0; i < spectrum_window; i++) 
						{
							plotbuff[i] = fir_curve[i];	
						}
						
						num_elements_to_display =  (int)spectrum_window;
						colour = VAL_CYAN;
						
						
						
						
						
					break; 
					
					case 301: 
						
						fir_curve[xint]= y;
						
						
						
						for(i = 0; i < 128; i++) 
						{
							plotbuff[i] = fir_curve[i];	
						}
						
						
					
						
						num_elements_to_display =  128;
						colour = VAL_MAGENTA;
						
							 
							for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
							{                                                                        			
							  
							  Ci_str_out[i] = Ci_out_temp[i]*fir_curve[i];
							  
						//	  Q_coeff[i] = Q_coeff_tmp[i]*fir_curve[i];
							  
							}
							
							send_ci_out(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_6,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
							
						//	Send_Q_coef(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_10,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
							
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
							
						
							
							
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Ci_str_out,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
						
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Ci_out_temp,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
							
					/*		
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Q_coeff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
						
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Q_coeff_tmp,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
						*/	
						
						
					break; 
					
					
					case 501: //exp_e[0][0]
						
						if(xint > 87){xint = 87;}
						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						
						exp_e[0][level][xint]= y;

						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;

						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						exp_e[1][level][xint]= y;

						  plotbuff[i] = exp_e[1][level][i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;

						  plotbuff[i] = exp_e[2][level][i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;

					case 504: //exp_e[3][0]
						if(xint > 87){xint = 87;}
						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}

						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;

					break;
					case 505: //exp_e[4][0]
						if(xint > 87){xint = 87;}
						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						
						exp_e[4][level][xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = exp_e[4][level][i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_CYAN; 
						
					break;
					
					///////////////
					
					case 506: //exp_d[0][0]
						if(xint > 87){xint = 87;}
						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						
						exp_d[0][level][xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)

						  plotbuff[i] = exp_d[0][level][i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_MAGENTA; 
						
					break;
					
					case 507: //exp_d[0][0]
						if(xint > 87){xint = 87;}
						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						
						exp_d[1][level][xint]= y;

						  plotbuff[i] = exp_d[1][level][i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_MAGENTA; 
						
					break;
					
					case 508: //exp_d[2][0]
						if(xint > 87){xint = 87;}
						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						
						exp_d[2][level][xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = exp_d[2][level][i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_MAGENTA; 
						
					break;
					
					case 509: //exp_d[3][0]
						if(xint > 87){xint = 87;}
						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						
						exp_d[3][level][xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)

						  plotbuff[i] = exp_d[3][level][i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_MAGENTA; 
						
					break;
					
					
					case 510: //exp_d[3][0]
						if(xint > 87){xint = 87;}
						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						
						exp_d[4][level][xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = exp_d[4][level][i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_MAGENTA; 
						
					break;
					
					///////////
					case 511: //exp_a[0][0]
						if(xint > 87){xint = 87;}
						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						
						exp_a[0][level][xint]= y;

						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_YELLOW; 
						
					break;

						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						
						exp_a[1][level][xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)

						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_YELLOW; 
						
					break;
					
					case 513: //exp_a[2][0]
						if(xint > 87){xint = 87;}
						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						
						exp_a[2][level][xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = exp_a[2][level][i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_YELLOW; 
						
					break;
					
					case 514: //exp_a[2][0]
						if(xint > 87){xint = 87;}
						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						
						exp_a[3][level][xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = exp_a[3][level][i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_YELLOW; 
						
					break;
					
					case 515: //exp_a[2][0]
						if(xint > 87){xint = 87;}
						if(xint < 0){xint = 0;}
						
						exp_a[4][level][xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = exp_a[4][level][i];                                             			
						}
						num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
						colour = VAL_YELLOW; 
						
					break;
					
					case 555:
						
						GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&ringslide2); 
						
 						switch( ringslide2)
							{
								case 0:
						
									ind_tail_0[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_tail_0[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
									colour = VAL_GREEN;
							 	break;
								case 1:
						
									ind_tail_1[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_tail_1[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
									colour = VAL_GREEN;
							 	break;
								case 2:
						
									ind_tail_2[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_tail_2[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
									colour = VAL_BLUE;
							 	break;
								case 3:
						
									ind_tail_3[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_tail_3[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
									colour = VAL_CYAN;
							 	break;
								case 4:
						
									ind_tail_4[xint]= y;                                                  			
									for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									  plotbuff[i] = ind_tail_4[i];                                             			
									}
									num_elements_to_display =  NumString;
									colour = VAL_RED;
							 	break;
							}
						
						
					break;	

					case 570:
						
						Mass[xint]= y; 
						 for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = Mass[i];                                             			
						}
						 num_elements_to_display =  elements;
						colour = VAL_CYAN;
						
					break;

				}
				
				if(param_num == 570)
				{
				   num_elements_to_display = elements;
				}
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,(double)num_elements_to_display);    
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
				
				                                                                                     						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, plotbuff,  num_elements_to_display, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, colour);  
				
						
			}			
			
			else
			{
				
				if(param_num != 300)
				{
						if(led_force == 1)
						{
							param_num = 200;
							GetActiveGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, &activeCursorNumber);
							GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,activeCursorNumber,&x,&y);
							X[activeCursorNumber - 1] = x;                                                          
							Y[activeCursorNumber - 1] = y;   
					
							Plot_spline (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_2,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
					
						}
						else
						{
							param_num = 201;
							GetActiveGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, &activeCursorNumber);
							GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,activeCursorNumber,&x,&y);
							X[activeCursorNumber - 1] = x;                                                          
							Y[activeCursorNumber - 1] = y; 
					
							X[0] = 0;
							Y[0] =0;
							X[event_counter -1] = 127;
							Y[event_counter -1] = 127;
					
							Plot_spline (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_2,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
					
					
						}
				}
				else
				{
					
							GetActiveGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, &activeCursorNumber);
							GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,activeCursorNumber,&x,&y);
							X[activeCursorNumber - 1] = x;                                                          
							Y[activeCursorNumber - 1] = y;  
							
							X[event_counter -1] = val - 1;
							Y[event_counter -1] = fir_curve[val - 1];
					
							Plot_spline (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_2,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
					
					
				}
				
			}
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Plot_spline (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int interpType, pointCount,j,i;
	double Second_deriv[256];
	double 	x_val;
	double yError;
	double interp_val;
	double plotbuff[3*Fir_length]; 
	double plotbuff_1[256];
	double plot_sd[256]; 
	int val,led; 
	double tt;
	double temp[Fir_length];
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			pointCount = 0;
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RING, &interpType);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED, &led);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_4,&val); 
			
			Spline (X, Y, event_counter, 0.0, 0.0, Second_deriv); 
			
			if(param_num < 8)
			{
	
				for(j = 0; j<  NumString; j ++)                                                    			
	                                                                                           			
				{	                                                                               			
					x_val = X[0] + j*(X[event_counter - 1] - X[0])/NumString;                          			
	                                                                                           			
					if (interpType==0)                                                                 			
	                    PolyInterp (X, Y, event_counter, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);              			
	                if (interpType==1)                                                             			
	                    RatInterp (X, Y, event_counter, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);               			
	                if (interpType==2)                                                             			
	                    SpInterp (X, Y, Second_deriv, event_counter , x_val , &interp_val);        			
	                                                                                           			
					plotbuff[j] = interp_val; 
                                                                                 			
				 }
			}
			else
			{
				if(param_num != 300)
				{
				
					for(j = 0; j<  128; j ++)                                                    			
	                                                                                           			
					{	                                                                               			
						x_val = X[0] + j*(X[event_counter - 1] - X[0])/128;                          			
	                                                                                           			
						if (interpType==0)                                                                 			
		                    PolyInterp (X, Y, event_counter, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);              			
		                if (interpType==1)                                                             			
		                    RatInterp (X, Y, event_counter, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);               			
		                if (interpType==2)                                                             			
		                    SpInterp (X, Y, Second_deriv, event_counter , x_val , &interp_val);        			
	                                                                                           			
						plotbuff[j] = interp_val; 
                                                                                 			
					 }
				}
				
			}
			
			if(param_num == 100)
			{
				for(j = 0; j<  64; j ++)                                                    			
	                                                                                           			
				{	                                                                               			
					x_val = X[0] + j*(X[event_counter - 1] - X[0])/64;                          			
	                                                                                           			
					if (interpType==0)                                                                 			
	                    PolyInterp (X, Y, event_counter, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);              			
	                if (interpType==1)                                                             			
	                    RatInterp (X, Y, event_counter, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);               			
	                if (interpType==2)                                                             			
	                    SpInterp (X, Y, Second_deriv, event_counter , x_val , &interp_val);        			
	                                                                                           			
					plotbuff[j] = interp_val; 
                                                                                 			
				 }	
			}
			
			if(param_num == 200)
			{
				for(j = 0; j<  256; j ++)                                                    			
	                                                                                           			
				{	                                                                               			
					x_val = X[0] + j*(X[event_counter - 1] - X[0])/256;                          			
	                                                                                           			
					if (interpType==0)                                                                 			
	                    PolyInterp (X, Y, event_counter, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);              			
	                if (interpType==1)                                                             			
	                    RatInterp (X, Y, event_counter, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);               			
	                if (interpType==2)                                                             			
	                    SpInterp (X, Y, Second_deriv, event_counter , x_val , &interp_val);        			
	                                                                                           			
					plotbuff[j] = interp_val; 
                                                                                 			
				 }	
			}
			
			if(param_num == 201)   //velocity
			{
				for(j = 0; j<  128; j ++)                                                    			
	                                                                                           			
				{	                                                                               			
					x_val = X[0] + j*(X[event_counter - 1] - X[0])/128;                          			
	                                                                                           			
					if (interpType==0)                                                                 			
	                    PolyInterp (X, Y, event_counter, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);              			
	                if (interpType==1)                                                             			
	                    RatInterp (X, Y, event_counter, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);               			
	                if (interpType==2)                                                             			
	                    SpInterp (X, Y, Second_deriv, event_counter , x_val , &interp_val);        			
	                                                                                           			
					plotbuff[j] = interp_val; 
                                                                                 			
				 }	
			}
			
			if(param_num == 300)   //fir_curve
			{
				for(j = 0; j<  val; j ++)                                                    			
	                                                                                           			
				{	                                                                               			
					x_val = X[0] + j*(X[event_counter - 1] - X[0])/val;                          			
	                                                                                           			
					if (interpType==0)                                                                 			
	                    PolyInterp (X, Y, event_counter, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);              			
	                if (interpType==1)                                                             			
	                    RatInterp (X, Y, event_counter, x_val,&interp_val, &yError);               			
	                if (interpType==2)                                                             			
	                    SpInterp (X, Y, Second_deriv, event_counter , x_val , &interp_val);        			
	                                                                                           			
					plotbuff[j] = interp_val; 
                                                                                 			
				 }	
			}
			
			
			///////////////////////
			
			switch (param_num)
				{
					case 100:
						
					for(i = 8; i < 64; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						 omega_coef[i] = plotbuff[i] ;                                             			
						}	                                                 			
						
						
					break;
					
					case 1:
						
					                                               			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						 decr_op[i] = plotbuff[i] ;                                             			
						}
						
					break;
					
					case 2:
						
						                                                 			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  decr_cl[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
						}
						
					break;
					
					case 3:
						
						                                                 			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						 width[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
						}
						
					break;
					
					case 4:
						
					                                                 			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						 del[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
						}
						
					break;
					
					case 5:
						
					                                                 			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						 dt[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
						}
						
					break;
					
					case 6:
						
						                                                 			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						 disp[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
						}
						
					break;
					
					case 7:
						
						                                                			
						for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						 decr_disp[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
						}
						
					break;
					
					case 8:
					break;
					
					case 9:
						
						                                                			
						for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						 CI_left_spline[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
						}
						
					break;
					
					case 10:
						
						                                                			
						for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						 vol_curve[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
						}
						
					break;
					
					case 201:
						for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						
						
							velocity[i] = plotbuff[i];  
						}
						
						
					break;
					
					case 300:
						for(i = 0; i < spectrum_window; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						
						
							fir_curve[i] = plotbuff[i];  
						}
						
						
					break;
					
					
					
					
					
					case 200:
						
						GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2, &val);
						switch (val)
						{
								case 0:
						
								for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
								{                                                                        			
								 Force_graph[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
								}
								break;
								case 1:
						
								for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
								{                                                                        			
								 Force_graph_1[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
								}
								break;
								
								case 2:
						
								for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
								{                                                                        			
								 Force_graph_2[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
								}
								break;
								
								case 3:
						
								for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
								{                                                                        			
								 Force_graph_3[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
								}
								break;
								
								case 4:
						
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
									 Force_graph_4[i] = plotbuff[i];                                             			
									}
								break;
						}
								
									 	for(i = 1; i < harm_num - 1; i ++)
										{
											plot_sd[i] =  0.5*(plotbuff[i + 1] - plotbuff[i - 1]);
										}
		
										plot_sd[0] = 0;
										plot_sd[255] = 0;
										
						
						
					break;
					
				
						
				}
			///////////////
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
			
			if(led == 0)
			{
			
					if( param_num <8)
					{
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, plotbuff,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);  
					}
					else
					{
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 	
					}
						
						for(j = 0; j<  event_counter; j ++)                                                    			
	                                                                                           			
					{	                             			
						PlotPoint(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,X[j],Y[j],VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND,VAL_YELLOW);  
			
					}
				
					if( param_num == 100)
					{
							    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                                        						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, plotbuff,  64, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);  
					}
			}
			else
			{
					if( param_num == 201)
					{
							    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                                        						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
			
						
						
					}
					
					if( param_num == 300)
					{
							    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                                        						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, plotbuff,  spectrum_window, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
								
							//	for(i = 0; i <val; i++)
							//		{
							//		plotbuff[i] = fir_curve[i]*amplitudeSpectrum[0][i];	
							//		}
								
								
							//	for(i = 0; i < 3*Fir_length; i++)
								for(i = 0; i < spectrum_window; i++) 
									{
									plotbuff[i] = fir_curve[i]*amplitudeSpectrum[0][i];	
									}
								
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
								
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  spectrum_window, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
								
								
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
								
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, fir_curve,  3*Fir_length, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
								
			
			for(j = 0; j < 512; j++)
				{
					
					tt = j*2*Pi()/((double)Fir_length);
					temp[j] = 0;
					for( i = 0; i < Fir_length; i++ )
					{
						
						temp[j] = temp[j] + plotbuff[i]*cos(tt*i + phaseSpectrum[0][i]);
				
					}
				}
			
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
				
				PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_MODE_2,temp,512, VAL_DOUBLE,
	    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);
				
						
						
					}
					
					if( param_num == 200)
					{
							    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                                        						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						
							    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                                        						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
							    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                                        						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, plot_sd,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);  
						
						
					}
			}
			
			//SetAxisRange(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0, VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0);   
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK reset_spline (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			for (i = 0; i < event_counter; i++)
			{
				
				XY[0][i] = 0;  	
				XY[1][i] = 0;  	
			                           	
				X[i] = 0;      	
				Y[i] = 0;      	


			}
			event_counter = 0;
		//		DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );       

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK scale_set (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_3, &val); 
			
			if(val == 0)
			{
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_LOCK,0.0,1.0);
			}
			else{SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0);}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Load_params (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	int i,j,nn,ni;
	char buf[250];
	double mult;
	double ratio;
	double xmax,xmin;
	int imax,imin;
	double a,b;
	double decimat_31 = 2147483648.0; 
	
	double temp[NumString][harm_num];
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
				FileToArray("D:/CVI_PIANOID_FANERA/gusli.srf", surface_int, VAL_UNSIGNED_INTEGER, Max_voice_num*Max_Cell_x*Max_Cell_y,Max_voice_num, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
				
			
			elements = 128;
			
			
			set_transmit_limits(panelHandle, PANEL_RING_3, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
			Construct_Pitch(panelHandle,PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_85,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
		  
			if(dir_selected_flag !=0)
			{
				
				SetDrive (letter);
				SetDir (dir_name);
				
			
			
				DirSelectPopup (dir_name, "Choose dir", 1, 0, dir_name);
				letter = dir_name[0] - 97;
			}
			else
			{
				DirSelectPopup ("", "Choose dir", 1, 0, dir_name);
				letter = dir_name[0] - 97;
				
			}
			SetDrive (letter);
			SetDir (dir_name);
			
			dir_selected_flag = 1;
			
			 SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING, " ");

             SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_STRING,dir_name);
			 
	//		 	FileToArray ("equal_loud.txt", equal_loud, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
	//			VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
			 
				for(i = 0; i <14;i++)
				{
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, btn_list_exibition[i],ATTR_DIMMED ,1);	
				}
			 
			
			 
			 	FileToArray ("ttn.txt", ttn, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);	
			 	FileToArray ("ttn.txt", ttn_orig, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
			 	FileToArray ("ttn_micro.txt", ttn_micro, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
					

				FileToArray ("decr_op.txt", decr_op, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
			 	FileToArray ("decr_cl.txt", decr_cl, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
			 	FileToArray ("disp.txt", disp, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				FileToArray ("damping.txt", damping, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
			 	FileToArray ("decr_disp.txt", decr_disp, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
			 	FileToArray ("FB.txt", FB, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
			 	FileToArray ("del.txt", del, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				FileToArray ("width.txt", width, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				FileToArray ("shteg.txt", shteg, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				GenShape_4 (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_87,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				
		//		FileToArray ("NL_2.txt", Amp_NL, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
		//		VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				
				FileToArray ("ttn_tails.txt", Quan_NL, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				FileToArray ("NL_disp.txt", NL_disp, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				FileToArray ("NL.txt", NL, VAL_DOUBLE, 128, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
					Quan_NL[i] = ttn[i];	
				}
				
				FileToArray ("velocity.txt", velocity, VAL_INTEGER, 128, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				FileToArray ("pedal_out.txt", pedal_out, VAL_INTEGER, 128, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				for(i = 70; i < 88; i++)
				{
					decr_cl[i] = decr_op[i];	
				}
				
				
				
				FileToArray ("ind_mult_0.txt", ind_mult_0, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				FileToArray ("ind_mult_1.txt", ind_mult_1, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				FileToArray ("ind_mult_2.txt", ind_mult_2, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				FileToArray ("ind_mult_3.txt", ind_mult_3, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				FileToArray ("ind_mult_4.txt", ind_mult_4, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);

int CVICALLBACK Send_params (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	double point_noise[88];


	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
		send_nl (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_8,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); //ttn
		Delay(0.001); 
		Send_block_param(2,decr_op);
		Delay(0.001); 
		Send_block_param(3,decr_cl);
		Delay(0.001); 
		Send_block_param(4,shteg);
		Delay(0.001); 
		Send_block_param(6,disp);
		Delay(0.001); 
		Send_block_param(7,decr_disp); 
		Delay(0.001); 
		Send_block_param(8,damping); 
		Delay(0.001); 
		
		for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
		{
			point_noise[i] = 5.;	
		}
		Send_block_param(9,point_noise);
		Delay(0.001); 
			
		Send_block_param(11,Quan_NL); 
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}
