			FileToArray("Force_graph_4_4.txt", Force_graph_4_4, VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
			
			FileToArray("Force_graph_4_5.txt", Force_graph_4_5, VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
			
			FileToArray("Force_graph_4_6.txt", Force_graph_4_6, VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
			
			FileToArray("Force_graph_4_7.txt", Force_graph_4_7, VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
			
*/			
			
			
		//	elements = 256;		
	break;				

	}
	
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Send_Omega (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j;
	int coef;
	int res;
	double temp[128];
	double decimat_100 =  100;
	double decimat_24 =  8388608;
	double decimat_32 =  2147483648;
	double ratio;
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result; 
	double val; 	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
	

			 forbid_notes_send = 1; 
				GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_87, &ratio);  
	//// KOSTYL - shift right 2///////
	//			for(i = 2; i < 128 ; i ++) 
	//			{
	//			   temp[i] =  omega_coef[i - 2];
//				}
//				temp[0]  = omega_coef[126];
//				temp[1]  = omega_coef[127];
	/// END KOSTYL
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_Ci_out; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 200;
	
			k = 0;
		
			for(i = 0; i < 256 ; i ++) 
			{
				res = (int)(4.0*omega_coef[i]*omega_coef[i]*ratio);   //Uncomment without Kostyl
	
//				res = (int)(4.0*temp[i]*temp[i]*ratio);   
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  
			
			forbid_notes_send = 0; 
		//	FlushOutQ (comport);
			Delay(0.1);
			
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK send_ci_out (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j;
	int coef;
	int res;
	int temp[harm_num];
	int Str,Harm; 
	double decimat_100 =  100;
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result; 
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			forbid_notes_send = 1;
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_Ci_out; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 202;
	
			k = 0;
		
			for(i = 0; i < 256 ; i ++) 
			{
				 	
				Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)Ci_str_out[i];
				res = Float_Result.int_val;
		//		res = Ci_str_out[i];
					
			
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
		//	FlushOutQ (comport);
			Delay(0.001);	
			forbid_notes_send = 0;  
	
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK draw_ci (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double x,y;
	
	double dval;
	static int xint; 
	double plotbuff[4096];
	double plotbuff_1[4096];
	double step; 
	int i,j,led,LED,LED_DEKA,pan;
	int val;
	int num_shape;
	int left_right;
	double  sc_val;
	int up_lo;
	int spline_mode;
	int okt_num;
	int imax,imin;
	double xmax,xmin,b;
	
	switch (event)
	{  
		case EVENT_MOUSE_POINTER_MOVE :
			
			GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,1,&x,&y); 
			
	//		SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_99,omega_coef[(int)x]);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_99,(double)eventData2); 
			
			
			
		break;
		
		case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
			
		 
		GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_15, &step);   
		GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,1,&x,&y);
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE, &val); 
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12, &LED_DEKA);
		
		xint=(int)x;                                                                   
		if(xint<0) xint=0;
		if(val == 0)//Omega
		{
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				omega_coef[xint] = omega_coef[xint] + (double)eventData2*step;
				
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				omega_coef[xint] = omega_coef[xint] - (double)eventData2*step; 
			
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_99,omega_coef[xint]);
			Send_Omega(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_5,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
										
								elements = 256;					
								                                                			
								for(i = 0; i < elements; i++)                                                 			
								{                                                                        			
								  plotbuff[i] = omega_coef[i];                                             			
								}
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);  
		}
		
		
 		if(val == 1)//Q
		{
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				Q_coeff[xint] = Q_coeff[xint] + (double)eventData2*step*0.01*Q_coeff[xint];
				
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				Q_coeff[xint] = Q_coeff[xint] - (double)eventData2*step*0.01*Q_coeff[xint]; 
			
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_99,Q_coeff[xint]);
			
			Send_Q_coef(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_10,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  							
								elements = 256;					
								                                                			
								for(i = 0; i < elements; i++)                                                 			
								{                                                                        			
								  plotbuff[i] = Q_coeff[i];                                             			
								}
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);  
		}
		
		if(val == 3)//NL
		{
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				NL[xint] = 1.05*NL[xint];
				
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				NL[xint] = 0.95*NL[xint]; 
			
			}
			
			send_nl_modes(panelHandle, PANEL_NL,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  							
							//	elements = 128;					
								                                                			
								for(i = 0; i < elements; i++)                                                 			
								{                                                                        			
								  plotbuff[i] = NL[i];                                             			
								}
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_MAGENTA);  
		}
		break;

		case EVENT_VAL_CHANGED :
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE, &val); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2, &num_shape);
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA, &nota); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12, &LED_DEKA); 
			
			okt_num = (int)nota/12;
			
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_19, &left_right); 
			
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,1,&x,&y);
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12, &LED);
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_28, &spline_mode);
			
				xint=(int)x;                                                                   
				if(xint<0) xint=0; 
				
					switch (val)
 					{
						case 0:   //Omega
							
							
							
								elements = 256;					
								omega_coef[xint]= y;                                                  			
								for(i = 0; i < elements; i++)                                                 			
								{                                                                        			
								  plotbuff[i] = omega_coef[i];                                             			
								}
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);  
							
						
						break; 
						
						case 1:		//  Q
							
						Q_coeff[xint]= y;
						SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_LOCK,0.0,1.0);
						for(i = 0; i < elements; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = Q_coeff[i]; 
					//	  Q_coeff_int[i] = (int)( 4294967295.0*plotbuff[i]);
					//	  Q_coeff_int[i] = (int)( plotbuff[i]); 
						}
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);    
						
						break;  
						
						case 2:			   //Ci_out
							
						LED_DEKA = 1;
  		/*
					if(LED_DEKA == 0)
					{
							Ci_str_out[xint]= y; 
							for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
							{                                                                        			
							  plotbuff[i] = Ci_str_out[i];
				//			  Ci_str_1_out[i] = Ci_str_out[i];
							  
							}
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
				//			send_ci_out(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_6,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
				  	}
	*/				
 				//	if(LED_DEKA == 1)
				//	{
							Ci_str_1_out[xint]= y; 
							
					//		MaxMin1D(Ci_str_out, 128, &xmax,&imax,&xmin,&imin);
					//		if(xmax < -1.0*xmin){b = -1000.0/xmin;}else{ b = 1000.0/xmax; } 
				
			
							for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
							{                                                                        			
							  plotbuff[i ] = Ci_str_1_out[i];
							  
							}
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);						
								
					//		send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
				//	}
				

						break;  
						
						
						case 3:		//NL
						if(xint > 127) {xint = 127;}
						NL[xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = NL[i];                                             			
						}
						
						
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_MAGENTA);    
						
						break;
						
							case 4:
							
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
							
							SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_LOCK,-1.0,1.0);
		
							RefreshGraph (panelHandle,PANEL_MODE);
							GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,1,&x,&y);
			
							xint=(int)x;                                                                   
							if(xint<0) xint=0;
							
							
							GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2, &num_shape);
							
						
							elements = 256;
							
							Force_vozb[num_shape][okt_num][xint]= y;
							
							for( i = 0; i < harm_num;i++)
							{
								plotbuff[i] = Force_vozb[num_shape][okt_num][i];
										
							}
							
							
							for( i = 1; i < harm_num - 1;i++)
										{
											plotbuff_1[i] = 0.5*(plotbuff[i + 1] - plotbuff[i - 1]);	
										}
										
										plotbuff_1[0] = 0;
										plotbuff_1[harm_num - 1] = 0;
								             						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
								
						//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, plotbuff_1,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
								
								
							break; 
							
							case 5:
								
							
							break;
						
					
					}

		break;
		
		
		case EVENT_COMMIT :
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE, &val);   
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED,&led); 
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_19, &left_right);  
				
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			
			okt_num = (int)(nota/12);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_OKT_NUM,okt_num);  
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12, &LED_DEKA);	
				
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,1,&x,&y);
			
				xint=(int)x;                                                                   
				if(xint<0) xint=0; 
				
					switch (val)
					{
						
						case 0:
						elements = 256;
						omega_coef[xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < elements; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = omega_coef[i];                                             			
						}
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);      
						 Send_Omega(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_5,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
						break; 
						
						case 1:
							
				//		elements = 128;	
						Q_coeff[xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < elements; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = Q_coeff[i];                                             			
						}
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						
						Send_Q_coef(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_10,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
						
						break;  
						
						case 2:
		/*					
					if(LED_DEKA == 0)
					{
							Ci_str_out[xint]= y; 
							for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
							{                                                                        			
							  plotbuff[i] = Ci_str_out[i];
						//	  Ci_str_1_out[i] = Ci_str_out[i];
							  
							}
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
							send_ci_out(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_6,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
				  	}
		*/			
		//			if(LED_DEKA == 1)
					{
							Ci_str_1_out[xint]= y; 
						//	MaxMin1D(Ci_str_out, 128, &xmax,&imax,&xmin,&imin);
						//	if(xmax < -1.0*xmin){b = -1000.0/xmin;}else{ b = 1000.0/xmax; } 
				
							for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
							{                                                                        			
							  plotbuff[i ] = Ci_str_1_out[i];
							  
							}
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);						
					/*			for(j = 0; j < 9; j++)
								{
									for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
									{
										decka_coeff[j][i] = decka_coeff_temp[j][i]*Ci_str_1_out[i];
										
									}
								}
					*/			
							//	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2,xint);
							//	TR_out(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
								Set_deka_mode_num(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
								send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
							}
			
						break;
						
						case 3:
						if(xint > 127) {xint = 127;}
						NL[xint]= y;                                                  			
						for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = NL[i];                                             			
						}
						
						send_nl_modes(panelHandle, PANEL_NL,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_MAGENTA); 
						break; 
						
						case 4:
							
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
							
							SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_LOCK,-1.0,1.0);
		
							RefreshGraph (panelHandle,PANEL_MODE);
							GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,1,&x,&y);
			
							xint=(int)x;                                                                   
							if(xint<0) xint=0;
							
							
							GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2, &num_shape);
							elements = 256;  
							
							Force_vozb[num_shape][okt_num][xint]= y;
							
							for( i = 0; i < harm_num;i++)
							{
								plotbuff[i] = Force_vozb[num_shape][okt_num][i];
										
							}
							
							
							
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
								
								Send_Force_vozb(num_shape,okt_num); 
								
							break; 
						
						
						case 5:
							break;
						
						
					}
				
			                   						
						
			
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK scale_set_1 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	int LED;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_4, &val);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12, &LED);
			
			if(val == 0)
			{
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_LOCK,0.0,1.0);
			}
			else{SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0);}
			
			RefreshGraph (panelHandle,PANEL_MODE);
			if(LED == 1)
			{
				if(val == 0)
				{
					SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_LOCK,0.0,1.0);
					SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_LOCK,0.0,1.0); 
				}
				else
				{
					SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0);
					SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0);
				}
				
				
			}
			break;	  
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_FB (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
 double val;
 int decimat32 =  2147483648;
 int res,led;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;    

	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_3, &val);
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_LED_7, &led);
			Gain_FB[1] = val;
			
			Float_Result.fl_val  = -1.0*(float)val; 
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_FB_test; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
			
		//		res = (int)(val*decimat32/2); 
				res = Float_Result.int_val;

			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 12);
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  
		//	FlushOutQ (comport);
			Delay(0.001);	
			
			if(led == 1)
			{
			
		//		SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON,1);
			
			//	Reset (panelHandle,PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON, EVENT_COMMIT,0, 0, 0);
	
				Delay(0.1);	
		//		SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON,0);
		//		Reset (panelHandle,PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON, EVENT_COMMIT,0, 0, 0);
			}
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}



int CVICALLBACK set_scale_omega (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double min, max;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_7, &min);  
					GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_8, &max);  
			
	 	SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,min,max); 
		
		SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,min,max); 
		SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,min,max);  
		SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,min,max);   
		
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}




int CVICALLBACK Send_Q_coef (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j;
	int coef;
	int res;
								
	double decimat_100 =  100.0;
	double decimat_28 = 2684354565.0;									   
		
	double decimat_30 = 4294967296.0;
	double decimat_24 = 16777216.0; 
	//double decimat_30 = 1073741824; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result; 
	double val;
	double ratio;
	double temp[128];
	switch (event)																
	{					
		 
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
	
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_79, &ratio);
				//// KOSTYL - shift right 2///////
	//			for(i = 2; i < 128 ; i ++) 
	//			{
	//			   temp[i] =  Q_coeff[i - 2];
	//			}
	//			temp[0]  = Q_coeff[126];
	//			temp[1]  = Q_coeff[127];
	/// END KOSTYL
		
			forbid_notes_send = 1;  
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_Ci_out; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 201;
	
			k = 0;
		
			for(i = 0; i < 256 ; i ++) 
			{
	//			
					res = (int)((Q_coeff[i] + 0.0)*ratio);		//Unkomment without Kostyl
	//			res = (int)((temp[i] + 0.0)*ratio);
			
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  
	
		//	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
		//	FlushOutQ (comport);
			Delay(0.001);
			
			forbid_notes_send = 0;  
	
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}






int CVICALLBACK save_Ci_out (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			SetDrive (letter);
			SetDir (dir_name);
 			ArrayToFile("Ci_str_out.txt", Ci_str_out , VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
			ArrayToFile("Ci_str_1_out.txt", Ci_str_1_out , VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK draw_Omega (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double x,y;                     	
	static int xint; 
	double plotbuff[harm_num];
	double plotbuff_1[harm_num]; 
	int i,j,led,pan,LED_DEKA;
	int nn,ni;
	int val;
	double a;
	int xi,yi,lb,rb,modif;
	
	int mode,LED,mod,mode_number;
	int numstr;
	double temp[64];
	
	switch (event)
	{  
		case EVENT_MOUSE_POINTER_MOVE : 
			
	//		GetRelativeMouseState (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,&xi,&yi,&lb, &rb, &modif);

				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale); 
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,1,&x,&y);
			
				xint=(int)x;                                                                   
				if(xint<0) xint=0;
	//			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_99,omega_coef[xint]);
			
			
			break;

		case EVENT_VAL_CHANGED :
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8, &LED);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_6, &mod); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36, &mode_number); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12, &LED_DEKA);
			
			nn = (int)(nota/2);
			ni = nota - nn*2;
			
				
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,1,&x,&y);
			
				xint=(int)x + offs_scale;                                                                  
				if(xint < 0) xint=0;
				if(xint > 255) xint=255;
				
				 
				
			for(i = 0; i< 255;i++)
			{
				plotbuff[i] = 0;
				plotbuff_1[i] = 0;
			}
			
			
     		if(MUTE_mode == 0)
			{
  				if(mod == 0 )
				{
  	  			 	if(LED == 1)
					{
				  
					  Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][xint + ni*256]= y; 
					  Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256]= y; 
                                               			
							for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
							{                                                                        			
							  plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256];
						  
							}
						
						
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
							
						//	doctor_ci(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_25,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
							C_2_Cstr_all(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_114,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
							send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
						if(LED_DEKA == 1)
						{
							/*
									for(j = 0; j < 9; j++)
									{
										for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
										{
											decka_coeff[j][i] = decka_coeff_temp[j][i]*Ci_str_out[i];
										
										}
									}
									
							*/
							send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 		
						}					
					}
					else
					{
					  if(mute_amps[xint] >0)
					  {
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
				
							nn = (int)(i/2);
							ni = i - nn*2;
						
					
					//		Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*128] = a*Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*128];
							Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256] = y*modes_extr[xint][i];    
						}
					  }
					
					displ_modes_amps (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_28, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
					 	
					}
					
				}
	 			else  
				{
				
					   if(mute_amps[xint] >0)
					   {	   
						modes_amps[xint] = y;
					
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
				
							nn = (int)(i/2);
							ni = i - nn*2;
					//		Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*128] = a*Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*128];
						
							Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256] = modes_amps[xint]*modes_extr[xint][i];
						 
							plotbuff[i] = modes_amps[xint]*modes_extr[xint][i];
				
						}
					
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36,xint);
					
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 88; i++)
							{
				
								nn = (int)(i/2);
								ni = i - nn*2;
								plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256]; 
					
							}	
			
									DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
									PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
									VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						
						
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{   
						
							plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = modes_amps[i];
						  
						}	
						
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
					
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_1,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
					
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{   
							if(mute_amps[i]>0){plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = 0;}
							else {plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = modes_amps[i];} 
						  
						}
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_1,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
					
					   }
					
				}
			}
			else  //mute on
			{
				
				
					
			if(xint !=xint_prev)
			{
				xint_prev = xint;
				mute_amps[xint] = -1.0*mute_amps[xint]; 
				for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
					{ 
						plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = modes_amps[i];
						  
					}
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_1,  256 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
				
					for(i = 0; i < 256;i++)
					{
						if(mute_amps[i] > 0)
						{
							plotbuff[i] = 0;	
						}
						else
						{
							plotbuff[i] = modes_amps[i];	
						}
					}
					for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
					{ 
						plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = plotbuff[i];
						  
					}
					PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_1,  256 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
					
				//	send_ci_zeros(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_101,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);

				}
			}
		break;
		
		case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK_UP:
			if(MUTE_mode == 0)
			{
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8, &LED);
				if(LED == 0) 
				{
					send_ci_zeros(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_101,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				send_ci_zeros(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_101,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   	
			}

		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8, &LED);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale); 
			nn = (int)(nota/2);
			ni = nota - nn*2;
			
 			for(i = 0; i< 256;i++)
			{
				plotbuff[i] = 0;
			}
			
				
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,1,&x,&y);
