				if(xint < 0) xint = 0; 
				if(xint > 255) xint = 255;
			
				  
 				if(LED == 1)
				{
					if(right_click_counter == 0)
					{
				  
		
					  Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256] = -Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256];
					}
					if(right_click_counter == 1)
					{
				  
		
					  Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256] = 0;
					}
					if(right_click_counter == 2)
					{
				  
		
					  Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][xint + ni*256];
					}
					
					right_click_counter ++;
					if(right_click_counter  ==3){right_click_counter = 0;}
                                               			
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256];
						  
						}
						
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						doctor_ci(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_25,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
						send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
					
				}			
			
				else
				{
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36, &mode_number); 
				
				
			
				x = (double)mode_number;
				y = modes_amps[mode_number];
			
				SetActiveCtrl (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2);
				SetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, 1, x,y );	
				}
		break;
		case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8, &LED);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_6, &mod);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36, &mode_number);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale);    
			
			GetGraphCursor(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,1,&x,&y);
			//////////////////////////////
			 xint = (int)x;
					if(mute_amps[xint] >0)
					   {
						   		if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
								{
				
									modes_amps[xint] = 1.1*modes_amps[xint];
				
								}
								if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
								{
									modes_amps[xint] = 0.9*modes_amps[xint];
			
								}
					
					
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
				
							nn = (int)(i/2);
							ni = i - nn*2;
					//		Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*128] = a*Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*128];
						
							Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*128] = modes_amps[xint]*modes_extr[xint][i];
						 
							plotbuff[i] = modes_amps[xint]*modes_extr[xint][i];
				
						}
					
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36,xint);
						
						displ_modes_amps (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_28, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
  /*					 
					
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 88; i++)
							{
				
								nn = (int)(i/2);
								ni = i - nn*2;
								plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*128]; 
					
							}	
			
									DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
									PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
									VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						
						
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{   
						
							plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = modes_amps[i];
						  
						}	
						
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
					
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_1,  128 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
					
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{   
							if(mute_amps[i]>0){plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = 0;}
							else {plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = modes_amps[i];} 
						  
						}
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_1,  128 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
*/					
					 }
					

					
		send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);	
			
			//////////////////////
			
						
							

							

		break;
		
		case EVENT_COMMIT :
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8, &LED);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_6, &mod); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36, &mode_number);  
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12, &LED_DEKA);
			nn = (int)(nota/2);
			ni = nota - nn*2;
			
				
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,1,&x,&y);
			
				xint=(int)x + offs_scale;                                                                  
				if(xint < 0) xint=0;
				if(xint > 255) xint=255;
				
				 
				
			for(i = 0; i< 256;i++)
			{
				plotbuff[i] = 0;
				plotbuff_1[i] = 0;
			}
			
			
   			if(MUTE_mode == 0)
			{
				if(mod == 0 )
				{
  	  			 	if(LED == 1)
					{
				  
					  Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][xint + ni*256]= y; 
					  Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256]= y; 
                                               			
							for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
							{                                                                        			
							  plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256];
						  
							}
						
						
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff,  256 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
							doctor_ci(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_25,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
							send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
						if(LED_DEKA == 1)
						{
							/*
									for(j = 0; j < 9; j++)
									{
										for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
										{
											decka_coeff[j][i] = decka_coeff_temp[j][i]*Ci_str_out[i];
										
										}
									}
									
							*/
							send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 		
						}					
					}
					else
					{
						displ_modes_amps (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_28, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  	
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36,xint);
					}
					
				}
				else  
				{
				  
					   if(mute_amps[xint] >0)
					   {	   
						modes_amps[xint] = y;
					
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
				
							nn = (int)(i/2);
							ni = i - nn*2;
					//		Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*128] = a*Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*128];
						
							Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256] = modes_amps[xint]*modes_extr[xint][i];
						 
							plotbuff[i] = modes_amps[xint]*modes_extr[xint][i];
				
						}
					
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36,xint);
					
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 88; i++)
							{
				
								nn = (int)(i/2);
								ni = i - nn*2;
								plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256]; 
					
							}	
			
									DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
									PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
									VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						
						
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{   
						
							plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = modes_amps[i];
						  
						}	
						
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
					
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_1,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
					
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{   
							if(mute_amps[i]>0){plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = 0;}
							else {plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = modes_amps[i];} 
						  
						}
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_1,  256 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
					
					   }
					
				}
			}
			else  //mute on
			{
				
				
					
			if(xint !=xint_prev)
			{
				xint_prev = xint;
				mute_amps[xint] = -1.0*mute_amps[xint]; 
				for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
					{ 
						plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = modes_amps[i];
						  
					}
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_1,  256 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
				
					for(i = 0; i < 256;i++)
					{
						if(mute_amps[i] > 0)
						{
							plotbuff[i] = 0;	
						}
						else
						{
							plotbuff[i] = modes_amps[i];	
						}
					}
					for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
					{ 
						plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = plotbuff[i];
						  
					}
					PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_1,  256 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
					
					send_ci_zeros(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_101,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				}
			}
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK draw_Q (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double x,y;                     	
	static int xint; 
	double plotbuff[harm_num];
	double temp[harm_num]; 
	double temp_1[harm_num]; 
	int i,led,pan;
	int nn, ni;
	int val;
	
	int mode,led_val;
	int toggle_q,audio; 
	int ixmax,ixmin;
	double xmax,xmin;
	
	switch (event)
	{  
		


		case EVENT_VAL_CHANGED :
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_3,&led_val);
			
			GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3,1,&x,&y);
			
			
			if(flag_audio == 0)
			{
			
			xint = (int)x;
			if(led_val == 1)
			{
			
				Ci_str_curve[xint] = y;
			
			
			
				nn = (int)(xint/2);
				ni = xint - nn*2;
							
							
					for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
								
					{
							Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*128] = Ci_str_curve[xint]*Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][i + ni*128]; 
							plotbuff[i] = 	Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*128];  
					}
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, Ci_str_curve,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED); 
					
						
			
			}
			else
			{
				Ci_str_curve_cos[xint] = y;
			
			
			
				nn = (int)(xint/2);
				ni = xint - nn*2;
							
							
					for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
								
					{
							Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*128] = Ci_str_curve_cos[xint]*Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][i + ni*128]; 
							plotbuff[i] = 	Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*128];  
					}				
						
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, Ci_str_curve_cos,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
				
			}
			
			}
			else
			{
				audio_threshold_level = y;	
			}
		break;
		
		case EVENT_COMMIT :
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_3,&led_val);   
			GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3,1,&x,&y);
			
			if(flag_audio == 0)
			{
			xint = (int)x;
			if(led_val == 1)
			{
			
				Ci_str_curve[xint] = y;
			
				nn = (int)(xint/2);
				ni = xint - nn*2;
							
							
					for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
								
					{
							Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*128] = Ci_str_curve[xint]*Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][i + ni*128]; 
							plotbuff[i] = 	Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*128];  
					}				
						
			
			
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, Ci_str_curve,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED); 
			
			}
			else
			{
				Ci_str_curve_cos[xint] = y;
			
			
			
				nn = (int)(xint/2);
				ni = xint - nn*2;
							
							
					for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
								
					{
							Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*128] = Ci_str_curve_cos[xint]*Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][i + ni*128]; 
							plotbuff[i] = 	Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*128];  
					}				
						
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, Ci_str_curve_cos,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
				
			}
			}
			else
			{
				audio_threshold_level = y;	
			}
		break;		
		
		
		break;
	}
	

	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK draw_Ci_out (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double x,y,a;                     	
	static int xint; 
	double plotbuff[harm_num],plotbuff_1[harm_num];
	double temp[NumString]; 
	int i,led,pan,j,mode_number;
	int val,LED,mod;
	double max,min,mult;
	int imax,imin;
	
	int mode,display_mod;
	int numstr;
	int num_h;
	int nn, ni;
	
	int val1;
	
	switch (event)
	{  
		
		case EVENT_MOUSE_POINTER_MOVE : 
			
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4,1,&x,&y);
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale);   
			
				xint=(int)x + offs_scale;                                                                
				if(xint<0) xint=0;
	//			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_99,omega_coef[xint]);
			
			
			break;
			
		case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8, &LED);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_6, &mod);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36, &mode_number);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale);    
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_BINARYSWITCH, &val1);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_DISPLAY, &display_mod);
			
			GetGraphCursor(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4,1,&x,&y);
			
			xint=(int)x + offs_scale;                                                                
			if(xint<0) xint=0;
			
							nn = (int)(x/2);
							ni = (int)x - nn*2;
   	 		if(mod == 0)   //strings
			{
						
   				if(LED == 1)	   //adj ci_coeff
				{							
				 if(val1 == 0)//ci_cos
						{
							
						if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
							{
				
								Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = 1.1*Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
				
							}
							if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
							{
			
								Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = 0.9*Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
			
							}		
					
								y = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
				
								SetGraphCursor(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4,1,x,y);  
				
										for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
										{
				
											nn = (int)(i/2);
											ni = i - nn*2;
											plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256]; 
					
										}
						
							update_modes_amp(plotbuff,mode_number,val1);
		
				
										DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
										PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, modes_amps,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
										VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
						
										for(i = offs_scale; i < 88; i++)
										{
				
											nn = (int)(i/2);
											ni = i - nn*2;
											plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]; 
					
										}	
			
												DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
												PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
												VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
					//		 doctor_ci(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_25,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 			
							 send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
						}
				 else
				 {
						if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
							{
				
								Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = 1.1*Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
				
							}
							if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
							{
			
								Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = 0.9*Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
			
							}		
					
								y = Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
				
								SetGraphCursor(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4,1,x,y);  
				
										for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
										{
				
											nn = (int)(i/2);
											ni = i - nn*2;
											plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256]; 
					
										}
						
							update_modes_amp(plotbuff,mode_number,val1);
		
				
										DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
										PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, modes_amps,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
										VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
						
										for(i = offs_scale; i < 88; i++)
										{
				
											nn = (int)(i/2);
											ni = i - nn*2;
											plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]; 
					
										}	
			
												DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
												PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
												VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED); 
					//		 doctor_ci(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_25,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 			
							 send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);	 
	 
				 }
				}
				else
				{
					
					if(display_mod == 0)
					{
					
						if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
								{
				
									modes_amps_str[xint]  = 1.1*modes_amps_str[xint];
				
								}
						if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
								{
			
									modes_amps_str[xint]  = 0.9*modes_amps_str[xint];
								}
					
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
				
							nn = (int)(i/2);
							ni = i - nn*2;
						
					
					//		Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*128] = a*Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*128];
							Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256] = modes_amps_str[xint]*modes_extr_str[xint][i];    
						}
					
						displ_modes_amps (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_28, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
						send_ci_zeros(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_101,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
					}
					else
					{
					
								if(val1 == 0)
								{
				
									nn = (int)(xint/2);
									ni = xint - nn*2;
									 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
										{
											Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = 1.1*Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
										}
									 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
										{
											Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = 0.9*Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
										}
									 
								for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
								{
				
									nn = (int)(i/2);
									ni = i - nn*2;
									plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256]; 
					
								}
						
								update_modes_amp(plotbuff,mode_number,val1);
		
				
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, modes_amps,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
						
								for(i = offs_scale; i < 88; i++)
								{
				
									nn = (int)(i/2);
									ni = i - nn*2;
									plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]; 
					
								}	
			
										DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
										PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
										VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						
							}
				
								else
								{
									nn = (int)(xint/2);
									ni = xint - nn*2;
									 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
										{
											Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = 1.1*Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
										}
									 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
										{
											Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = 0.9*Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
										}
									 
								for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
								{
				
									nn = (int)(i/2);
									ni = i - nn*2;
									plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256]; 
					
								}
						
								update_modes_amp(plotbuff,mode_number,val1);
		
				
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, modes_amps,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
						
								for(i = offs_scale; i < 88; i++)
								{
				
									nn = (int)(i/2);
									ni = i - nn*2;
									plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]; 
					
								}	
			
										DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
										PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
										VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED); 
	
								}
						}
						
						
						
		
					}
				
			}
			else
			{
					if(val1 == 0)
					{
						    nn = (int)(xint/2);
							ni = xint - nn*2;
						
							if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
							 {
								Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = 1.1*Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
							 }
							if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
							{
								Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = 0.9*Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
							}				
						
				
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
				
							nn = (int)(i/2);
							ni = i - nn*2;
							plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256]; 
					
						}
						
			update_modes_amp(plotbuff,mode_number,val1);
		
				
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, modes_amps,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
						
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 88; i++)
						{
				
							nn = (int)(i/2);
							ni = i - nn*2;
							plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]; 
					
						}	
			
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						
					}
					else
					{
							nn = (int)(xint/2);
							ni = xint - nn*2;
				
							if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
							 {
								Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = 1.1*Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
							 }
							if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
							{
								Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = 0.9*Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256];
							}				
						
				
							for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
							{
				
								nn = (int)(i/2);
								ni = i - nn*2;
								plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]; 
					
							}
			
			
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED); 
							
					}
				send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			}

			

				break;

		case EVENT_VAL_CHANGED :
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8, &LED);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_6, &mod);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36, &mode_number);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale);    
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_BINARYSWITCH, &val1); 
			
			nn = (int)(nota/2);
			ni = nota - nn*2;
			
				
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4,1,&x,&y);
			
				xint=(int)x + offs_scale;                                                                  
				if(xint < 0) xint=0;
				if(xint > 255) xint=255;
				
			for(i = 0; i< 256;i++)
			{
				plotbuff[i] = 0;
			}
				
   	 		if(mod == 0)   //strings
			{
						
    			if(LED == 1)	   //adj ci_coeff
				{
				  
				  Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][xint + ni*256]= y; 
				  Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256]= y; 
                                               			
					for(i = offs_scale; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256];
						  
						}
						
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						
						send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
					
				}
				else
				{
					
				//	a = y*modes_amps_str[xint];
					
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{
				
						nn = (int)(i/2);
						ni = i - nn*2;
						
					
				//		Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*128] = a*Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*128];
						Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256] = y*modes_extr_str[xint][i];    
					}
					
					displ_modes_amps (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_28, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
					
				}
	
			}
			else
			{
					if(val1 == 0)
					{
				
							nn = (int)(xint/2);
							ni = xint - nn*2;
				
						Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = y;
				
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
				
							nn = (int)(i/2);
							ni = i - nn*2;
							plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][xint + ni*256]; 
					
						}
						
			update_modes_amp(plotbuff,mode_number,val1);
		
				
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, modes_amps,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
						
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 88; i++)
						{
				
							nn = (int)(i/2);
							ni = i - nn*2;
							plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]; 
					
						}	
			
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						
					}
					else
					{
							nn = (int)(xint/2);
							ni = xint - nn*2;
				
						Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = y;
				
							for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
							{
				
								nn = (int)(i/2);
								ni = i - nn*2;
								plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]; 
					
							}
			
			
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED); 
							
					}
				
			}

				
						
					
		break;
		
		case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK_UP:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8, &LED);
			if(LED == 0) 
			{
				send_ci_zeros(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_101,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			}

		break;
		
		
			
			
		break;
		
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8, &LED);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale); 
			nn = (int)(nota/2);
			ni = nota - nn*2;
			
			for(i = 0; i< 256;i++)
			{
				plotbuff[i] = 0;
			}
			
				
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4,1,&x,&y);
			
				xint=(int)x + offs_scale;;                                                                   
				if(xint < 0) xint = 0; 
				if(xint > 255) xint = 255;
			
				  
 				if(LED == 1)
				{
					if(right_click_counter == 0)
					{
				  
		
					  Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256] = -Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256];
					}
					if(right_click_counter == 1)
					{
				  
		
					  Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256] = 0;
					}
					
					if(right_click_counter == 2)
					{
				  
		
					  Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][xint + ni*256];
					}
					
					right_click_counter ++;
					
					if(right_click_counter  == 3){right_click_counter = 0;}
                                               			
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256];
						  
						}
						
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						doctor_ci(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_25,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
						send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
					
				}			
			
		break;
				
		
		case EVENT_COMMIT: 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8, &LED);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_6, &mod);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36, &mode_number);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale);    
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_BINARYSWITCH, &val1); 
			
			nn = (int)(nota/2);
			ni = nota - nn*2;
			
				
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4,1,&x,&y);
			
				xint=(int)x + offs_scale;                                                                  
				if(xint < 0) xint=0;
				if(xint > 255) xint= 255;
				
			for(i = 0; i< 256;i++)
			{
				plotbuff[i] = 0;
			}
				
 		 	if(mod == 0)   //strings
			{
						
				if(LED == 1)	   //adj ci_coeff
				{
				  
				  Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][xint + ni*256]= y; 
				  Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256]= y; 
                                               			
					for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256];
						  
						}
						
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						
						send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
					
				}
				else
				{
					
				//	a = y*modes_amps_str[xint];
					
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{
				
						nn = (int)(i/2);
						ni = i - nn*2;
						
					
				//		Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256] = a*Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256];
						Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256] = y*modes_extr_str[xint][i];    
					}
					
					displ_modes_amps (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_28, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
					
				}
	
			}
			else
			{
					if(val1 == 0)
					{
				
							nn = (int)(xint/2);
							ni = xint - nn*2;
				
						Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = y;
				
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
				
							nn = (int)(i/2);
							ni = i - nn*2;
							plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]; 
					
						}
						
			update_modes_amp(plotbuff,mode_number,val1);
		
				
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, modes_amps,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW); 
						
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 88; i++)
						{
				
							nn = (int)(i/2);
							ni = i - nn*2;
							plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_number + ni*256]; 
					
						}	
			
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 			
								 
						
					}
					else
					{
							nn = (int)(xint/2);
							ni = xint - nn*2;
				
						Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]  = y;
				
							for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
							{
				
								nn = (int)(i/2);
								ni = i - nn*2;
								plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][mode_number + ni*256]; 
					
							}
			
			
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED); 
							
					}
				
			}

					
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK display_m (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val,LED,LED_DEKA;
	int up_lo;
	int i;
	double plotbuff[harm_num]; 
	double plotbuff1[harm_num];
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE, &val);
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_LOG, &LED); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12, &LED_DEKA);  
			
     		SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, ATTR_XAXIS_GAIN, 1.0);
			
			
			switch (val)
					{
						case 0:
					                                            			
						for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = omega_coef[i]; 
						  
						}
						 param_num = 100; 
						SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LINEAR); 
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
				    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
						break; 
						
						case 1:
							
							
				//		SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,(double)elements); 
						for(i = 0; i < elements; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = Q_coeff[i]; 
					//	  Q_coeff_int[i] = (int)( 4294967295.0*plotbuff[i]);
						}
						SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LINEAR); 
						
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);    
						
						break;  
				  		
						case 2:
							
							
					                                 			
							for(i = 0; i < elements; i++)                                                 			
							{                                                                        			
							  plotbuff1[i] = Ci_str_out[i]; 
							  plotbuff[i] = Ci_str_1_out[i]; 
						  
						
							  
							}
				//			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS   ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,1.0); 
				//			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
						
				//			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS    ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,128.0); 
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
							LED_DEKA = 1;
					        if(LED_DEKA == 0)
							{                                                                                   						
						
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff1,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
							}
							else
							{
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);	
							}
							
						  
						
						break;  
						
						
						case 3:
							
						                                               			
						for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = NL[i];                                             			
						}
						//SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LINEAR); 
						
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_MAGENTA);    
						
						break; 
						
						case 5:
						break;  
						
					}
			
			if( LED == 0)
			{
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LINEAR);
			}
			if( LED == 1)
			{
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LOG);
			}
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK zero_ci_str_current (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,nn,ni;
	int numstr;
	int mode;
	int log_sw;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		
			
			nn = (int)(nota/2);
			ni = nota - nn*2;
						
			for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
			{                                                                        			
				Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256] = 0;
				Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] = 0;
				
			}
		
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}



int CVICALLBACK save_Omega (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			SetDrive (letter);
			SetDir (dir_name);			
 			ArrayToFile("omega_coef.txt", omega_coef, VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}



int CVICALLBACK Button_FRQ (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	char *char_val;
	unsigned int lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 	
	double val;
	int vol;
	int i,k;
	double msec;
	int interpType;
	double x_val;
	double temp;
	double ind_mult_0_send[88];	

	int c0,c1;
	int res,res_current;
	int ringslide2;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0};
	double decimat_24 = 4294967296.0 ;
//	double decimat_23 = 8388608.0;
	double decimat_23 = 65000.0; 
	double temp_array[128];
	int count;
	int led;
	
	double Second_deriv[11];
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,&vol);
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&ringslide2); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_4,&led); 
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0;
			param_num = 8;
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0);
			
			
			   // ind_mult  вместо nota ставить vol
				char_val = StrDup ("ind_mult");
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
				 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
				 
				 
				 switch(ringslide2)
				 {
			 		case 0:
			
						SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,88.0); 
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_0[nota]); 	                                                                                           						
				    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_0,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
					count = 88; 
					break;
					case 1:
			
						SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,88.0); 
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_1[nota]); 	                                                                                           						
				    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_1,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
					count = 88; 
					break;
					case 2:
			
						SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,88.0); 
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_2[nota]); 	                                                                                           						
				    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_2,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
					count = 88; 
					break;
					case 3:
			
						SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,88.0); 
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_3[nota]); 	                                                                                           						
				    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_3,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
					count = 88; 
					break;
					case 4:
			
						SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,88.0); 
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_4[nota]); 	                                                                                           						
				    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_4,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
					count = 88; 
					break;
				
				 }
		
			
			
				
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
		
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
	break;
	
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&ringslide2); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_4,&led); 
			
 		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				 
   					switch (ringslide2)
					{
						case 0:
				
						 ind_mult_0[nota] = ind_mult_0[nota] + eventData2*step[8];
						 res_current =   (int)(ind_mult_0[nota]*decimat_23); 
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_mult_1[nota] = ind_mult_0[nota];
							ind_mult_2[nota] = ind_mult_0[nota];
							ind_mult_3[nota] = ind_mult_0[nota];
							ind_mult_4[nota] = ind_mult_0[nota]; 
						 }
						
						 c0 =  cmd0_FRQ;
						 c1 = 0;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_0[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_0,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_FAT_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
							
						
							
				//			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_1,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,

							count = 88;
						break;

						 ind_mult_1[nota] = ind_mult_1[nota] + eventData2*step[8];
						 res_current =   (int)(ind_mult_1[nota]*decimat_23); 
						 if(led == 1)

							ind_mult_0[nota] = ind_mult_1[nota];

							ind_mult_3[nota] = ind_mult_1[nota];

						 }
						 
						
						 c0 =  cmd0_FRQ;
						 c1 = 1;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_1[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_1,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_FAT_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
							
				//			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_2,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				//			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
							
				//			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_0,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				//			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_BLUE);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 2:
				
						 ind_mult_2[nota] = ind_mult_2[nota] + eventData2*step[8];
						 res_current =   (int)(ind_mult_2[nota]*decimat_23); 
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_mult_1[nota] = ind_mult_2[nota];
							ind_mult_0[nota] = ind_mult_2[nota];
							ind_mult_3[nota] = ind_mult_2[nota];
							ind_mult_4[nota] = ind_mult_2[nota]; 
						 }
						 
						 c0 =  cmd0_FRQ;
						 c1 = 2;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_2[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );

				//			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 3:
				
						 ind_mult_3[nota] = ind_mult_3[nota] + eventData2*step[8];
						 res_current =   (int)(ind_mult_3[nota]*decimat_23); 
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_mult_1[nota] = ind_mult_3[nota];
							ind_mult_2[nota] = ind_mult_3[nota];
							ind_mult_0[nota] = ind_mult_3[nota];
							ind_mult_4[nota] = ind_mult_3[nota]; 
						 }
						 
						 c0 =  cmd0_FRQ;
						 c1 = 3;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_3[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );

							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
							
					//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_2,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
							
					//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_4,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 4:
				
						 ind_mult_4[nota] = ind_mult_4[nota] + eventData2*step[8];
						  res_current =   (int)(ind_mult_4[nota]*decimat_23);  
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_mult_1[nota] = ind_mult_4[nota];
							ind_mult_2[nota] = ind_mult_4[nota];
							ind_mult_3[nota] = ind_mult_4[nota];
							ind_mult_0[nota] = ind_mult_4[nota]; 
						 }
						 
						 c0 =  cmd0_FRQ;
						 c1 = 4;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_4[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_4,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
							
					//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_3,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
					}
				
				
			}
 	 		if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
		
		
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&ringslide2); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_4,&led); 

				
					switch (ringslide2)
 					{
						case 0:
				
						 ind_mult_0[nota] = ind_mult_0[nota] - eventData2*step[8];
						 
						 if(ind_mult_0[nota]< 0.1){ind_mult_0[nota] = 0.1;} 
						 res_current =   (int)(ind_mult_0[nota]*decimat_23);  
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_mult_1[nota] = ind_mult_0[nota];
							ind_mult_2[nota] = ind_mult_0[nota];
							ind_mult_3[nota] = ind_mult_0[nota];
							ind_mult_4[nota] = ind_mult_0[nota]; 
						 }
						 
						 c0 =  cmd0_FRQ;
						 c1 = 0;
						 
						 SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_0[nota]);   
				 			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
						 	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_0,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_FAT_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
							
					//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_4,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
							
					//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_1,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_BLUE);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 1:
				
						 ind_mult_1[nota] = ind_mult_1[nota] - eventData2*step[8];
						 if(ind_mult_1[nota]< 0.1){ind_mult_1[nota] = 0.1;}
						 
						 res_current =   (int)(ind_mult_1[nota]*decimat_23);  
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_mult_0[nota] = ind_mult_1[nota];
							ind_mult_2[nota] = ind_mult_1[nota];
							ind_mult_3[nota] = ind_mult_1[nota];
							ind_mult_4[nota] = ind_mult_1[nota]; 
						 }
						 
						 c0 =  cmd0_FRQ;
						 c1 = 1;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_1[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_1,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_FAT_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
							
					//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_2,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
							
					//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_0,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_BLUE);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 2:
				
						 ind_mult_2[nota] = ind_mult_2[nota] - eventData2*step[8];
						 
						 if(ind_mult_2[nota]< 0.1){ind_mult_2[nota] = 0.1;} 
						 
						 res_current =   (int)(ind_mult_2[nota]*decimat_23);  
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_mult_1[nota] = ind_mult_2[nota];
							ind_mult_0[nota] = ind_mult_2[nota];
							ind_mult_3[nota] = ind_mult_2[nota];
							ind_mult_4[nota] = ind_mult_2[nota]; 
						 }
						 
						 c0 =  cmd0_FRQ;
						 c1 = 2;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_2[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_2,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);

					//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
							
					//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_3,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 3:
				
						 ind_mult_3[nota] = ind_mult_3[nota] - eventData2*step[8];
						 
						 if(ind_mult_3[nota]< 0.1){ind_mult_3[nota] = 0.1;} 
						 
						 res_current =   (int)(ind_mult_3[nota]*decimat_23);  
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_mult_1[nota] = ind_mult_3[nota];
							ind_mult_2[nota] = ind_mult_3[nota];
							ind_mult_0[nota] = ind_mult_3[nota];
							ind_mult_4[nota] = ind_mult_3[nota]; 
						 }
						 
						 c0 =  cmd0_FRQ;
						 c1 = 3;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_3[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_3,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
							
					//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_2,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
							
					//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_4,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 4:
				
						 ind_mult_4[nota] = ind_mult_4[nota] - eventData2*step[8];
						 if(ind_mult_4[nota]< 0.1){ind_mult_4[nota] = 0.1;} 
						 res_current =   (int)(ind_mult_4[nota]*decimat_23);  
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_mult_1[nota] = ind_mult_4[nota];
							ind_mult_2[nota] = ind_mult_4[nota];
							ind_mult_3[nota] = ind_mult_4[nota];
							ind_mult_0[nota] = ind_mult_4[nota]; 
						 }
						 
						 c0 =  cmd0_FRQ;
						 c1 = 4;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ind_mult_4[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_4,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);

					//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
					}
			
			}
			
			forbid_notes_send = 1;
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_FRQ; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = c1;
	
			k = 0;
			for(i = 0; i < count; i ++)
			{
				switch (ringslide2)
					{
						case 0:   
							res =   (int)(ind_mult_0[i]*decimat_23);
						break;
						case 1:   
							res =   (int)(ind_mult_1[i]*decimat_23);
						break;
						case 2:   
							res =   (int)(ind_mult_2[i]*decimat_23);
						break;
						case 3:   
							res =   (int)(ind_mult_3[i]*decimat_23);
						break;
						case 4:   
							res =   (int)(ind_mult_4[i]*decimat_23);
						break;
						
					}
				
				//	res =   100; //(int)(0.00001*16777216);  
				
			
					
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  
	
		//	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
			
			forbid_notes_send = 0;
/////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_10, res_current); 
			
			msec = 242000.0*1/res_current;
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC, msec);
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, ATTR_XAXIS_GAIN, 0.0012);
			Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			 
			
			
	//		mode_addr_shift =  6;
	//		Send_param_pack4(disp[nota],disp[nota],disp[nota]);
		 }
		 
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[8] = step[8]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[8] = step[8]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,step[8]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}




int CVICALLBACK toggle_fb (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
		int val;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
	int err;
	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		
		//	GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_7, &val);
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_VOL, &val); 
			
			
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_toggle_fb;
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x03; 
						   
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x1;
		
			sendBuff[7]	 = val;
		
			sendBuff[8] = 0x23;
	
 //       	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 9);	

			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}



int CVICALLBACK copy_coeff (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,numstr;
	
	int nn,ni;
	
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr); 
			
			nn = (int)numstr/2;
			ni = numstr - nn*2;
			for(j = 0; j < 256; j++)
			{
				 Copy_ci_cos[j] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256];
				 Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_cos[j];
				
				 Copy_ci_str[j] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][j + ni*256];   
				 Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_str[j] ; 
			}
		

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK paste_coeff (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,numstr; 
	int nn,ni;
	int num;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_100,&num);
			nn = (int)numstr/2;
			ni = numstr - nn*2;
			
			for(j = num; j < 256; j++)
			{
				  Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_cos[j]; 
				  Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_cos[j]; 
				
				 Ci_coeff_str[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_str[j]; 
				 Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_str[j]; 

			}
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, Copy_ci_cos,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, Copy_ci_str,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);   

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)

		case EVENT_COMMIT:

			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_74, &fb_mult);  
			
if(all_single == 0)
			{
				
				//	gen_test_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_91, EVENT_COMMIT, 0, 0, 0);
					GenShape_4(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_87, EVENT_COMMIT, 0, 0, 0);

						}

					{

							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff;

int CVICALLBACK save_all_ci (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
		/*		ArrayToFile("Ci_str.txt", Ci_str , VAL_DOUBLE, NumString*harm_num, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
				
		
				ArrayToFile("Ci_coef_sin.txt", Ci_coeff_sin , VAL_DOUBLE, NumString*harm_num, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
		*/		
				   	ArrayToFile("Ci_coef_cos.txt", Ci_coeff_cos , VAL_DOUBLE, NumString*2*harm_num, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
					
					ArrayToFile("Ci_coef_str.txt", Ci_coeff_str , VAL_DOUBLE, NumString*2*harm_num, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
					
					
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}



int CVICALLBACK Button_FB (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char *char_val;
	int nn,ni,i;
	
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
/*			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE, 3);
				display_m(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				gen_test_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_91, EVENT_COMMIT, 0, 0, 0);   
*/			
			
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("FB");
			param_num = 0;
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FB,alpha[nota]); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FB,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0);
			 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, alpha,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
			
		//	SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FB,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				alpha[nota] = alpha[nota] + eventData2*step[9];
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				alpha[nota] = alpha[nota] - eventData2*step[9];		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FB,alpha[nota]);
			
			mode_addr_shift =  10;
			
		//	mode_addr_shift =  0;  
		
						Send_param_pack4(nota,(alpha[nota]),(alpha[nota]),(alpha[nota]));  	
				
		
	//		gen_test_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_91, EVENT_COMMIT, 0, 0, 0);   
	//		send_shape (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_22, EVENT_COMMIT, 0, 0, 0);
	//	
		 }
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, alpha,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[9] = step[9]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[9] = step[9]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FB,step[9]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FB,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_DEL,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK notes_off (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	int i;

	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 

	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			for( i = 0; i <88; i++)
			{
				
				sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
				sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
				sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
				sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
				sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
				sendBuff[7]	 = i; 
				sendBuff[8]	 = 0; 
			
		
		
				sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
		//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
				UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
				Delay(0.001);
				
				
			}
			
							sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
							sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_pedal;
							sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
							sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
							sendBuff[6]	 = 0x01;
		
							sendBuff[7]	 = 127; 
		
							sendBuff[8] = 0x23;
																				 
							UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 9);
			
	                
		
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK scale_set_2 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
int val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
			

			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_29, &val); 
			
		
			if(val == 0)
			{
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_LOCK,-0.001,0.001); 
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_3, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_LOCK,0.0,1.0);  
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_4, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_LOCK,-0.001,0.001);  
			}
			else
			{
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,-0.001,0.001);
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_3, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,1.0);
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_4, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,-0.001,0.001); 
			
			}
			
			RefreshGraph (panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2);
			RefreshGraph (panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_4);   
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_scale_Mode_3 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
double min, max;
int toggle_q;
int elements,i,j;
double temp[harm_num];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_34, &min);  
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_33, &max); 
				 
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_3, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,min,max);  
				
				
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Yamaha_player (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	int a;
	int MIDI_Y;
	int MIDI_P;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_11, &player_device);
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_76, &MIDI_Y);
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_77, &MIDI_P);
			
				
				
				midiInStop( hMIDhnd); 
				midiInClose(hMIDhnd);
				notes_off (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_29, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0) ;
				
				if(player_device == 0)   //Moscow
				{
					nDev = MIDI_Y;	
				}
				if(player_device == 1) 
				{
					nDev = MIDI_P;		 //Tel Aviv
				}
				
				a =	 midiInGetNumDevs();
			
				midErr = midiInOpen(&(hMIDhnd),nDev,(DWORD)&midProc,dwUserParam,CALLBACK_FUNCTION);
				midErr = midiInStart(hMIDhnd);
			
		

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}



int CVICALLBACK omega_adjust (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char *char_val;
	
	double val;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
		//	char_val = StrDup ("Tn");
		//	param_num = 0;
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36,&Harmonic);
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_OMEGA_ADJUST,omega_coef[Harmonic]);
		//	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_OMEGA_ADJUST,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, omega_coef,  harm_num, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
		
		
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_OMEGA_ADJUST,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				omega_coef[Harmonic] = omega_coef[Harmonic] + eventData2*step[11];
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				omega_coef[Harmonic] = omega_coef[Harmonic] - eventData2*step[11];		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_OMEGA_ADJUST,omega_coef[Harmonic]);
			
			Send_Omega( panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_5,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
		
			Delay(0.1);
			
		 }
		 
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[11] = step[11]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[11] = step[11]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_OMEGA_ADJUST,step[11]);  
			 
		 }
		 
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
		 PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, omega_coef,  harm_num, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
		 VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_OMEGA_ADJUST,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_OMEGA_ADJUST,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK log_set (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_2,&val);
			if(val == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LOG); 
			}
			else
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LINEAR); 	
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_all (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val,i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
				for(i = 0; i <14;i++)
				{
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, btn_list_exibition[i],ATTR_DIMMED ,1);	
				}
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON,1);
			Reset(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			Delay(0.1); 
		//	gen_test_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_91, EVENT_COMMIT, 0, 0, 0);  
			
			// start upload
			
			
			
			send_pitch();
			Send_params(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_16, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			Flag_Upload = 1;
			send_all_molot(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_50, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			Delay(0.01);
			Flag_Upload = 0;
//////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

	//		Gn_all_shape4(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_88, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			send_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_22, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);

			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_8,0); 
			
	
	//		send_all_molot(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_50, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			send_exp_coef(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_58, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			Delay(0.01);   
			
			
/////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////			
			
			send_udar_param(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_53, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			Delay(0.01);
			
			
			
			///fb & nl

		//	GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_12,&val);
		//	send_nl_slave(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_12, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  //nl slave

			send_mass();
			
			SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_RING_15,2);
			force_string_osc(panelHandle, PANEL_RING_15,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			
			 SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_27,1); 
			 init_DAC (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_27,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			 
			 
			SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_35,1); 
			show_hide_picture (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_35,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			
			 SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_11,1);
			 Yamaha_player (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_11,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
			 
			 
				decode_surface();
				
	//			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				
	//			show_play_pan(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_3,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
	//			send_fill(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_2,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
			 
			
			
	//		send_nl(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_8,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
//			send_FIR(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_60,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			
			Delay(0.001); 
	//		send_sdvig_in(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_98,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
	//		send_sdvig_slave(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			
			
			SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_RING_SEL_OUT, 3);
//			sw_str_svertka(panelHandle, PANEL_RING_SEL_OUT,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			
			FIR_sel(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_3,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
			
		//	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_3, 0.2); 
		//	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_17,2); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_16,12); 
			
		//	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_16,0); 
		//	send_all_FRQ (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_25, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);

			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON,0);

				for(i = 0; i <14;i++)

				}

	}
