}

int CVICALLBACK log_linear_sw_1 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	int log_sw_X,log_sw_Y;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_LOG_2,&log_sw_Y);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_LOG_3,&log_sw_X); 
			
			if( log_sw_Y == 0)
			{
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LINEAR);
				
			}
			if( log_sw_Y == 1)
			{
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LOG);
				
			}
			
			if( log_sw_X == 1)
			{
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LOG);	
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LOG);}
			if( log_sw_X == 0)
			{
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LINEAR);
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4,ATTR_YMAP_MODE,VAL_LINEAR);    
				
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}



int CVICALLBACK molot_sw (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	int i;
	double plotbuff[harm_num];
	double plotbuff_1[harm_num]; 
	
	
	
	int led_force;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&val);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_5,&led_force);
			
			level = val;
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			
			okt_num = (int)(nota/12);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_OKT_NUM,okt_num);  
		
			
	//		SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE,4);
	//		display_m (panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE, EVENT_COMMIT,0, 0,0);
			
			if(led_force == 0)
			{
			
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,88.0);
			}
			else
			{
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,255.0);
			}
			
		//	SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,255.0);
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
			
			if(led_force == 0)
			{
				switch(val)
				{
					case 0:
					
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,2);
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_0,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
					
				
					break;
					case 1:
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,31);
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_1,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
					break;
					case 2:
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,63); 
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_2,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
					break;
					case 3:
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,95);   
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_3,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
					
					break;
					case 4:
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,127);   
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_mult_4,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
					
					break;
				
				}
			}
			else
			{
						
				switch(val)
				{
					case 0:
					
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,2);
					break;
					
					case 1:
					
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,31);
					break;	
					
					case 2:
					
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,63);
					break;	
					
					case 3:
					
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,95);
					break;
					
					case 4:
					
						SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,127);
					break;
				}
					
					
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E0, exp_e[0][level][nota]);
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E1, exp_e[1][level][nota]);
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E2, exp_e[2][level][nota]);
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E3, exp_e[3][level][nota]);
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E4, exp_e[1][level][nota]); 
						
												
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X0, exp_d[0][level][nota]);
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X1, exp_d[1][level][nota]);
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X2, exp_d[2][level][nota]);
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X3, exp_d[3][level][nota]);
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X4, exp_d[1][level][nota]); 
						
												
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A0, exp_a[0][level][nota]);
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A1, exp_a[1][level][nota]);
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A2, exp_a[2][level][nota]);
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A3, exp_a[3][level][nota]);
						SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A4, exp_a[1][level][nota]);
						
						Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				
			}
			
			//	DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );      
			//	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff_1,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			//			VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW); 
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

				construct_molot(nota);
				Delay(0.01);
			}
			
			a = shape_to_flash[0][0];
			
			;

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK constr_ci_str_single_invert (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double x,y;                     	
	static int xint; 
	double plotbuff[harm_num];
	double plotbuff_red[harm_num];
	int i,j,nn,ni;
	int val;
	
	int mode;
	int numstr;
	double min, max; 
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
							nn = (int)(numstr/2);
							ni = numstr - nn*2;
			
			
						   elements = 128;
							
							for(j = 0; j < 128; j++)    
							{
						
											plotbuff[j] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256];
						
							}
							
			 for(i = 0; i < 128 - 1; i++)
			 {
				plotbuff_red[i] = 0; 
				// first
				 if( (omega_coef[i] <= Notes_freqs[numstr])&&(omega_coef[i + 1] > Notes_freqs[numstr]))
				 {
					plotbuff_red[i] = plotbuff[i];  	 
				 }
			 
				 if( (omega_coef[i] <= 2.0*Notes_freqs[numstr])&&(omega_coef[i + 1] > 2.0*Notes_freqs[numstr]))
				 {
					plotbuff_red[i] = plotbuff[i];  	 
				 }
				 if( (omega_coef[i] <= 3.0*Notes_freqs[numstr])&&(omega_coef[i + 1] > 3.0*Notes_freqs[numstr]))
				 {
					plotbuff_red[i] = plotbuff[i];  	 
				 }
				 if( (omega_coef[i] <= 4.0*Notes_freqs[numstr])&&(omega_coef[i + 1] > 4.0*Notes_freqs[numstr]))
				 {
					plotbuff_red[i] = plotbuff[i];  	 
				 }
				 
				 
				 
			 
			 }
							
							
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_red,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);    
						
				
						
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK ind_ci_cos (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double val;
	double plotbuff[harm_num]; 
	int i,j,nn,ni;
	int numstr,elements;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
				GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA, &nota); 
				GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_4,&val);
				SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_LED_3,0);
				
				Ci_str_curve_cos[nota] = val; 
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, Ci_str_curve_cos,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
							
				nn = (int)(nota/2);
				ni = nota - nn*2;
							
							
				for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
								
				{
						Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256] = val*Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][i + ni*256]; 
						plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256];	
				}
				doctor_ci(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_25,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
				send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
				

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK ind_ci_STR (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double val,val1;
	double plotbuff[harm_num]; 
	int i,j,nn,ni;
	int numstr,elements;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA, &nota);
		
				SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_LED_3,1);
				
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_13,&val);
			Ci_str_curve[nota] = val;
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, Ci_str_curve,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
			
			
			
			
							nn = (int)(nota/2);
							ni = nota - nn*2;
							
							
				for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
								
				{
						Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] = val*Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][i + ni*256]; 
						plotbuff[i] = 	Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256];  
				}				
						
			
				
			doctor_ci(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_25,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
									
									
	//		send_single_Ci_str (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_13, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_udar_param (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	char *char_val;
	unsigned int lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 	
	int i,k;
	int c1;
	int res;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0};
	double decimat_24 = 16777215;
	double decimat_23 = 65000.0; 
	double ind_mult_0_send[88];
	double temp;

	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_55,&pp); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_56,&p); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_57,&mf); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_58,&f); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_68,&ff); 
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_72,&v_0); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_71,&v_1); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_70,&v_2); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_69,&v_3);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_107,&v_4);
		

	for(c1 = 0; c1 < 5; c1++)
	{
	////////////////////////////////ind_mult
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_FRQ;
			sendBuff[4]	 = c1;
	
			k = 0;
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i ++)
			{
				    switch(c1)
					{
						case 0:
							
						res =   (int)(ind_mult_0[i]*decimat_23);
						break;
						case 1:
						res =   (int)(ind_mult_1[i]*decimat_23);
						break;
						case 2:
						res =   (int)(ind_mult_2[i]*decimat_23);
						break;
						case 3:
						res =   (int)(ind_mult_3[i]*decimat_23);
						break;
						case 4:
						res =   (int)(ind_mult_4[i]*decimat_23);
						break;
					
					}
						
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 
			Delay(0.001); 
			
	}		
			
		
	for(c1 = 0; c1 < 5; c1++)
	{
			///////////////ind_vol
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd_total_vol; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = c1;
	
			k = 0;
			for(i = 0; i < 88 ; i ++)
			{
				    switch(c1)
					{
						case 0:   
							res =   (int)(v_0*ind_vol_0[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						case 1:   
							res =   (int)(v_1*ind_vol_1[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						case 2:   
							res =   (int)(v_2*ind_vol_2[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						case 3:   
							res =   (int)(v_3*ind_vol_3[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						case 4:   
							res =   (int)(v_4*ind_vol_4[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
					/*	
						case 5:   
							res =   (int)(v_4*ind_tail_0[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						case 6:   
							res =   (int)(v_4*ind_tail_1[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						case 7:   
							res =   (int)(v_4*ind_tail_2[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						case 8:   
							res =   (int)(v_4*ind_tail_3[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						case 9:   
							res =   (int)(v_4*ind_tail_4[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						
				*/		
						
						
					}
					
					
				//	res =   100; //(int)(0.00001*16777216);  
					
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  
			Delay(0.001); 
			
	}		
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK FIR_sel (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
int val;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
	int a0,a1;

	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_3, &val);
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_fb_input; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[7]	 = val & 0x000000FF; 
			sendBuff[8]  = 0x00;
			sendBuff[9]  = 0x23;
		
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10); 
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
	

			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_FIR (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,j,k,cc;
	int res;
	double decimat_32 =  8388607;
	int temp[Fir_length];
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			Transmit_Command  =   cmd0_fir;
	//		Transmit_Command_1  =   cmd0_fir_1;
			send_FIR_subr();

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Vol_set (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	unsigned int val;
	unsigned char vval;
	unsigned char val_slice_hi_2;
	unsigned char val_slice_lo_5; 
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0};     
	
	double temp[256],first_temp[256],second_temp[256];
	
	double temp_[256],first_temp_[256],second_temp_[256];  
	
	double plotbuff[256];
	double pp,p,mf,f,ff;
	double delta_t;
	double time;
	
	int nn,ni;
	
	double A;
	
	int i,j;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_VAL_CHANGED:
			
		//	nota = 24;
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA, &nota); 
			okt_num = (int)(nota/12);  
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_55,&pp); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_56,&p); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_57,&mf); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_58,&f); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_68,&ff); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,&val);
			
//			val = 32 + (int)((double)val*96.0/128.0); 
			
			vval = val;
			
			val_slice_hi_2 =  ((vval) & 0x60) >> 5;  //1100000
			val_slice_lo_5 =  (vval) & 0x1F;		 //0011111
			
			
				A = (double)val_slice_lo_5/32.0;
				Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
									
									
 		notes_off (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_29, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
		
		Delay(0.05);
			
	
		 
				sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
				sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
				sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
				sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
				sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
				sendBuff[7]	 = nota; 
				sendBuff[8]	 = val; 
			
		
		
				sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
		//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
				UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
				
			break;
			
			case EVENT_COMMIT:
				
				
					nn = (int)(nota/2);
						ni = nota - nn*2;
						
						for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256];  
				
						}
						 
				
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256];  
				
						}
						 
				
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
						
						
				
	;
			
//			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA, &nota); 
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_VOL, &val); 
			
			
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_toggle_fb;
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x03; 
						   
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x2;
		
			sendBuff[7]	 = val;
			
			sendBuff[8]	 = nota; 
		
			sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
        //	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10); 
			Delay(0.005);		
/*			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_toggle_fb;
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x03; 
						   
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x2;
		
			sendBuff[7]	 = 0;
			
			sendBuff[8]	 = nota; 
		
			sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
        	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
*/				
				
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK load_fFIR (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	

	int i,j;
	double delta_250, delta_48;
	double cc;
	int work_frq;
	int khz_96_on_off;
	
	double xmax,xmin;
	int imax,imin;
	double a,b;
	
	double first_derr[16384],second_derr[16384];
	double result_L[3*Fir_length];	   
	double result_R[3*Fir_length];
	double integral_R,integral_L,multiplier;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			if(dir_selected_flag_fir !=0)
			{
			
				DirSelectPopup (dir_name_fir, "choose dir", 1, 0, dir_name_fir);
				letter_fir = dir_name[0] - 97;
			
				SetDrive (letter_fir);
				SetDir (dir_name_fir);
				
			}
			else
			{
				DirSelectPopup ("", "choose dir", 1, 0, dir_name_fir);
				letter_fir = dir_name[0] - 97;
			
				SetDrive (letter_fir);
				SetDir (dir_name_fir);
				
			}
			dir_selected_flag_fir = 1;
			
			 SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_2, " ");

             SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_2,dir_name_fir);
			 
			 
			 	FileToArray ("impulse_resp_L.txt", impulse_resp_Left, VAL_DOUBLE,3*Fir_length, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				FileToArray ("impulse_resp_R.txt", impulse_resp_Right, VAL_DOUBLE, 3*Fir_length, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);

				FileToArray ("impulse_resp_LS.txt", result_L, VAL_DOUBLE,3*Fir_length, 1,

				FileToArray ("impulse_resp_RS.txt", result_R, VAL_DOUBLE, 3*Fir_length, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,128.0); 
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,128.0);

				{

			//		impulse_resp_Right[i] = impulse_resp_Right[i] +result_R[i];

					impulse_resp_Left[i] = 0.5*(impulse_resp_Left[i] +  impulse_resp_Right[i]);
					impulse_resp_Right[i] =  impulse_resp_Left[i];
					
				}
				
	////////////end mono
				Copy1D(impulse_resp_Left,3*Fir_length,impulse_resp_Left_tmp);
				Copy1D(impulse_resp_Right,3*Fir_length,impulse_resp_Right_tmp);
		
				
				prepare_FIR();
				
				
				
				
				/*	
				if(khz_96_on_off == 0)
				{
					

				
							delta_250 = 1.0/(100000000.0/1024.00);  
							delta_48 = 1.0/48000.0;
						//	delta_48 = 1.0/96000.0;  
				
				
						impulse_resp_L[0] = 0;
						impulse_resp_L[1] = 0; 
				
						for(i = 2; i < 8191; i ++)
						{
							first_derr[i] = (impulse_resp_L[i + 1] - impulse_resp_L[i - 1])/2.0;
							second_derr[i] = impulse_resp_L[i + 1] + impulse_resp_L[i - 1] - 2*impulse_resp_L[i];
						}
				
						first_derr[0] = 0;
						first_derr[1] = 0;
						first_derr[8191] = 0;
						first_derr[8191] = 0; 
				
						second_derr[0] = 0;
						second_derr[1] = 0;
						second_derr[8191] = 0;
						second_derr[8191] = 0;

							cc = cc + delta_250;
							if(cc >= delta_48)
							{
								j++;
								cc = 0;
							}
						}
				
						impulse_resp_R[0] = 0;
						impulse_resp_R[1] = 0;

							second_derr[i] = impulse_resp_R[i + 1] + impulse_resp_R[i - 1] - 2*impulse_resp_R[i];
						}
				
						first_derr[0] = 0;
						first_derr[1] = 0;
						first_derr[8191] = 0;
						first_derr[8191] = 0;

						second_derr[8191] = 0;

							cc = cc + delta_250;

							{
								j++;
								cc = 0;
							}

						MaxMin1D(result_L, Fir_length, &xmax,&imax,&xmin,&imin); 
				
						if(xmax < -1.0*xmin){a = -1.0/xmin;}else{ a = 1.0/xmax; }
				
				
						MaxMin1D(result_R, Fir_length, &xmax,&imax,&xmin,&imin); 
				
						if(xmax < -1.0*xmin){b = -1.0/xmin;}else{ b = 1.0/xmax; } 
				
			
						for(i = 1; i < Fir_length; i ++)
						{
							impulse_resp_L[i] = a* result_L[i]; 
							impulse_resp_R[i] = b*result_R[i]; 
					
						}
				
				}
				
			*/
				
				
/*				
				
				integral_R = 0; 
				integral_L = 0;
				for(i = 0; i <  Fir_length*4;i++)
				{
					integral_R =  integral_R + impulse_resp_Right[i]*impulse_resp_Right[i];
					
					integral_L =  integral_L + impulse_resp_Left[i]*impulse_resp_Left[i];
				}
				
				multiplier = sqrt(integral_R/integral_L);
				
				for(i = 0; i <  Fir_length*4;i++)
				{
				
					impulse_resp_Left[i] = impulse_resp_Left[i]*multiplier;	
				}
				
				
		
				
				
				/*
						MaxMin1D(impulse_resp_L, Fir_length, &xmax,&imax,&xmin,&imin); 
				
						if(xmax < -1.0*xmin){a = -1.0/xmin;}else{ a = 1.0/xmax; }
				
				
						MaxMin1D(impulse_resp_R, Fir_length, &xmax,&imax,&xmin,&imin); 
				
						if(xmax < -1.0*xmin){b = -1.0/xmin;}else{ b = 1.0/xmax; } 
				
			
						for(i = 0; i < Fir_length; i ++)
						{
							impulse_resp_L[i] = impulse_resp_L[i]*a; 
							impulse_resp_R[i] = impulse_resp_R[i]*b; 
					
						}
					
				
				  
				
			  				
				for(i = 0; i < Fir_length; i++)
				{
					
					impulse_resp_R[i] = impulse_resp_Right[i]	;
					
					impulse_resp_R1[i] = impulse_resp_Right[i + Fir_length - 21]	;
					
					impulse_resp_R2[i] = impulse_resp_Right[i + 2*(Fir_length - 21)]	; 
					
					impulse_resp_R3[i] = impulse_resp_Right[i + 3*(Fir_length - 21)]	;
					
					
					impulse_resp_L[i] = impulse_resp_Left[i]	;
					
					impulse_resp_L1[i] = impulse_resp_Left[i + Fir_length - 21]	;
					
					impulse_resp_L2[i] = impulse_resp_Left[i + 2*(Fir_length - 21)]	; 
					
					impulse_resp_L3[i] = impulse_resp_Left[i + 3*(Fir_length - 21)]	;
					
					
				}

				
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
				
				PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_GRAPH,impulse_resp_Left,3*Fir_length, VAL_DOUBLE,
	    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);
				
				PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_GRAPH,impulse_resp_Right,3*Fir_length, VAL_DOUBLE,
	    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
				
*/				
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Button_SHTEG (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	
char *char_val;
double temp_length,length;
	
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("Shteg");
			param_num = 14;
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG,shteg[nota]); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, shteg,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
			
	//		GenShape_4(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_87,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);	
	//		Draw_Shape(); 
			
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
		
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
	   GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);  
		
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			 
		//	temp_val = (double)((int)shteg[nota]);
			 
			 temp_length = Pitch[nota][0][0]- shteg[nota];
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				shteg[nota] = shteg[nota] + (double)eventData2*step[17];
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				shteg[nota] = shteg[nota] - (double)eventData2*step[17]; 		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG,shteg[nota]);
			
		//	GenShape_4(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_87,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);	
		//	Draw_Shape();
		
			mode_addr_shift =  4;
			send_shteg(nota,shteg[nota]);
		//	send_shape (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_22, EVENT_COMMIT, 0, 0, 0); 
			length = Pitch[nota][0][0]- shteg[nota];
			ttn[nota] = ttn[nota]*(length/temp_length)*(length/temp_length);   
			mode_addr_shift = 0;
			Send_param_pack4(nota,ttn[nota],ttn[nota],ttn[nota]);
/*			
			if(nota >= start_dupple_note)
			{
				mode_addr_shift =  1;
				Send_param_pack4(nota,(ttn[nota] + dt[nota]),(ttn[nota] + dt[nota]),(ttn[nota] + dt[nota]));
			}
			if(nota >= start_tripple_note)
			{
				mode_addr_shift =  5;
				Send_param_pack4(nota,(ttn[nota] - dt[nota]),(ttn[nota] - dt[nota]),(ttn[nota] - dt[nota]));
			}
*/			
		 }
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, shteg,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[17] = step[17]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[17] = step[17]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG,step[17]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
	
	
}

int CVICALLBACK set_working_dir (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char buf[250]; 
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			DirSelectPopup (dir_name, "choose dir", 1, 0, dir_name);
				letter = dir_name[0] - 97;
				
				SetDrive (letter);
			SetDir (dir_name);
			
			dir_selected_flag = 1;
			
			 SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING, " ");

             SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_STRING,dir_name);

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK Button_Chem (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
char *char_val;
	
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			
			param_num = 15;
			
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("damping");
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_2,damping[nota]); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_2,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, damping,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
		
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_2,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
	
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				damping[nota] = damping[nota] + eventData2*step[18];
				if( damping[nota] < 0){ damping[nota] = 0;} 
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				damping[nota] = damping[nota] - eventData2*step[18];
				if( damping[nota] < 0){ damping[nota] = 0;} 
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_2,damping[nota]);
			mode_addr_shift =  8; 
			
			Send_param_pack4(nota,damping[nota],damping[nota],damping[nota]); 
	
		 }
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, damping,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[18] = step[18]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[18] = step[18]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_2,step[18]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_2,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_2,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
	

	
}

int CVICALLBACK send_ind_mult (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char *char_val;
	unsigned int lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int res;
	int ringslide2;
	int c1;
	int i,k;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0};
	
//	double decimat_23 = 8388608.0;
	double decimat_23 = 65000.0;   
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&ringslide2);
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_FRQ; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = ringslide2;
	
			k = 0;
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i ++)
			{
				switch (ringslide2)
					{
						case 0:   
							res =   (int)(ind_mult_0[i]*decimat_23);
							c1 = 0;
						break;
						case 1:   
							res =   (int)(ind_mult_1[i]*decimat_23);
							c1 = 1;
						break;
						case 2:   
							res =   (int)(ind_mult_2[i]*decimat_23);
							c1 = 2;
						break;
						case 3:   
							res =   (int)(ind_mult_3[i]*decimat_23);
							c1 = 3;
						break;
						case 4:   
							res =   (int)(ind_mult_4[i]*decimat_23);
							c1 = 4;
						break;
						
					}
			
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
//			ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);   		
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

					}
				
				
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
		
   					switch (ringslide2)
					{
						case 0:
				
						 ind_vol_0[nota] = ind_vol_0[nota] - eventData2*step[19];
						 
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_vol_1[nota] = ind_vol_0[nota];
							ind_vol_2[nota] = ind_vol_0[nota];
							ind_vol_3[nota] = ind_vol_0[nota];
							ind_vol_4[nota] = ind_vol_0[nota]; 
						 }
						 
						 
						 c1 = 0;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_2,ind_vol_0[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_vol_0,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_DK_GREEN);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 1:
				
						 ind_vol_1[nota] = ind_vol_1[nota] - eventData2*step[19];
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_vol_0[nota] = ind_vol_1[nota];
							ind_vol_2[nota] = ind_vol_1[nota];
							ind_vol_3[nota] = ind_vol_1[nota];
							ind_vol_4[nota] = ind_vol_1[nota];
							
						 }
						 
						 
						 
						 c1 = 1;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_2,ind_vol_1[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_vol_1,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 2:
				
						 ind_vol_2[nota] = ind_vol_2[nota] - eventData2*step[19];
						 
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_vol_1[nota] = ind_vol_2[nota];
							ind_vol_2[nota] = ind_vol_2[nota];
							ind_vol_3[nota] = ind_vol_2[nota];
							ind_vol_4[nota] = ind_vol_2[nota]; 
						
						 }
						 
						 
						 
						 c1 = 2;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_2,ind_vol_2[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_vol_2,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 3:
				
						 ind_vol_3[nota] = ind_vol_3[nota] - eventData2*step[19];
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_vol_0[nota] = ind_vol_3[nota];
							ind_vol_1[nota] = ind_vol_3[nota];
							ind_vol_2[nota] = ind_vol_3[nota];
							ind_vol_4[nota] = ind_vol_3[nota];
							
						 }
						 
						 
						 c1 = 3;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_2,ind_vol_3[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_vol_3,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_DK_CYAN);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 4:
				
						 ind_vol_4[nota] = ind_vol_4[nota] - eventData2*step[19];
						 
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_vol_0[nota] = ind_vol_4[nota];
							ind_vol_1[nota] = ind_vol_4[nota];
							ind_vol_2[nota] = ind_vol_4[nota];
							ind_vol_3[nota] = ind_vol_4[nota];
							 
						 }
						 
						 
						 c1 = 4;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_2,ind_vol_4[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_vol_4,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
				
					}
			
			}
			
		
			
/////////////////////////////////////
			
		
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd_total_vol; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = c1;
	
			k = 0;
			for(i = 0; i < count; i ++)
			{
				switch (ringslide2)
					{
						case 0:   
							res =   (int)(v_0*ind_vol_0[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						
						case 1:   
							res =   (int)(v_1*ind_vol_1[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						case 2:   
							res =   (int)(v_2*ind_vol_2[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						case 3:   
							res =   (int)(v_3*ind_vol_3[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						case 4:   
							res =   (int)(v_4*ind_vol_4[i]*8388607.5);
						break;
						
					}
				
				//	res =   100; //(int)(0.00001*16777216);  
					
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);     
	
//			ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
/////////////////////////////////////////////////////////			
			
	//		mode_addr_shift =  6;
	//		Send_param_pack4(disp[nota],disp[nota],disp[nota]);
		 }
		 
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[19] = step[19]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[19] = step[19]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_2,step[19]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_2,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_2,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK detune_off_on (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int detune,i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_23, &detune);
		
		if( detune == 0)
		{
			for(nota = 0; nota < 88; nota++)
			{
				mode_addr_shift = 0; 
				Send_param_pack4(nota,ttn[nota],ttn[nota],ttn[nota]);
			//	Delay(0.1);
			
				if(nota >= start_dupple_note)
				{
					mode_addr_shift =  1;
					Send_param_pack4(nota,(ttn[nota] + dt[nota]),(ttn[nota] + dt[nota]),(ttn[nota] + dt[nota]));
					if(nota >= start_tripple_note)
					{
						mode_addr_shift =  5;
						Send_param_pack4(nota,(ttn[nota] - dt[nota]),(ttn[nota] - dt[nota]),(ttn[nota] - dt[nota]));
					}
				}
			}
			
			
		}
		else
		{
			for(nota = 0; nota < 88; nota++)
			{
				mode_addr_shift = 0; 
				Send_param_pack4(nota,ttn[nota],ttn[nota],ttn[nota]);
			//	Delay(0.1);
			
				if(nota >= start_dupple_note)
				{
					mode_addr_shift =  1;
					Send_param_pack4(nota,ttn[nota],ttn[nota],ttn[nota]); 
					if(nota >= start_tripple_note)
					{
						mode_addr_shift =  5;
						Send_param_pack4(nota,ttn[nota],ttn[nota],ttn[nota]); 
					}
				}
			}
			
		}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK copy_vol (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&val);
			switch(val)
			{
				case 4:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{ 	
						copied_vol[i] = ind_vol_4[i];
				
					}
			
				break;
				case 3:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++) 
					{ 	
						copied_vol[i] = ind_vol_3[i];
				
					}
			
				break;	
				case 2:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++) 
					{ 	
						copied_vol[i] = ind_vol_2[i];
				
					}
			
				break;
				case 1:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++) 
					{ 	
						copied_vol[i] = ind_vol_1[i];
				
					}
			
				break;	
				
				case 0:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++) 
					{ 	
						copied_vol[i] = ind_vol_0[i];
				
					}
			
				break;	
				
				
				
				
			}
		
			break;
	}
	return 0;
	
}

int CVICALLBACK  set_transmit_limits(int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RING_3,&val);
			
			switch(val)
			{
				case 0:	   //32 Lo
					
					lo_nota_limit = 0;
	                hi_nota_limit = 32; 
	
		 			lo_grp_num = 0;
		 			hi_grp_num = 2;
					
					
				break;
				case 1:	   //Hi 24
					
					lo_nota_limit = 64;
	                hi_nota_limit = 88; 
	
		 			lo_grp_num = 5;
		 			hi_grp_num = 8;
					
					
				break;
				
				case 2:	   //Mid 32
					lo_nota_limit = 32;
	                hi_nota_limit = 64; 
	
		 			lo_grp_num = 2;
		 			hi_grp_num = 4;					
					
				break;
				case 3:	   //All
					lo_nota_limit = 0;
	                hi_nota_limit = 88; 
	
		 			lo_grp_num = 0;
		 			hi_grp_num = 10;					
					
				break;
				
				case 4:	   //Mid 40 
					lo_nota_limit = 24;
	                hi_nota_limit = 28; 
	
		 			lo_grp_num = 1;
		 			hi_grp_num = 2;					
					
				break;
				
					case 5:	   //0 - 80
					lo_nota_limit = 0;
	                hi_nota_limit = 80; 
	
		 			lo_grp_num = 1;
		 			hi_grp_num = 2;					
					
				break;
				
				case 6:	   //12 - 76
					lo_nota_limit = 12;
	                hi_nota_limit = 76; 
	
		 			lo_grp_num = 1;
		 			hi_grp_num = 2;					
					
				break;
				case 7:	   //
					lo_nota_limit = 40;
	                hi_nota_limit = 48; 
	
		 			lo_grp_num = 1;
		 			hi_grp_num = 2;					
					
				break;
				case 8:	   //44 -52
					lo_nota_limit = 44;
	                hi_nota_limit = 52; 
	
		 			lo_grp_num = 1;
		 			hi_grp_num = 2;					
					
				break;
				case 9:	   //0 - 52
					lo_nota_limit = 0;
	                hi_nota_limit = 48; 
	
		 			lo_grp_num = 1;
		 			hi_grp_num = 2;					
					
				break;
				case 10:	   //66 - 88
					lo_nota_limit = 66;
	                hi_nota_limit = 88; 
	
		 			lo_grp_num = 1;
		 			hi_grp_num = 2;					
					
				break;	
				
				
			}

		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result; 
	int i,k,j;

	int res;
	int LED; 
	double decimat_20 = 1048576.0;
	
	double decimat_24 = 16777215.0;
	double decimat_25 = 16777215.0*2.0; 
	double decimat_28 = 268435456.0; 
	double decimat_30 = 1073741824.0; 
	double decimat_32 = 4294967296.0; 
	double decimat_31 = 2147483648.0; 
	
	double val; 
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11, &val); 
			{
				amp_deka = val;
				send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
			
			}
			break;
	}
	return 0;
	

}

int CVICALLBACK gen_test_shape (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double tt[1000];
	double tt_sdv[256];
	double ww;
	double ddel;
	double ddd,dx;
	double a,b,c, D;
	double X[1000],Y[1000], x_val;
	double secondDerivatives[1000];
	int i;
	int val;
	int numberOfElements;
	double xmax,xmin,ceil;
	int imax,imin;
	int mute,shift;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_15, &val);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_102, &ceil);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_22, &mute);
			
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_10, &shift);
			
			
			ww = width[nota];
			ddel = del[nota];
			numberOfElements = 256;
			dx = 1.0/256.0;
			
			/////////////////////////////
			
		 //   amp0*exp(-1.0*ex0*((double)i*dt - dist0)*((double)i*dt - dist0)) +
		
			
			//////////////////////////////
			
			if(val == 0)  // VITALIK
			{
				for(i = 0; i < numberOfElements; i++)
				{
					tt[i] = exp(-1.0*ww*((double)i*dx - ddel)*((double)i*dx - ddel));
					
				}
			
			}
			else	    // GAUSS    
			{
				for(i = 0; i < numberOfElements; i++)
				{
					
					c = 0.5 - (double)i/(double)numberOfElements;
					
					a = c*c - 2*ddel*c + ddel*ddel + ww*ww - 1;
					
					D = 4*ww*ww - 4*a;
					tt[i] = (2*ww + sqrt(D))/2;
					if(tt[i] < 0) tt[i] = 0;
					   
					
				}
			
			
			}
		MaxMin1D(tt, numberOfElements, &xmax,&imax,&xmin,&imin); 
			
			if(xmax < -1.0*xmin){b = -1.0/xmin;}else{ b = 1.0/xmax; } 
				
			
						for(i = 0; i < numberOfElements; i ++)
						{
							 
							tt[i] = tt[i]*b; 
					
						}
						
						for(i = 0; i < numberOfElements; i ++)
						{
						
						
							if(tt[i] > ceil) tt[i] = ceil;
							
						}
			
			
			///////////spline interp
			
	for(i = 0; i < numberOfElements;i++)
	{
		
		X[i] = (double)i/(double)numberOfElements;
		Y[i] = tt[i];
	}
	
	Spline (X, tt,numberOfElements,0,0,secondDerivatives); 
	
	for(i = 0; i < 256;i++)
	{
		shape_256[nota][i] = 0;	
	}
	
	for(i = 0; i < 256;i++)
	{
		ddd = (double)i/256.0 + ddel;
		if(ddd <0 || ddd > 1)
		{
			tt_sdv[i]  = 0;	
		}
		else
		{
			if(mute ==0)
			{
			SpInterp (X, Y,secondDerivatives,numberOfElements, ddd, &shape_256[nota][i]);
			
			if(nota < 16)
			{
			shape_256[nota][i] = shape_256[nota][i]*FB[nota];
			}
			else
			{
				if(nota < 25)
				{
					shape_256[nota][i] = shape_256[nota][i]*FB[nota]/sqrt(2.0); 		
				}
				else
				{
					shape_256[nota][i] = shape_256[nota][i]*FB[nota]/sqrt(3.0); 	
				}
			}
			}
			else
			{
				shape_256[nota][i] = 0;
			}
		}

	}
	
	
	if(val == 0)  // VITALIK
			{
				for(i = 0; i < numberOfElements; i++)
				{
					shape_256[nota][i] = FB[nota]*exp(-1.0*ww*((double)i*dx - ddel)*((double)i*dx - ddel));
					
				}
			
			}
	
/*	   	
	for(i = 0; i < 256;i++)
	{
		shape_256[nota][i] = 0;	
	}
	

	
	 shift =  2;
		shape_256[nota][(int)shteg[nota] +2 + shift] = 0;//NL_disp[nota];
		shape_256[nota][(int)shteg[nota] +    shift] = -1.0*NL_disp[nota];
		shape_256[nota][(int)shteg[nota] +1 + shift] = 0;//NL_disp[nota];
		shape_256[nota][(int)shteg[nota] -1 + shift] = 0;//NL_disp[nota]; 
		shape_256[nota][(int)shteg[nota] -2 + shift] = 0;//NL_disp[nota]; 
	//	shape_256[nota][(int)shteg[nota] -3 + shift] = NL_disp[nota];
	//	shape_256[nota][(int)shteg[nota] +3 + shift] = NL_disp[nota];
			
		DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
		
		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, shape_256[nota],  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
 */
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}



int CVICALLBACK set_zero (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_3,0.0);
			send_FB(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_3,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_ci_coef_single (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int nn,ni,na;
	int i,k,d;
	int res;
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;   
	
	double decimat_31 = 2147483648;
	double decimat_24 = 16777216; 
	double temp[256];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA, &nota);
			
			
			
			nn = (int)nota/2;
			ni = nota - nn*2;

				sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
				sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_Ci_out; 
				sendBuff[4]	 = nota;
	
				k = 0;
				d = 0;
				
			
		
				for(i = 0; i < 256 ; i ++) 
				{
					
					if(mute_amps[i] > 0)
					{
					Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni* 256];
					res = Float_Result.int_val;
					}
					else
					{
					Float_Result.fl_val  = 0;
					res = Float_Result.int_val;	
					}
					
			
						lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
						mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
						hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
						hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

					sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
					sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
					sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
					sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
					k+=4;

				}
				Length232 = k;
				sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
				sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
				sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
		//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
				UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);    
			//	FlushOutQ (comport);
				Delay(0.001); 
				
			//////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////	
				sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
				sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_Ci_out; 
				sendBuff[4]	 = nota + 88;
	
				k = 0;
		
				for(i = 0; i < 256 ; i ++) 
				{
				
						
						Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256];
						res = Float_Result.int_val;
						
				//		res = Ci_coeff_str[nn][i]*decimat_31;    
			
						lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
						mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
						hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
						hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

					sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
					sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
					sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
					sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
					k+=4;

				}
				Length232 = k;
				sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
				sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
				sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
		//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
				UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  
			
				Delay(0.001); 	
  
			
		
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}



int CVICALLBACK zero_ci_str_all (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,nn,ni; 
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
		for(j = 0; j <  88; j++)
		{	
			for(i = 0; i < 512; i++)                                                  			
			{                                                                        			
				Ci_coeff_cos[j][i] = 0.0000; 
				Ci_coeff_str[j][i] = 0.0000; 
				
				
			}
			
		
		}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_ci_zeros (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA, i);
				nota = i;
				send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				
				Delay(0.02); 
				
			}
			
			 SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA, nota); 

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_scale_ci (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double min, max;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_95, &min);  
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_94, &max);  
			
	 	SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,min,max); 
		
		SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_4, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,min,max); 
		
		SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_3, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,min,max);    
		
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK scale16 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale); 
			if(flag_audio == 0)
			{
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,15); 
			}
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,15);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_4, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,15); 
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,15); 
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,15);
		//	SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_DEKA_2, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,15); 
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_4,ATTR_XAXIS_OFFSET,(double)offs_scale); 
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2,ATTR_XAXIS_OFFSET,(double)offs_scale); 
			
			elements = 16;

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK scale_fb (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	double scale;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_RING_5,&val);
			switch(val)
			{
				case 0:
					scale =  0.00001;
					
				break;
				case 1:
					scale =  0.0001;
					
				break;
				case 2:
					scale =  0.001;
					
				break;
				case 3:
					scale =  0.01;
					
				break;
				case 4:
					scale =  0.1;
					
				break;
				case 5:
					scale =  1.0;
					
				break;
				case 6:
					scale =  10.0;
					
				break;
				
				case 7:
					scale =  100.0;
					
				break;
				
				
			}
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_3,ATTR_MAX_VALUE, scale);
			
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK float_calculator (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int cntrl;
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			if(control == PANEL_NUMERIC_FL_VAL)
			{
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_FL_VAL,&Float_Result.fl_val);
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_INT_VAL,Float_Result.int_val);
				
			}
			
			if(control == PANEL_NUMERIC_INT_VAL)
			{
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_INT_VAL,&Float_Result.int_val);
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_FL_VAL,Float_Result.fl_val);
				
			}
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_sdvig_slave (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{   
	int val;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 

	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_97, &val);
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_sdvig; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[7]	 = val & 0x000000FF; 
			sendBuff[8]  = 0x00;
			sendBuff[9]  = 0x23;
		
			
			
		//	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10); 

			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK copy_ci_ci_str (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,nn,ni;
	double plotbuff[128];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
							
							
							GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&j);
							nn = (int)(j/2);
							ni = j - nn*2;
							
												   
							for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
								
							{
						//		Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] = -Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256]  ;
								
						//		Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][i + ni*256] = 0.0001*Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] ;  
								
									Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] = -Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256]*omega_coef[0]/(omega_coef[i]) ;
								
									Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][i + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] ;  
								plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256];
								
							}				
							
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);    
						
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK paste_force (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	int i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&val);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_OKT_NUM,&okt_num); 
			
			for( i = 0; i < harm_num;i++)
			{
				Force_vozb[val][okt_num][i] = copied_Force_graph[i] ;	
							
			}
					

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK display_vs_omega (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int nn,ni,i;
	int val;
	double plotbuff[128];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			nn = (int)(nota/2);
			ni = nota - nn*2;
			
				
						for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256];
				
						}
						GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_DISPLAY_VS_OMEGA,&val);
				
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
						if(val == 0)
						{
				//			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,ATTR_XMAP_MODE,VAL_LINEAR);
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);  
						}
						else
						{
							SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,-0.001,0.001);  
						PlotXY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,omega_coef, plotbuff, 64, VAL_DOUBLE,
							VAL_DOUBLE, VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_SQUARE, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);   
							
						}
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK paste_num (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,nn,ni,num;
	double plotbuff[128],plotbuff_1[128];
	
	int numstr; 
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_100,&num);
			nn = (int)numstr/2;
			ni = numstr - nn*2;
			for(j = 0; j <= num; j++)
			{
				  Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_cos[j]; 
				  Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_cos[j]; 
				
				
				 Ci_coeff_str[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_str[j]; 
			
				 Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_str[j];
			}
			
				for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
								
					{
						plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256];
						
						plotbuff_1[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256];
					}
			
			
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff_1,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);   

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK past_all_num (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,nn,ni,num;
	double plotbuff[128],plotbuff_1[128];
	
	int numstr; 
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_100,&num);
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
			{
				nn = (int)i/2;
				ni = i - nn*2;
				for(j = num; j < 256; j++)
				{
					  Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_cos[j]; 
					  Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_cos[j]; 
				
				
					 Ci_coeff_str[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_str[j]; 
			
					 Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_str[j];
				}
			
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK C_2_Cstr_all (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,nn,ni;
	double plotbuff[128];
	double mult_12,mult_24,mult;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
							mult_12 = sqrt(2.0);
							mult_24 = sqrt(3.0);
							
							for(j = 0; j < 88;j++)
							{
								nn = (int)(j/2);
								ni = j - nn*2;
						/*		
								if(j < 16){mult = 1.0;}
								else
								{
									if(j<28){mult = 1/mult_12;}
									else
									{
										mult = 1/mult_24; 		
									}
									
								}
									
						*/	
								mult = 1.0;				   
								for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
								
								{
							//		Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] = mult*omega_coef[0]* omega_coef[0]* Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256]/(omega_coef[j]*omega_coef[j] ) ;
							
							//		Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][i + ni*256] = mult*Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] ;  
									
									Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] = -Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256] ;
								
									Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][i + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] ;  
									
									
								}
							}
							
							
							
						
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK set_force_spline (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val,i,num_shape,xint,vel_on;
	int num_cursors;
	double x,y;
	double plotbuff[256];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_5,&val);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_103,&event_counter);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&num_shape);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_20,&vel_on);    
			
				if(val == 1)
				{
					if(vel_on == 0)
					{
					
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
					
						SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,event_counter);
					
						SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0,255.0);
						SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS,VAL_MANUAL,-1.0,1.0);
					
						for(i = 1; i < event_counter +1; i++)
						{
						
						
							SetCursorAttribute (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, i, ATTR_CURSOR_POINT_STYLE, VAL_EMPTY_CIRCLE);
							SetCursorAttribute (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, i, ATTR_CROSS_HAIR_STYLE, VAL_NO_CROSS);
	
	
						
							x = (double)(i - 1)*255.0/(double)(event_counter -1);
							xint = (int)x;
							X[i-1] = x;
							switch( num_shape)
							{
								case 0:
									y = Force_graph[xint];
									Y[i-1] = y; 
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
										plotbuff[i] = Force_graph[i];                                             			
									}
									
									
									break;
								case 1:
									y = Force_graph_1[xint];
									Y[i-1] = y;
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
										plotbuff[i] = Force_graph_1[i];                                             			
									}
									
								break;
							
								case 2:
									
									switch (okt_num )
									{ 
										case 0: 
												y = Force_graph_2_0[xint];
												Y[i-1] = y;
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
										{                                                                        			
											plotbuff[i] = Force_graph_2_0[i];                                             			
										}
												
										break;
										case 1: 
												y = Force_graph_2_1[xint];
												Y[i-1] = y; 
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
											plotbuff[i] = Force_graph_2_1[i];                                             			
										}			
										break; 
										case 2:

												Y[i-1] = y;
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
										{                                                                        			
											plotbuff[i] = Force_graph_2_2[i];                                             			
										}			
										break; 
										case 3:
												y = Force_graph_2_3[xint];
												Y[i-1] = y; 
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
										{                                                                        			
											plotbuff[i] = Force_graph_2_3[i];                                             			
										}			
										break; 
									}
									
								break;
							
								case 3:
									switch (okt_num )
									{ 
										case 0: 
												y = Force_graph_3_0[xint];
												Y[i-1] = y;
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
										{                                                                        			
											plotbuff[i] = Force_graph_3_0[i];                                             			
										}			
												
										break;
										case 1: 
												y = Force_graph_3_1[xint];
												Y[i-1] = y;
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
										{                                                                        			
											plotbuff[i] = Force_graph_3_1[i];                                             			
										}			
												
										break; 
										case 2:  
												y = Force_graph_3_2[xint];
												Y[i-1] = y;
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
										{                                                                        			
											plotbuff[i] = Force_graph_3_2[i];                                             			
										}			
												
										break; 
										case 3:
												y = Force_graph_3_3[xint];
												Y[i-1] = y; 
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
										{                                                                        			
											plotbuff[i] = Force_graph_3_3[i];                                             			
										}			
												
										break; 
									}
								break;
								case 4:
									switch (okt_num )
									{ 
										case 0: 
												y = Force_graph_4_0[xint];
												Y[i-1] = y;
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
										{                                                                        			
											plotbuff[i] = Force_graph_4_0[i];                                             			
										}			
												
										break;
										case 1: 
												y = Force_graph_4_1[xint];
												Y[i-1] = y;
									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
										{

										}

									for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)

							}
