						}
					
					
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
					
					}
					else
					{
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
					
						SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,event_counter);
					
						SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0,127.0);
						SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,127.0);
					
						for(i = 1; i < event_counter +1; i++)
						{
						
						
							SetCursorAttribute (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, i, ATTR_CURSOR_POINT_STYLE, VAL_EMPTY_CIRCLE);
							SetCursorAttribute (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, i, ATTR_CROSS_HAIR_STYLE, VAL_NO_CROSS);
	
	
						
							x = (double)(i - 1)*127.0/(double)(event_counter -1);
							xint = (int)x;
							X[i-1] = x;
							y = velocity[xint];
							Y[i-1] = y; 
							
						
							SetGraphCursor(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,i,x,y);
							}
						
					
									for(i = 0; i < 127; i++)                                                 			
									{                                                                        			
										plotbuff[i] = velocity[i];                                             			
									}
					
						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
						
					}
				
				}

			
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK Construct_force_shape(int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val,Vol,v,dv;
	int i,j,k;
	double dx,dt;
	double ex0,ex1,ex2,ex3,ex4;
	double dist0,dist1,dist2,dist3,dist4;
	double shift_down, shift_down0,shift_down1,shift_down2,shift_down3,shift_down4;
	double amp0,amp1,amp2,amp3,amp4;
	double d1,d2;
	double temp0[1000000],temp1[256],temp2[256],temp3[256],temp4[256];
	double result[256];
	double sign,sign1;
	double gain;
	int sign_int,sign1_int;
	double integr_0,integr_1,integr_2;
	int start_i_circle,end_i_circle,start_i_circle_1,end_i_circle_1;
	int dd;
	
	double ratio,diff,diff1,diff0,A,G,A_1,G_1;
	double x_circle,radius,x_circle_1,radius_1;
	int focus;
//	double decimat_23 = 65000.0;
	double decimat_23 = 8388607.0; 
	double decimat_32 = 2147000000;
	double decimat_24 = 8388607.0;
	double temp44;
	
	
	int length;
	double r0,r1,r2,r3,r4;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
	//		param_num = 500;
			
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&val);  
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,&Vol);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
		//	GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_OKT_NUM,&okt_num);
			
			
			
		
	/////////////////////////////////////////////		
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E0,&exp_e[0][val][nota]);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E1,&exp_e[1][val][nota]); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E2,&exp_e[2][val][nota]); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E3,&exp_e[3][val][nota]); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E4,&exp_e[4][val][nota]); 
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X0,&exp_d[0][val][nota]);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X1,&exp_d[1][val][nota]); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X2,&exp_d[2][val][nota]); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X3,&exp_d[3][val][nota]); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X4,&exp_d[4][val][nota]); 
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A0,&exp_a[0][val][nota]);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A1,&exp_a[1][val][nota]);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A2,&exp_a[2][val][nota]);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A3,&exp_a[3][val][nota]);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A4,&exp_a[4][val][nota]);
			
		
			v = (int)(Vol/32);
			dv = Vol - v*32;
			switch(v)
			{
				case 0:
					
				dt = ind_mult_0[nota] +  dv*(ind_mult_1[nota] - ind_mult_0[nota])/31;
				
				ex0 =   exp_e[0][0][nota] + dv*(exp_e[0][1][nota] - exp_e[0][0][nota] )/31;
				amp0 =  exp_a[0][0][nota] + dv*(exp_a[0][1][nota] - exp_a[0][0][nota] )/31;
				dist0 = exp_d[0][0][nota] + dv*(exp_d[0][1][nota] - exp_d[0][0][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex1 =   exp_e[1][0][nota] + dv*(exp_e[1][1][nota] - exp_e[1][0][nota] )/31;
				amp1 =  exp_a[1][0][nota] + dv*(exp_a[1][1][nota] - exp_a[1][0][nota] )/31;
				dist1 = exp_d[1][0][nota] + dv*(exp_d[1][1][nota] - exp_d[1][0][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex2 =   exp_e[2][0][nota] + dv*(exp_e[2][1][nota] - exp_e[2][0][nota] )/31;
				amp2 =  exp_a[2][0][nota] + dv*(exp_a[2][1][nota] - exp_a[2][0][nota] )/31;
				dist2 = exp_d[2][0][nota] + dv*(exp_d[2][1][nota] - exp_d[2][0][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex3 =   exp_e[3][0][nota] + dv*(exp_e[3][1][nota] - exp_e[3][0][nota] )/31;
				amp3 =  exp_a[3][0][nota] + dv*(exp_a[3][1][nota] - exp_a[3][0][nota] )/31;
				dist3 = exp_d[3][0][nota] + dv*(exp_d[3][1][nota] - exp_d[3][0][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex4 =   exp_e[4][0][nota] + dv*(exp_e[4][1][nota] - exp_e[4][0][nota] )/31;
				amp4 =  exp_a[4][0][nota] + dv*(exp_a[4][1][nota] - exp_a[4][0][nota] )/31;
				dist4 = exp_d[4][0][nota] + dv*(exp_d[4][1][nota] - exp_d[4][0][nota] )/31; 
				
				shift_down = ind_tail_0[nota] + dv*(ind_tail_1[nota] - ind_tail_0[nota] )/31;

					
				break;
				case 1:
					
				dt =  ind_mult_1[nota]    +  dv*(ind_mult_2[nota] - ind_mult_1[nota])/31;
				ex0 =   exp_e[0][1][nota] + dv*(exp_e[0][2][nota] - exp_e[0][1][nota] )/31;
				amp0 =  exp_a[0][1][nota] + dv*(exp_a[0][2][nota] - exp_a[0][1][nota] )/31;
				dist0 = exp_d[0][1][nota] + dv*(exp_d[0][2][nota] - exp_d[0][1][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex1 =   exp_e[1][1][nota] + dv*(exp_e[1][2][nota] - exp_e[1][1][nota] )/31;
				amp1 =  exp_a[1][1][nota] + dv*(exp_a[1][2][nota] - exp_a[1][1][nota] )/31;
				dist1 = exp_d[1][1][nota] + dv*(exp_d[1][2][nota] - exp_d[1][1][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex2 =   exp_e[2][1][nota] + dv*(exp_e[2][2][nota] - exp_e[2][1][nota] )/31;
				amp2 =  exp_a[2][1][nota] + dv*(exp_a[2][2][nota] - exp_a[2][1][nota] )/31;
				dist2 = exp_d[2][1][nota] + dv*(exp_d[2][2][nota] - exp_d[2][1][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex3 =   exp_e[3][1][nota] + dv*(exp_e[3][2][nota] - exp_e[3][1][nota] )/31;
				amp3 =  exp_a[3][1][nota] + dv*(exp_a[3][2][nota] - exp_a[3][1][nota] )/31;
				dist3 = exp_d[3][1][nota] + dv*(exp_d[3][2][nota] - exp_d[3][1][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex4 =   exp_e[4][1][nota] + dv*(exp_e[4][2][nota] - exp_e[4][1][nota] )/31;
				amp4 =  exp_a[4][1][nota] + dv*(exp_a[4][2][nota] - exp_a[4][1][nota] )/31;
				dist4 = exp_d[4][1][nota] + dv*(exp_d[4][2][nota] - exp_d[4][1][nota] )/31; 
				
				shift_down = ind_tail_1[nota] + dv*(ind_tail_2[nota] - ind_tail_1[nota] )/31; 
					
				break;
				case 2:
					
				dt =  ind_mult_2[nota] +  dv*(ind_mult_3[nota] - ind_mult_2[nota])/31;
					
				
				ex0 =   exp_e[0][2][nota] + dv*(exp_e[0][3][nota] - exp_e[0][2][nota] )/31;
				amp0 =  exp_a[0][2][nota] + dv*(exp_a[0][3][nota] - exp_a[0][2][nota] )/31;
				dist0 = exp_d[0][2][nota] + dv*(exp_d[0][3][nota] - exp_d[0][2][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex1 =   exp_e[1][2][nota] + dv*(exp_e[1][3][nota] - exp_e[1][2][nota] )/31;
				amp1 =  exp_a[1][2][nota] + dv*(exp_a[1][3][nota] - exp_a[1][2][nota] )/31;
				dist1 = exp_d[1][2][nota] + dv*(exp_d[1][3][nota] - exp_d[1][2][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex2 =   exp_e[2][2][nota] + dv*(exp_e[2][3][nota] - exp_e[2][2][nota] )/31;
				amp2 =  exp_a[2][2][nota] + dv*(exp_a[2][3][nota] - exp_a[2][2][nota] )/31;
				dist2 = exp_d[2][2][nota] + dv*(exp_d[2][3][nota] - exp_d[2][2][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex3 =   exp_e[3][2][nota] + dv*(exp_e[3][3][nota] - exp_e[3][2][nota] )/31;
				amp3 =  exp_a[3][2][nota] + dv*(exp_a[3][3][nota] - exp_a[3][2][nota] )/31;
				dist3 = exp_d[3][2][nota] + dv*(exp_d[3][3][nota] - exp_d[3][2][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex4 =   exp_e[4][2][nota] + dv*(exp_e[4][3][nota] - exp_e[4][2][nota] )/31;
				amp4 =  exp_a[4][2][nota] + dv*(exp_a[4][3][nota] - exp_a[4][2][nota] )/31;
				dist4 = exp_d[4][2][nota] + dv*(exp_d[4][3][nota] - exp_d[4][2][nota] )/31; 
				
				shift_down = ind_tail_2[nota] + dv*(ind_tail_3[nota] - ind_tail_2[nota] )/31; 
					
				break;
				case 3:
					
				dt =  ind_mult_3[nota] +  dv*(ind_mult_4[nota] - ind_mult_3[nota])/31;
				ex0 =   exp_e[0][3][nota] + dv*(exp_e[0][4][nota] - exp_e[0][3][nota] )/31;
				amp0 =  exp_a[0][3][nota] + dv*(exp_a[0][4][nota] - exp_a[0][3][nota] )/31;
				dist0 = exp_d[0][3][nota] + dv*(exp_d[0][4][nota] - exp_d[0][3][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex1 =   exp_e[1][3][nota] + dv*(exp_e[1][4][nota] - exp_e[1][3][nota] )/31;
				amp1 =  exp_a[1][3][nota] + dv*(exp_a[1][4][nota] - exp_a[1][3][nota] )/31;
				dist1 = exp_d[1][3][nota] + dv*(exp_d[1][4][nota] - exp_d[1][3][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex2 =   exp_e[2][3][nota] + dv*(exp_e[2][4][nota] - exp_e[2][3][nota] )/31;
				amp2 =  exp_a[2][3][nota] + dv*(exp_a[2][4][nota] - exp_a[2][3][nota] )/31;
				dist2 = exp_d[2][3][nota] + dv*(exp_d[2][4][nota] - exp_d[2][3][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex3 =   exp_e[3][3][nota] + dv*(exp_e[3][4][nota] - exp_e[3][3][nota] )/31;
				amp3 =  exp_a[3][3][nota] + dv*(exp_a[3][4][nota] - exp_a[3][3][nota] )/31;
				dist3 = exp_d[3][3][nota] + dv*(exp_d[3][4][nota] - exp_d[3][3][nota] )/31; 
				
				ex4 =   exp_e[4][3][nota] + dv*(exp_e[4][4][nota] - exp_e[4][3][nota] )/31;
				amp4 =  exp_a[4][3][nota] + dv*(exp_a[4][4][nota] - exp_a[4][3][nota] )/31;
				dist4 = exp_d[4][3][nota] + dv*(exp_d[4][4][nota] - exp_d[4][3][nota] )/31; 
				
				shift_down = ind_tail_3[nota] + dv*(ind_tail_4[nota] - ind_tail_3[nota] )/31;
					
				break;
			}
			
			decimat_23 = 65500.0; 
			
			dd = 0.5*(int)(dt*decimat_23);
			
			
			decimat_23 = 65000.0; 
			
			dist0 = dist0*decimat_23;
			dist1 = dist1*decimat_23;
			dist2 = dist2*decimat_23;
			dist3 = dist3*decimat_23;
			dist4 = dist4*decimat_23;
			
			
			decimat_23 = 2*1048576.0;
			
			
			
			ex0 = ex0/decimat_23;
			ex1 = ex1/decimat_23;
			ex2 = ex2/decimat_23;
			ex3 = ex3/decimat_23;
			ex4 = ex4/decimat_23;
			
			k = 0;
			i = 0;
			
			
//			if(dd == 0){dd = 0.0001;}
			
			while(k < 1000000000)
			{   
				i++;
				length = i;
	//			if(i == 1000000){ break;} 
				k = k + dd;	
				
			}
			
			dx = (double)length/(double)k;
			
			dt = dt*10; 
			
			for(i = 0; i < length; i++)
			{	
			
	 		    temp0[i] =  amp0*exp(-1.0*ex0*((double)i*dt - dist0)*((double)i*dt - dist0)) +
							amp1*exp(-1.0*ex1*((double)i*dt - dist1)*((double)i*dt - dist1)) + 							 
							amp2*exp(-1.0*ex2*((double)i*dt - dist2)*((double)i*dt - dist2)) + 
							amp3*exp(-1.0*ex3*((double)i*dt - dist3)*((double)i*dt - dist3))  - shift_down/82 ;
				if(temp0[i]<0){temp0[i]=0;}
				
			
			  Foorce_single[i] = temp0[i];
			}
			

			
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,(double)length/10.0);
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
			
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, temp0,  length/10.0, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);  
			
			
		
			
    /*			
			
				for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
				{
					result[i] = fcnstr[val][okt_num].A2*temp[i] + fcnstr[val][okt_num].A3*temp1[i] + 
						fcnstr[val][okt_num].A4*temp2[i]  + temp_circle[i] + temp_circle_1[i] ;
				
				
					force_current[i] =  result[i];
					Force_vozb[val][okt_num][i] = result[i]; 
				}
		
			
			
			
 			
			integr_0 = 0;
			for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
			{
				integr_0 = integr_0 + temp[i];
				
			}
			
			for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
			{
				
				 
				  temp[i] = gain*temp[i]/integr_0;  
			
				
			}
			

			
			
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,255.0);
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
			
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Force_vozb[val][okt_num],  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);  
			
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, result,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_FAT_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);  
*/						
//						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, temp_circle,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
//						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);    
						
			
//			send_single_molot(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_90, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK make_derrivate (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	int i;
	double temp[256];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&val);
				switch(val)
				{
					case 0:
						
						for( i = 1; i < 255; i++)
						{
							temp[i] = (Force_graph[i + 1]- Force_graph[i - 1])/2.0; 	
							
						}
						temp[0] = 0;
						
						temp[255] = 0;
						
						for( i = 1; i < 255; i++)
						{
							Force_graph[i] = temp[i]; 	
							
						}
						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Force_graph,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
					   
						break;
						
					case 1:
						
						for( i = 1; i < 255; i++)
						{
							temp[i] = (Force_graph_1[i + 1]- Force_graph_1[i - 1])/2.0; 	
							
						}
						temp[0] = 0;
						
						temp[255] = 0;
						
						for( i = 1; i < 255; i++)
						{
							Force_graph_1[i] = temp[i]; 	
							
						}
						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Force_graph_1,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
					   
						break;
						
						
					case 2:
						
						for( i = 1; i < 255; i++)
						{
							temp[i] = (Force_graph_2[i + 1]- Force_graph_2[i - 1])/2.0; 	
							
						}
						temp[0] = 0;
						
						temp[255] = 0;
						
						for( i = 1; i < 255; i++)
						{
							Force_graph_2[i] = temp[i]; 	
							
						}
						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Force_graph_2,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
					   
						break;
						
						
					case 3:
						
						for( i = 1; i < 255; i++)
						{
							temp[i] = (Force_graph_3[i + 1]- Force_graph_3[i - 1])/2.0; 	
							
						}
						temp[0] = 0;
						
						temp[255] = 0;
						
						for( i = 1; i < 255; i++)
						{
							Force_graph_3[i] = temp[i]; 	
							
						}
						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Force_graph_3,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
					   
						break;
						
						
					case 4:
						
						for( i = 1; i < 255; i++)
						{
							temp[i] = (Force_graph_4[i + 1]- Force_graph_4[i - 1])/2.0; 	
							
						}
						temp[0] = 0;
						
						temp[255] = 0;
						
						for( i = 1; i < 255; i++)
						{
							Force_graph_4[i] = temp[i]; 	
							
						}
						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Force_graph_4,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
					   
						break;
						
					
				}
			
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK scale_vol_out (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	double scale;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_RING_6,&val);
			switch(val)
			{
				case 0:
					scale =  0.001;
					
				break;
				case 1:
					scale =  0.01;
					
				break;
				case 2:
					scale =  0.1;
					
				break;
				case 3:
					scale =  1.0;
					
				break;
				case 4:
					scale =  10.0;
					
				break;
				case 5:
					scale =  100.0;
					
				break;
				case 6:
					scale =  1000;
					
				break;
				case 7:
					scale =  10000;
					
				break;
				case 8:
					scale =  100000;
					
				break;
				case 9:
					scale =  5000000;
					
				break;
				
				
				
				
				
			}
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,ATTR_MAX_VALUE, scale);
			
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}



int CVICALLBACK sw_str_svertka (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
//switch generator/pianoid
{
	int val;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
	int err;
	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_RING_SEL_OUT, &val);
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_notes_off; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
						   
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x1;
		
			sendBuff[7]	 = 0;
		
			sendBuff[8] = 0x23;
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 9); 
	
      //  	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 9);	

			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

	int natural_harm_index[128];

	switch (event)
	{

int CVICALLBACK save_all (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			 
			
			save_params(panelHandle, PANEL_SAVE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			save_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_48,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			save_all_ci(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_26,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			save_Ci_out(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_12,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			save_Omega(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_19,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			save_Q_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_37,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			ArrayToFile("Q_coeff.txt", Q_coeff, VAL_DOUBLE,  harm_num, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
			ArrayToFile("Mass.txt", Mass, VAL_DOUBLE,  harm_num, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
			ArrayToFile("NL_disp.txt", NL_disp , VAL_DOUBLE, NumString, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
 			ArrayToFile("ttn_tails.txt", Quan_NL , VAL_DOUBLE, NumString, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
			ArrayToFile("NL.txt", NL , VAL_DOUBLE, 128, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
			
 			ArrayToFile("impulse_resp_L.txt", impulse_resp_Left , VAL_DOUBLE, Fir_length*3, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
 			ArrayToFile("impulse_resp_R.txt", impulse_resp_Right , VAL_DOUBLE, Fir_length*3, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
			
 			ArrayToFile("velocity.txt", velocity , VAL_INTEGER, 128, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 5, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
				ArrayToFile("pedal_out.txt", pedal_out , VAL_INTEGER, 128, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 5, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
			ArrayToFile("decka_coeff.txt", decka_coeff, VAL_DOUBLE, 16*harm_num, 16, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE ); 
			
			ArrayToFile("Shapee.txt", shape_256, VAL_DOUBLE, 88*256, 88, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 5, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE ); 
			
		  	ArrayToFile("Shape_512.txt", shape_512, VAL_DOUBLE, 88*512, 88, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 5, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE ); 
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Button_Amp_nl (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	
char *char_val;
double temp_val;
	
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("Amp_NL");
			param_num = 16;
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_4,Amp_NL[nota]); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_4,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, Amp_NL,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
		
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_4,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
	
		
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			 
			temp_val = Amp_NL[nota];
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				Amp_NL[nota] = Amp_NL[nota] + eventData2*step[18];
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				Amp_NL[nota] = Amp_NL[nota] - eventData2*step[18];  		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_4,Amp_NL[nota]);
		
			mode_addr_shift =  9;
			Send_param_pack4(nota,Amp_NL[nota],Amp_NL[nota],Amp_NL[nota]);
			
			
		 }
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, Amp_NL,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[18] = step[18]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[18] = step[18]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_4,step[18]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_4,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_4,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
	
}

int CVICALLBACK Button_Quant_NL (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
char *char_val;
double temp_val;
	
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("Amp_NL");
			param_num = 17;
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_3,Quan_NL[nota]); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_3,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, Quan_NL,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
		
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_3,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
	
		
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			 
			temp_val = Quan_NL[nota];
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				Quan_NL[nota] = Quan_NL[nota] + eventData2*step[22];
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				Quan_NL[nota] = Quan_NL[nota] - eventData2*step[22];  
				if(  Quan_NL[nota] < 0){Quan_NL[nota] = 0;}
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_3,Quan_NL[nota]);
		
			mode_addr_shift =  11;
			Send_param_pack4(nota,Quan_NL[nota],Quan_NL[nota],Quan_NL[nota]);
			
			
		 }
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, Quan_NL,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[22] = step[22]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[22] = step[22]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_3,step[22]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_3,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_3,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK send_nl (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0};    
	int j,k,res;
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
						sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
						sendBuff[3]	 = CMD_recieve_ttn; 
						sendBuff[4]	 = 5;
	
						k = 0;
						
							for(j = 0; j < 88; j ++)
							{
								
							
								res = ttn[j]; 
								
							//	res = (int)val;
			
									lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
									mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 
									
									
								sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
								sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
								sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
								sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
								k+=4;
							}
							
		
						Length232 = k;
						sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
						sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
						sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
				//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
						UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 
						Delay(0.003);
						//////////////   dt  ////////////////////////////////////////////
						
						sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
						sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
						sendBuff[3]	 = CMD_recieve_ttn; 
						sendBuff[4]	 = 6;
	
						k = 0;
						
							for(j = 0; j < 88; j ++)
							{
								
							
								res = dt[j]; 
								
							//	res = (int)val;
			
									lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
									mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
									hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 
									
									
								sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
								sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
								sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
								sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
								k+=4;
							}
							
		
						Length232 = k;
						sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
						sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
						sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
				//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
						UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 
						Delay(0.003); 

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Button_NL_DISP (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	
char *char_val;
double temp_val;
double view_val;
double decim = 2097000;
	
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
			char_val = StrDup ("NL_DISP");
			param_num = 18;
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,87.0);
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NL_DISP,NL_disp[nota]); 
		//	view_val = NL_disp[nota]/decim;
			
		//	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SHTEG_3,view_val);
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_NL_DISP,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, NL_disp,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
		
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_NL_DISP,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
	
		
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			 
			temp_val = NL_disp[nota];
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				NL_disp[nota] = NL_disp[nota] + eventData2*step[20];
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				NL_disp[nota] = NL_disp[nota] - eventData2*step[20];
				
				if(NL_disp[nota]  < 0 ){NL_disp[nota]  = 0;}
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NL_DISP,NL_disp[nota]);
			gen_test_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_91, EVENT_COMMIT, 0, 0, 0);
			Draw_Shape(); 
			send_shape (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_22, EVENT_COMMIT, 0, 0, 0);
			
		 }
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, NL_disp,  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[20] = step[20]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[20] = step[20]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NL_DISP,step[20]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_NL_DISP,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_NL_DISP,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
	
}




int CVICALLBACK zero_ci_str (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,nn,ni;
	int numstr;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			for(j = 0; j < 88; j++)
			{
			
			
							nn = (int)(j/2);
							ni = j - nn*2;
							
							
				for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
								
				{
						Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] = 0; 
					//	plotbuff[i] = 	Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256];  
				}				
						
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Set_ci_str_num (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	int nn,ni;
	double plotbuff[128];
	
	int i;
	double amp;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERIC_2,&val);
		//	GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERIC_3,&pow); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE,&amp); 
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				
				nn = (int)(i/2);
				ni = i - nn*2;
				
				Ci_coeff_str[nn][val + ni*256] = amp*Ci_str_curve[i]*Ci_coeff_cos[nn][val + ni*256]; 
				plotbuff[i] = 	Ci_coeff_str[nn][val + ni*256];  
				
				
			}
			
			
			
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);    
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_curva_formula (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double power;
	int i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERIC_3,&power);
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
			{
				
			Ci_str_curve[i] = pow(1.0 +(double)i,power);	
			}
			
				
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, Ci_str_curve,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED); 
			

		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK constr_ci_str (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j;
	int nn,ni,num,mod;
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
										 
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERIC_100,&num); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_2,&val);
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_RING_8,&mod); 
			
			
			for(mod = 37; mod < 92; mod++)
			{
			
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
					nn = (int)(i/2);
					ni = i - nn*2;
					Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mod + ni*256] = 0.01*omega_coef[0]*omega_coef[0]/(omega_coef[i]*omega_coef[i]);
					
				}
				
			}
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK mute (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,nn,ni;
	int val;
	int mute;
	double dd;
	double plotbuff[128];
	
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_33,&mute);
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERIC_36,&val); 
		
		
		if(mute == 1)
		{
		
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
					for(j = 0; j < 128;j++)
					{
				
						nn = (int)(i/2);
						ni = i - nn*2;
					
						if(val ==j)
						{
						Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256] = 0;
						}
					}
				
				}
		}
		else
			
		{
		
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
					for(j = 0; j < 128;j++)
					{
				
						nn = (int)(i/2);
						ni = i - nn*2;
					
						Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256] =  Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][j + ni*256];
						
					}
				
				}
		}
			
					
			nn = (int)(nota/2);
			ni = nota - nn*2;
					
					
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
					
					plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][val + ni*256]; 
				
				}
			
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, plotbuff,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_MAGENTA); 
						
			send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Read_tmp_pi (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
	int err;
	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_13,&val);
		
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_read; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = val; 
						   
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x1;
		
			sendBuff[7]	 = 1;
		
			sendBuff[8] = 0x23;
	
      //  	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 9);	
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 9);  
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK copy_str_param (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int numstr; 
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			
			copied_str_params[0] = ttn[numstr];
			copied_str_params[1] = dt[numstr]; 
			copied_str_params[2] = decr_op[numstr];
			copied_str_params[3] = decr_cl[numstr];
			
			copied_str_params[4] = disp[numstr]; 
			copied_str_params[5] = decr_disp[numstr];
			copied_str_params[6] = shteg[numstr];
			copied_str_params[7] = damping[numstr]; 
			copied_str_params[8] = NL_disp[numstr];
			copied_str_params[9] = width[numstr];
			copied_str_params[10] = del[numstr];
			copied_str_params[11] = FB[numstr];  
				
				copied_str_params[12] = ind_mult_0[numstr];
				copied_str_params[13] = ind_mult_1[numstr];
				copied_str_params[14] = ind_mult_2[numstr];
				copied_str_params[15] = ind_mult_3[numstr];
				copied_str_params[16] = ind_mult_4[numstr];
				
				copied_str_params[17] = ind_vol_0[numstr];
				copied_str_params[18] = ind_vol_1[numstr]; 
				copied_str_params[19] = ind_vol_2[numstr]; 
				copied_str_params[20] = ind_vol_3[numstr]; 
				copied_str_params[21] = ind_vol_4[numstr]; 
				
												 		 
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK paste_str_param (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int numstr;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			
			
			
			ttn[numstr] = copied_str_params[0]; 
			dt[numstr] = copied_str_params[1]; 
			decr_op[numstr] = copied_str_params[2];
			 decr_cl[numstr] = copied_str_params[3];
			
			 disp[numstr] = copied_str_params[4]; 
			 decr_disp[numstr] = copied_str_params[5];
			 shteg[numstr] = copied_str_params[6];
			 damping[numstr] = copied_str_params[7]; 
			 NL_disp[numstr] = copied_str_params[8];
			 width[numstr] = copied_str_params[9];
			 del[numstr] = copied_str_params[10];
			 FB[numstr] = copied_str_params[11];  
				
				ind_mult_0[numstr] = copied_str_params[12];
				ind_mult_1[numstr] = copied_str_params[13];
				ind_mult_2[numstr] = copied_str_params[14];
				ind_mult_3[numstr] = copied_str_params[15];
				ind_mult_4[numstr] = copied_str_params[16];
				
				 ind_vol_0[numstr] = copied_str_params[17];
				 ind_vol_1[numstr] = copied_str_params[18]; 
				 ind_vol_2[numstr] = copied_str_params[19]; 
				 ind_vol_3[numstr] = copied_str_params[20]; 
				 ind_vol_4[numstr] = copied_str_params[21]; 
				 
				 
				 
				 
				
				
				mode_addr_shift = 0;
				Send_param_pack4(numstr,ttn[numstr],ttn[numstr],ttn[numstr]);
				
				   if(numstr >= start_dupple_note)
				   {
					mode_addr_shift =  1;
					Send_param_pack4(numstr,ttn[numstr] + dt[numstr],ttn[numstr] + dt[numstr],ttn[numstr] + dt[numstr]);
				
						if(nota >= start_tripple_note)
						{
						
							mode_addr_shift =  5;
							Send_param_pack4(numstr,(ttn[numstr] - dt[numstr]),(ttn[numstr] - dt[numstr]),(ttn[numstr] - dt[numstr]));
						
						}
				
				   }
				mode_addr_shift =  2;
				Send_param_pack4(numstr,decr_op[numstr],decr_op[numstr],decr_op[numstr]);
				
				mode_addr_shift =  3;
				Send_param_pack4(numstr,decr_cl[numstr],decr_cl[numstr],decr_cl[numstr]);
				
				mode_addr_shift =  6;
				Send_param_pack4(numstr,disp[numstr],disp[numstr],disp[numstr]);
				
				mode_addr_shift =  7;
				Send_param_pack4(numstr,decr_disp[numstr],decr_disp[numstr],decr_disp[numstr]);	
				
				mode_addr_shift =  4;
				Send_param_pack4(numstr,shteg[numstr],shteg[numstr],shteg[numstr]);
				
				mode_addr_shift =  8;
			
				Send_param_pack4(numstr,damping[numstr],damping[numstr],damping[numstr]);
				
				send_udar_param(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_53,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				 
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK paste_mult (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	int val;
	double delta;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&val); 
			switch(val)
			{
				case 4:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{ 	
						ind_mult_4[i] = copied_ind_mult[i];
				
					}
			
				break;
				case 3:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++) 
					{ 	
						ind_mult_3[i] = copied_ind_mult[i];
				
					}
			
				break;	
				case 2:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++) 
					{ 	
						ind_mult_2[i] = copied_ind_mult[i];
				
					}
			
				break;
				case 1:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++) 
					{ 	
						ind_mult_1[i] = copied_ind_mult[i];
				
					}
			
				break;	
				
				case 0:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++) 
					{ 	
						ind_mult_0[i] = copied_ind_mult[i];
				
					}
			
				break;	
				
				
				
				
			}
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK paste_vol (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	int val;
	double delta;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&val); 
			switch(val)
			{
				case 4:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{ 	
						ind_vol_4[i] = copied_vol[i];
				
					}
			
				break;
				case 3:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++) 
					{ 	
						ind_vol_3[i] = copied_vol[i];
				
					}
			
				break;	
				case 2:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++) 
					{ 	
						ind_vol_2[i] = copied_vol[i];
				
					}
			
				break;
				case 1:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++) 
					{ 	
						ind_vol_1[i] = copied_vol[i];
				
					}
			
				break;	
				
				case 0:
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++) 
					{ 	
						ind_vol_0[i] = copied_vol[i];
				
					}
			
				break;	
				
				
				
				
			}
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_ci_scale (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	double min, max;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RING_9, &val);
		switch (val)
		{
			case 0:
				min = -20.0000001;
				max = 20.0000001;
			break;
			case 1:
				min = -0.000001;
				max = 0.000001;
			break;
			case 2:
				min = -0.00001;
				max = 0.00001;
			break;
			case 3:
				min = -0.0001;
				max = 0.0001;
			break;
			case 4:
				min = -0.001;
				max = 0.001;
			break;
			case 5:
				min = -0.01;
				max = 0.01;
			break;
			case 6:
				min = -0.1;
				max = 0.1;
			break;
			
			
			
			
			
		}
			
		SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_95, min);  
		SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_94, max);  
			
	 	SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,min,max); 
		
		SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_4, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,min,max); 
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK doctor_ci (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,nn,ni,nnn;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
					for(nnn = 0; nnn < 87;nnn++)
						
					{
						
						
						nn = (int)(nnn/2);
						ni = nnn - nn*2;
						
						for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  if((Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256]*Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256]) > 0)
						  {
							Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] = -1.0*Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256];	  
						  }
						   
				
						}
					}
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK set_mode_num_display (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,nn,ni;
	int val,val1,val2;
	double plotbuff[128];
	double mult;
	
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERIC_36,&val);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_BINARYSWITCH,&val2);
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERIC_99,omega_coef[val]);
			
			
			
		if(val2 == 1)
		{
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
					nn = (int)(i/2);
					ni = i - nn*2;
					plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][val + ni*256]; 
					
				}
			
			
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						
		}
		else
		{
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
					nn = (int)(i/2);
					ni = i - nn*2;
					plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][val + ni*256]; 
					
				}
			
			
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED); 
			
			
		}
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK displ_modes_amps (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,nn,ni;
	int val,mod;
	double plotbuff[harm_num],plotbuff_1[harm_num];

	double mult;
	double max,min;
	int imax,imin;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERIC_36,&val);
			
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale);
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_4,ATTR_XAXIS_OFFSET,(double)offs_scale); 
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2,ATTR_XAXIS_OFFSET,(double)offs_scale); 
		
			for(mod = 0; mod < 256; mod++)
			{
			
			
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{
				
						nn = (int)(i/2);
						ni = i - nn*2;
						plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mod + ni*256];
						plotbuff_1[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][mod + ni*256]; 
						

					}
				
				MaxMin1D (plotbuff, 88,&max, &imax, &min, &imin);
				
				if( max < -1.0*min)
				{
					modes_amps[mod] = -1.0*min;	
				}
				else
				{
					modes_amps[mod] = max;	
				}
				
				MaxMin1D (plotbuff_1, 88,&max, &imax, &min, &imin);
				
				if( max < -1.0*min)
				{
					modes_amps_str[mod] = -1.0*min;	
				}
				else
				{
					modes_amps_str[mod] = max;	
				}
				
			}
			
			
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = modes_amps_str[i];
						  
						  plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = modes_amps[i];
						  
						}
						
						

						
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  256 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW); 
			
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_1,  256 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW); 
			
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
						{   
							if(mute_amps[i]>0){plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = 0;}
							else {plotbuff_1[i - offs_scale] = modes_amps[i];} 
						  
						}
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, plotbuff_1,  256 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GRAY);
								

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_velocity (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val,i;
	int p_level,mf_level,f_level;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
		//	 p_level = 30;
			 
		//	 mf_level = 90;
			 
		//	 f_level = 110;
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_20,&val);
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_19,&p_level); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_135,&mf_level);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_136,&f_level);
			
			flag_velocity = val;
			
 /*
			for(i = 1; i < 128; i++)
			{
				if(i < p_level)
				{
					velocity[i] = 1;
				}
					else
					{
						if(i <= mf_level)
						{
							velocity[i] = 32 + (i - p_level)*32/(mf_level - p_level);
							
						}
						else
						{
							if(i <= f_level)
							{
								velocity[i] = 64 + (i - mf_level)*32/(f_level - mf_level);
							}
							else
							{ 
									velocity[i] = 96 + (i - f_level)*32/(127 - f_level);}	
							  		if( velocity[i] > 127){velocity[i] = 127;}
							}
						}
			}


*/
			if(val == 1)
			{
				
		
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0,127);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0,127);
				
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, velocity,  128, VAL_INTEGER,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
			}
			
			
			param_num = 21;

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK show_hide_picture (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_35,&val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_TAB, ATTR_VISIBLE,val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, ATTR_VISIBLE,val);
			//	SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_PICTURE, ATTR_PLOT_AREA_WIDTH,1024);

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Get_From_Field (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double x,y;
	int xCoordinate;
	int yCoordinate; 
	int leftButtonDown; 
	int rightButtonDown;
	int keyModifiers;
	int voice_n,param_n;
	int a,b,tt,RGB;
	int Xi_c,Yi_c,xc,yc,i,j,k;
	int par;
	int count;
	
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	
	
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_KEYPRESS:
			
			
		SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC,eventData1);
		switch(eventData1)
		{
			case 122: // z, voice0
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,0);
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
			
			case 120: // x, voice1
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,1);
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
			
			case 99: // c, voice2
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,2);
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
			
			case 118: // v, voice3
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,3);
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
			
			case 98: // b, voice4
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,4);
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
			
			case 1536: // arrow up, param
				
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_FLAG,&par);
			par = par + 1;
			if(par > 5) {par = 0;}
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_FLAG,par);
			
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
			
			
			case 1792: // arrow down, param 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_FLAG,&par);  
			par = par - 1;
			if(par < 0) {par = 5;}
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_FLAG,par);
			
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
			
			case 47: 
				FILL_selected (tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_COMMANDBUTTON, EVENT_COMMIT, 0,0,0);
			break; 
			
			case 113: //Q
				GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_0,&a);
				a = 1 - a;
				SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_0,a);
			
				mute_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_0,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			break;
			
			case 119: //W
				GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_1,&a);
				a = 1 - a;
				SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_1,a);
			
				mute_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_0,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
			
			case 101: //E
				GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_2,&a);
				a = 1 - a;
				SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_2,a);
				
				mute_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_0,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
			
			case 114: //R
				GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_3,&a);
				a = 1 - a;
				SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_3,a);
			
				mute_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_0,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
			
			case 116: //T
				GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_4,&a);
				a = 1 - a;
				SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_4,a);
			
				mute_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_0,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
			
			
			
			
			case 32: // space, copy notes from keyboard
				
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DXI,&xc);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DYI,&yc);
			
 			count = 0;
			for(j = 0; j < Max_voice_num; j++)
			{
				
				if(flag_voice_editable[j] > 0 )
				{
					
					for(i = count; i < 88; i++)
					{
						if(flag_nota_on[i] > 0 )
						{
							   voice_nota[j][xc][yc] = i;
							   voice_vol[j][xc][yc] = flag_nota_on[i];
							
							   count = i +1;
							   break;
						}
						
					
					
					}
				}
				
				
				
			}
			
			 
			update_tab();
			code_surface(); 
			send_sell(xc,yc);
			
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
			
			
			break;
			
			case 262211:    //ctrl c
				
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DXI,&xc);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DYI,&yc);
				
				
			for(j = 0; j < Max_voice_num; j++)
			{
				
			copied_cell[j] = surface_int[j][xc][yc];	
			}

				
			break;
			
			case 262230:    //ctrl v
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DXI,&xc);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DYI,&yc);
				
				
			for(j = 0; j < Max_voice_num; j++)
			{
				
			surface_int[j][xc][yc] = copied_cell[j];	
			}
			
			decode_surface();
				
				
			

			update_tab();
			code_surface(); 
			send_sell(xc,yc);
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
				
			break;
			 
			case 256: //del
				
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DXI,&xc);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DYI,&yc);
				
				
			for(j = 0; j < Max_voice_num; j++)
			{
				
			surface_int[j][xc][yc] = 0;	
			}
			
			decode_surface();
				
				
			

			update_tab();
			code_surface(); 
			send_sell(xc,yc);
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
				
				
			break;
			
			
			
			
		}
		
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			
			if(Play_Edit_Mode == 1)
			{
			
				GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DXI,&Xi_curr);
				GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DYI,&Yi_curr);
			
				a =Yi_curr*32; 
				b =Xi_curr*32;
			
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_COLOR,VAL_DK_GRAY); 
				CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(a,b,31,31), VAL_DRAW_FRAME);
			
			
			
			
				GetRelativeMouseState (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, &xCoordinate, &yCoordinate, &leftButtonDown, &rightButtonDown, &keyModifiers);
			
				Xi_curr = xCoordinate >> 5;
				Yi_curr = yCoordinate >> 5; 
			
				SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI,Xi_curr);
				SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI,Yi_curr);
			
				SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DXI,Xi_curr);
				SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DYI,Yi_curr);
			
				a =Yi_curr*32; 
				b =Xi_curr*32;
			
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_COLOR,VAL_YELLOW); 
				CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(a,b,31,31), VAL_DRAW_FRAME);
			
			
			
				update_tab();
				code_sell();
			}	
			
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			
			
			if(Play_Edit_Mode == 1)
			{
		
		//	GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI,&Xi_c);
		//	GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI,&Yi_c);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,&voice_n);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_FLAG,&param_n);
			
			GetRelativeMouseState (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, &xCoordinate, &yCoordinate, &leftButtonDown, &rightButtonDown, &keyModifiers);
			
			
			Xi_c = xCoordinate >> 5;
			Yi_c = yCoordinate >> 5; 
			
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI_END,Xi_c);
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI_END,Yi_c);
			
			
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DXI,Xi_c);
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DYI,Yi_c);
			
			
			
		
			
			switch(param_n)
			{
				case 2:   //gliss
					
				voice_gliss[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = 1 - voice_gliss[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
					code_sell();
			
					tt = voice_gliss[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
					a = Xi_c*32;
					b = Yi_c*32;
					RGB = MakeColor (tt*127, tt*127, 0);    
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
					CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(b+1,a+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
				
					
				break;
				
				case 3:   //noise
					
				voice_noise[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = 1 - voice_noise[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
				code_sell(); 
					tt = voice_noise[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
					a = Xi_c*32;
					b = Yi_c*32;
					RGB = MakeColor (tt*127, 0, tt*127);    
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
					CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(b+1,a+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
				
					
				break;
				
				case 4:   //single
					
				voice_single[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = 1 - voice_single[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];	
				code_sell(); 
					tt = voice_single[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
					a = Xi_c*32;
					b = Yi_c*32;
					RGB = MakeColor (0, 0, tt*127);    
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
					CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(b+1,a+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
				
					
				break;
				
				case 5:   //reset_tn
					
				voice_reset_tn[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = 1 - voice_reset_tn[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
				code_sell(); 
					tt = voice_reset_tn[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
					a = Xi_c*32;
					b = Yi_c*32;
					RGB = MakeColor (0, tt*127, tt*127);    
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
					CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(b+1,a+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
					
				break;
				
			}
			
			code_surface();
		
			send_sell(Xi_c,Yi_c);
			update_tab();
	//		show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			}
		break;
		
		
		case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
			
			
			if(Play_Edit_Mode == 1)
			{
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,&voice_n);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_FLAG,&param_n);
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI,&Xi_c);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI,&Yi_c);
				
				
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				if(param_n == 0)  // nota 
				{
					voice_nota[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = voice_nota[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] +	1*eventData2;
					if(voice_nota[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] > 87){voice_nota[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = 87;}
					
//					code_surface();
					
					code_cell(Xi_c,Yi_c); 
			
					tt = voice_nota[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
					a = Xi_c*32;
					b = Yi_c*32;
					if((tt > Pianoid_low_nota - 1)||(tt < Pianoid_hi_nota +1))
						{
						RGB = MakeColor (tt, 127 - tt, 127); 
						}
					else
						{
						RGB = MakeColor (0, 0, 0); 
						}
						
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
					CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(b+1,a+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
					
					
				}
				
				
				if(param_n == 1) // vol
				{
					voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] +	1*eventData2;
					if(voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] > 127){voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = 127;}
			//		code_surface();
					code_cell(Xi_c,Yi_c); 
					tt = voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
					a = Xi_c*32;
					b = Yi_c*32;
					if(tt > 0)
						{
						RGB = MakeColor (0, 127 - tt, tt);  
						}
					else
						{
						RGB = MakeColor (0, 0, 0); 
						}
					
					  
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
					CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(b+1,a+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
					
					
				}
				
				
				update_tab();
			}
			
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				if(param_n == 0)  // nota 
				{
					voice_nota[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = voice_nota[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] -	1*eventData2;
					if(voice_nota[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] < 0){voice_nota[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = 0;}
					
				//	code_surface();
					code_cell(Xi_c,Yi_c); 
			
					tt = voice_nota[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
					a = Xi_c*32;
					b = Yi_c*32;
					RGB = MakeColor (tt, 127 - tt, 127);    
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
					CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(b+1,a+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
				}
				
				
				if(param_n == 1) // vol
				{
					voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] -	1*eventData2;
					if(voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] < 0){voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = 0;}
					if(voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] > 127){voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c] = 127;} 
					
			//		code_surface();
					code_cell(Xi_c,Yi_c); 
			
					tt = voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c];
					a = Xi_c*32;
					b = Yi_c*32;
					RGB = MakeColor (0, 127 - tt, tt);    
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
					CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(b+1,a+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
					
					
				}
				
			}
			
					
			send_sell(Xi_c,Yi_c);
			
				sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
				sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
				sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
				sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
				sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
				sendBuff[7]	 = voice_nota[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c]; 
				sendBuff[8]	 = voice_vol[voice_n][Xi_c][Yi_c]; 
			
		
		
				sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
		//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
				UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
				
				Delay(0.005); 
				
			
	//		show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			update_tab();   
			
			}
			
		break;
		
		
		case	EVENT_MOUSE_POINTER_MOVE:
			
		
			
		
		//	GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_PICTURE,1,&x, &y);
			
			GetRelativeMouseState (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, &xCoordinate, &yCoordinate, &leftButtonDown, &rightButtonDown, &keyModifiers);
			
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_X,xCoordinate);
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_Y,yCoordinate);
			
			Xi_curr = xCoordinate >> 5;
			Yi_curr = yCoordinate >> 5; 
			
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI_2,Xi_curr);
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI_2,Yi_curr);
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;			
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_flash_prog_on_off ; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
			
			

			
							lo    = (xCoordinate)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (xCoordinate >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (yCoordinate)      & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (yCoordinate >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 12);
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12); 
		Delay(0.005);
						
	
		
			
			
			
		break;
	}
	return 0; 
	
}


int CVICALLBACK edit_voice (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	int xc,yc,voice_n,param_n;
	
	int a,b,c,d,e;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
	
	
	
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,&voice_n);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_FLAG,&param_n);
	
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DXI,&xc);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DYI,&yc);
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_VOICE_0,&voice_vol[0][xc][yc]);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_VOICE_1,&voice_vol[1][xc][yc]); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_VOICE_2,&voice_vol[2][xc][yc]); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_VOICE_3,&voice_vol[3][xc][yc]); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_VOICE_4,&voice_vol[4][xc][yc]); 
				
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_V0,&voice_nota[0][xc][yc]);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_V1,&voice_nota[1][xc][yc]); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_V2,&voice_nota[2][xc][yc]); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_V3,&voice_nota[3][xc][yc]); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_V4,&voice_nota[4][xc][yc]);
				
				
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_GLISS_0,&a);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_GLISS_1,&b); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_GLISS_2,&c); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_GLISS_3,&d); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_GLISS_4,&e);
			
			voice_gliss[0][xc][yc] = a;
			voice_gliss[1][xc][yc]  = b; 
			voice_gliss[2][xc][yc]  = c; 
			voice_gliss[3][xc][yc]  = d; 
			voice_gliss[4][xc][yc]  = e;
			
				
		
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NOISE_0,&a);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NOISE_1,&b); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NOISE_2,&c); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NOISE_3,&d); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NOISE_4,&e);
			
			voice_noise[0][xc][yc] = a;
			voice_noise[1][xc][yc]  = b; 
			voice_noise[2][xc][yc]  = c; 
			voice_noise[3][xc][yc]  = d; 
			voice_noise[4][xc][yc]  = e;
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_SINGLE_0,&a);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_SINGLE_1,&b); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_SINGLE_2,&c); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_SINGLE_3,&d); 
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_SINGLE_4,&e);
			
			voice_single[0][xc][yc] = a;
			voice_single[1][xc][yc]  = b; 
			voice_single[2][xc][yc]  = c; 
			voice_single[3][xc][yc]  = d; 
			voice_single[4][xc][yc]  = e;
			
			
			
			 code_surface(); 
			 send_sell(xc,yc); 
//			 show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK mute_voice (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
	
	int a0,a1,a2,a3,a4,a5,a6,a7;
	int aa;
	int val,vn;

	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		
			GetCtrlVal (tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_0, &a0);
			GetCtrlVal (tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_1, &a1);
			GetCtrlVal (tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_2, &a2);
			GetCtrlVal (tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_3, &a3);
			GetCtrlVal (tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MUTE_4, &a4);
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TTN_VOICE, &vn); 
			
			a5 = vn	& (1 << 0); 
			a6 = (vn	& (1 << 1)) >>1; 
			a7 = (vn	& (1 << 2)) >>2; 
			
			
			val = a0<<0|a1<<1|a2<<2|a3<<3|a4<<4|a5<<5|a6<<6|a7<<7;
		
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_write_voice; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[7]	 = val & 0x000000FF; 
			sendBuff[8]  = 0x00;
			sendBuff[9]  = 0x23;
		
			
			
//			ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10); 
		Delay(0.005);
			
	
		
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:

			break;
	}

}

		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int a;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_EN_0,&a);
			flag_voice_editable[0] = a;
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_EN_1,&a);
			flag_voice_editable[1] = a;
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_EN_2,&a);
			flag_voice_editable[2] = a;
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_EN_3,&a);
			flag_voice_editable[3] = a;
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_EN_4,&a);
			flag_voice_editable[4] = a;
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK save_surface (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char surface_path_name[MAX_PATHNAME_LEN]; 
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			FileSelectPopup ("",  "*.srf",  "*.srf", "choose surface", VAL_OK_BUTTON ,0, 1, 1, 0, surface_path_name);
			
			
		code_surface();	
			
 			ArrayToFile(surface_path_name, surface_int , VAL_UNSIGNED_INTEGER, Max_voice_num*Max_Cell_x*Max_Cell_y, Max_voice_num ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			

			break;
			
			
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK load_surface (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char surface_path_name[MAX_PATHNAME_LEN];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			FileSelectPopup ("",  "*.srf",  "*.srf", "choose surface", VAL_SELECT_BUTTON ,0, 1, 1, 0, surface_path_name);

			
				FileToArray(surface_path_name, surface_int, VAL_UNSIGNED_INTEGER, Max_voice_num*Max_Cell_x*Max_Cell_y,Max_voice_num, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
				
				decode_surface();
				
				show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				
				show_play_pan(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_3,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
				send_fill(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_2,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK show_play_pan (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int voice_n, param_n;
	int i,j,k;
	int a,b;
	int RGB;
	
	int tt;
	int temp[Max_Cell_x][Max_Cell_y]; 
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
					for(i = 0; i < Max_Cell_x;i++)
					{
						for(j = 0; j < Max_Cell_y; j++)
						{
							
							b = 32*i;
							a = 32*j;
							
							
							for(voice_n =0; voice_n < 5; voice_n++)
							{
								
								if(voice_nota[voice_n][i][j] < 80) 
								{
									if(voice_vol[voice_n][i][j] > 0)
									{
									
										SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_WIDTH,3);
			
										SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_COLOR,VAL_YELLOW); 
										CanvasDrawRect  (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(a,b,31,31), VAL_DRAW_FRAME);
									}
									
									
									if(voice_vol[voice_n][i][j] > 0)
									{
										if( voice_noise[voice_n][i][j] > 0)
										{
									
											SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_COLOR,VAL_CYAN);
											SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_WIDTH,5); 
									//		CanvasDrawPoint (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakePoint(b + 15,a + 15));
											CanvasDrawOval  (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(a+5,b + 5,25,25), VAL_DRAW_FRAME);
									
										}
								
										if( voice_gliss[voice_n][i][j] > 0)
										{
									
				
											SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_COLOR,VAL_MAGENTA);
											SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_WIDTH, 7);
											CanvasDrawPoint (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakePoint(b + 15,a + 15));
									
										}
										
										if( voice_single[voice_n][i][j] == 0)
										{
									
				
											SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_COLOR,VAL_RED);
											SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_WIDTH, 10);
											CanvasDrawPoint (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakePoint(b + 5,a + 5));        
									
										}
										
								
									}
							
								}
							
							
								if( voice_reset_tn[voice_n][i][j] > 0)
								{
									
									SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_WIDTH,5);
			
									SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_COLOR,VAL_RED); 
									CanvasDrawOval  (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(a,b,31,31), VAL_DRAW_FRAME);
									
								}
							
							
							}
						  
						}
					}
					
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_WIDTH,1);
					
					
						  

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK show_voice (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int voice_n, param_n;
	int i,j,k;
	int a,b;
	int RGB;
	
	int tt;
	int temp[Max_Cell_x][Max_Cell_y]; 
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,&voice_n);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_FLAG,&param_n);
			
			
			switch(param_n)
			{
				
				case 0: // nota
							
					for(i = 0; i < Max_Cell_x;i++)
					{
						for(j = 0; j < Max_Cell_y; j++)
						{
						  tt = voice_nota[voice_n][i][j];
						  
						  
							b = i*32;
							a = j*32;
							if(tt > 0)
								{
								RGB = MakeColor (tt, 127 - tt, 127); 
								}
							else
								{
								RGB = MakeColor (0, 0, 0); 
								}
						
							   
				
							SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
				
							CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(a+1,b+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
	
						  
						}
						
						
				
					}
				break;
				
				case 1: // vol
							
					for(i = 0; i < Max_Cell_x;i++)
					{
						for(j = 0; j < Max_Cell_y; j++)
						{
						  
						  tt = voice_vol[voice_n][i][j];
						  
						  
							b = i*32;
							a = j*32;
						if(tt > 0)
						{
							RGB = MakeColor (0, 127 - tt, tt);  
						}
						else
						{
							RGB = MakeColor (0, 0, 0); 
						}
					
				
							SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
				
							CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(a+1,b+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
	
						  
						}
				
					}
				break;
				
				case 2: // glissando
							
					for(i = 0; i < Max_Cell_x;i++)
					{
						for(j = 0; j < Max_Cell_y; j++)
						{
						  tt = voice_gliss[voice_n][i][j];
						  
						  if(voice_gliss[voice_n][i][j] >0){tt = 1;}else{tt = 0;}
							b = i*32;
							a = j*32;
							RGB = MakeColor (tt*127, tt*127, 0);    
				
							SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
				
							CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(a+1,b+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
	
						  
						  
						}
				
					}
				break;	
				
				case 3: // noise
							
					for(i = 0; i < Max_Cell_x;i++)
					{
						for(j = 0; j < Max_Cell_y; j++)
						{
						 
						//  tt = voice_noise[voice_n][i][j];
						  
						  if(voice_noise[voice_n][i][j] > 0){tt = 1;}else{tt = 0;}
							b = i*32;
							a = j*32;
							RGB = MakeColor (tt*127, 0, tt*127);    
				
							SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
				
							CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(a+1,b+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
						  
						}
				
					}
				break;	
				
				case 4: // single
							
					for(i = 0; i < Max_Cell_x;i++)
					{
						for(j = 0; j < Max_Cell_y; j++)
						{
						 
						  tt = voice_single[voice_n][i][j];
						  
						  if(voice_single[voice_n][i][j] >0){tt = 1;}else{tt = 0;}
							b = i*32;
							a = j*32;
							RGB = MakeColor (0, 0, tt*127);    
				
							SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
				
							CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(a+1,b+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
				  
						  
						}
				
					}
				break;	
				
				case 5: // reset_ttn
							
					for(i = 0; i < Max_Cell_x;i++)
					{
						for(j = 0; j < Max_Cell_y; j++)
						{
						  
						  tt = voice_reset_tn[voice_n][i][j];
						  
						  if(voice_reset_tn[voice_n][i][j] >0){tt = 1;}else{tt = 0;}
							b = i*32;
							a = j*32;
							RGB = MakeColor (0, tt*127, tt*127);    
				
							SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_FILL_COLOR,RGB); 
				
							CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(a+1,b+1,30,30), VAL_DRAW_INTERIOR); 
				  
						  
						}
				
					}
				break;	
				
			}
			
			
			
			
			for(i = 0; i < Max_Cell_x;i++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < Max_Cell_y; j++)
				{
					b = i*32;
					a = j*32;
					
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS,ATTR_PEN_COLOR,VAL_DK_GRAY); 
					
				CanvasDrawRect (panelHandle, PANEL_CANVAS, MakeRect(a,b,31,31), VAL_DRAW_FRAME);
	
				}
				
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK send_fill (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,c,vc,k,cmd;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			for(vc = 0; vc < Max_voice_num; vc++)
			{
				switch(vc)
				{
					case 0:
					cmd = cmd0_voice0;
					break;
					case 1:
					cmd = cmd0_voice1;
					break;
					
					case 2:
					cmd = cmd0_voice2;
					break;
					
					case 3:
					cmd = cmd0_voice3;
					break;
					
					case 4:
					cmd = cmd0_voice4;
					break;
				}
				
				for(c = 0; c < Max_Cell_y; c++) 
				{
			
				sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
				sendBuff[3]	 = cmd; 
				sendBuff[4]	 = c;
	
					k = 0;
		
					for(i = 0; i < Max_Cell_x ; i ++) 
					{
				
							lo    = (surface_int[vc][i][c])      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (surface_int[vc][i][c] >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (surface_int[vc][i][c]>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (surface_int[vc][i][c]>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
						sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
						sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
						sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
						k+=4;

					}
				Length232 = k;
				sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
				sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
				sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
	
	//			ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
				UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  
			
				Delay(0.005); 
				}
			}
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK FILL_selected (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int x_strt,y_strt,x_end,y_end,voice_n,param_n;
	int i,j,k,val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI,&x_strt);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI,&y_strt);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI_END,&x_end);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI_END,&y_end);
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,&voice_n);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_FLAG,&param_n);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_PARAMETERS,&val); 
			
			
			
			for(i = x_strt; i < x_end +1; i++)
			{
				for(j = y_strt; j < y_end +1; j++)
				{
					switch(param_n)
					{
						case 0:
						voice_nota[voice_n][i][j] = val;	
							
						break;
						case 1:
						voice_vol[voice_n][i][j] = val;	
							
						break;
						case 2:
						voice_gliss[voice_n][i][j] = val;	
							
						break;
						case 3:
						voice_noise[voice_n][i][j] = val;	
							
						break;
						
						case 4:
						voice_single[voice_n][i][j] = val;	
							
						break;
						case 5:
						voice_reset_tn[voice_n][i][j] = val;	
							
						break;
						
						
						
						
						
						
					}
					
				}
				
			}
			
			
			code_surface();
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}
