int CVICALLBACK send_nl_slave (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j;
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;  
	int res;
	
	double decimat_24 = 16777215.0;
	double decimat_25 = 16777215.0*2.0; 
	double decimat_28 = 268435456.0; 
	double decimat_30 = 1073741824.0; 
	double decimat_32 = 4294967296.0; 
	double decimat_31 = 2147483648.0; 

	float val; 
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_12, &val);     		
			Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)val;
			
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_listen; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
			
		//		res = (int)(val*4096);
			res = Float_Result.int_val; 

			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);     
	//	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 12);
		//	FlushOutQ (comport);
	//		Delay(0.1);		
			
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK modes_extractor (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,nn,ni;
	int mod;
	double plotbuff[128],plotbuff_1[128];
	double mult;
	double max,min;
	int imax,imin;
	
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			for(mod = 0; mod < 256; mod++)
			{
			
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
					nn = (int)(i/2);
					ni = i - nn*2;
					plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mod + ni*256];
					plotbuff_1[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][mod + ni*256];
					
				}
				MaxMin1D (plotbuff, 88,&max, &imax, &min, &imin);
				
				if( max < -1.0*min)
				{
					mult = -1.0/min;	
				}
				else
				{
					mult = 1.0/max;	
				}
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
					modes_extr[mod][i] = mult*plotbuff[i]; 
					
				}
				
				/////////////
				
				MaxMin1D (plotbuff_1, 88,&max, &imax, &min, &imin);
				
				if( max < -1.0*min)
				{
					mult = -1.0/min;	
				}
				else
				{
					mult = 1.0/max;	
				}
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
					modes_extr_str[mod][i] = mult*plotbuff_1[i]; 
					
				}
				//////////
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
					nn = (int)(i/2);
					ni = i - nn*2;
					Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mod + ni*256] = modes_extr[mod][i];
					Ci_coeff_str[nn][mod + ni*256] = modes_extr_str[mod][i]; 
					
				}
				
				
			
			}
			
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, modes_extr[1],  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
			
		
		break;	
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK load_single (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	char name[MAX_PATHNAME_LEN];  
	int val;
	static char *single_file_name;
	static char *string; 
	char letter_n; 
	int err;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RING_4,&val);
			
			DirSelectPopup ("", "Choose dir", 1, 0, name); 
			
			
			 SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_2,name);
			letter_n = name[0] - 97;
				
			
//			SetDrive (letter_n);
			 
			 
			
			 
			 switch (val)
			 {
				 
				case 0: //Omega
					
				err =	SetDir (name); 
				FileToArray("omega_coef.txt", omega_coef, VAL_DOUBLE, 128,1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, omega_coef,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);    
				
					
				break;
				
				case 1: //Q
					
				err =	SetDir (name); 
				FileToArray("Q_coeff.txt", Q_coeff, VAL_DOUBLE, 128,1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Q_coeff,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);    
				
				break; 
				
				case 2: //Ci
					
				err =	SetDir (name); 
				
				FileToArray("Ci_str_out.txt", Ci_str_out, VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Ci_str_out,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);    
				
				break; 		
				
				 
				 
				 
			 }
			 
			 SetDir (dir_name);
			 
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_Spectrum (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double df;
	int val,i,j;
	double grad, tt;
	double temp[3*Fir_length];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
		//	df = 1.0/((double)(4*Fir_length));
			
			param_num = 25;
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_4,&val);
			
			AmpPhaseSpectrum (impulse_resp_Left, 3*Fir_length, 0,1.0/44100.0,amplitudeSpectrum[0], phaseSpectrum[0], &df);

			AmpPhaseSpectrum (impulse_resp_Right, 3*Fir_length, 0,1.0/44100.0,amplitudeSpectrum[1], phaseSpectrum[1], &df);

			for(i = 0; i < val; i++)
			{
				
				temp[i] = fir_curve[i]*amplitudeSpectrum[0][i];	
				
			}
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
				
				PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_MODE,amplitudeSpectrum[0],val, VAL_DOUBLE,
	    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);
				
				PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_MODE,temp,val, VAL_DOUBLE,
	    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
				
				
				
/*				
			grad = Pi()/180.0;
			
			
			for(j = 0; j < 3*Fir_length; j++)
				{
					
					tt = j*2*Pi()/(3*(double)Fir_length);
					temp[j] = 0;
					for( i = 0; i < 3*Fir_length; i++ )
					{
						temp[j] = temp[j] + amplitudeSpectrum[0][i]*cos(tt*i + phaseSpectrum[0][i]);
				
					}
				}
			
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
				
				PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_MODE,temp,3*Fir_length, VAL_DOUBLE,
	    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);
				
			
*/							 

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK adj_fir (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val,i,adj_achx;
	double x,y;                     	
	static int xint; 
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_16,&val);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_103,&event_counter);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SPECTRUM_WIND,&spectrum_window);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_ADJ_ACHX,&adj_achx);
									
			if(val == 1)
			{
				if(adj_achx == 0)
				{
				

					SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0,spectrum_window);
					SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0,spectrum_window);  
				
			
					DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
					PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, fir_curve,  spectrum_window, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
					
					param_num = 300;
					
				}
				else
				{
					SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0,128);
					
				//	SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_XAXIS_OFFSET,-omega_coef[0]);
						
					DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
					PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, fir_curve,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_MAGENTA);
					
					param_num = 301;
				}
			
					
					
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,1);
					
			}						                                         			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK create_fir (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j;
	double tt;
	double temp[3*Fir_length];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			for(j = 0; j < 3*Fir_length; j++)
				{
					
					tt = j*2*Pi()/(3*(double)Fir_length);
					
					impulse_resp_Left[j] = 0;
					impulse_resp_Right[j] = 0; 
					
					for( i = 0; i < 3*Fir_length; i++ )
					{
						
						impulse_resp_Left[j] = impulse_resp_Left[j] + fir_curve[i]*amplitudeSpectrum[0][i]*cos(tt*i + phaseSpectrum[0][i]);
						
						impulse_resp_Right[j] = impulse_resp_Right[j] + fir_curve[i]*amplitudeSpectrum[1][i]*cos(tt*i + phaseSpectrum[1][i]);     
				
					}
				}
			
			
				prepare_FIR();
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

}

int CVICALLBACK set_fir_curve_scale (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
		int val,i;
	double x,y;                     	
	static int xint; 

	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_4,&val);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0,(double)val);
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0,(double)val);
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_103,&event_counter);
			
			
				
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, fir_curve,  val, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
			
			
			
			param_num = 300;
			
					
						SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,ATTR_NUM_CURSORS,event_counter);
					
					
						for(i = 1; i < event_counter ; i++)
						{
						
						
							SetCursorAttribute (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, i, ATTR_CURSOR_POINT_STYLE, VAL_EMPTY_CIRCLE);
							SetCursorAttribute (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, i, ATTR_CROSS_HAIR_STYLE, VAL_NO_CROSS);
	
	
						
							x = (double)(i - 1)*(double)val/(double)(event_counter -1);
							xint = (int)x;
						//	if( xint == 3*Fir_length){break;}
							X[i-1] = x;
							
									y = fir_curve[xint];
									Y[i-1] = y; 
							
							SetGraphCursor(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,i,x,y);
					
						}
						
						X[0]  = 0;
						Y[0]  = fir_curve[0]; 
						
						
						X[event_counter-1]  = (double)val;
						Y[event_counter-1]  = fir_curve[val]; 
					
									                                         			
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK make_seq (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i, j,val,count;
	int voice_n,param_n;
	int gamma_type;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,&voice_n);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_FLAG,&param_n);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING,&gamma_type); 
			
					for(j = 0; j < 80; j++)
					{
						if(flag_nota_on[j] > 0 )
						{
						
							
							switch(gamma_type)
							{ 
								
								case 0: // Maj
										for(i = 0; i < 8; i++)
										{
							
											gamma_seq[i*7]  = j + i*12;
											gamma_seq[i*7 + 1]  = gamma_seq[i*7 + 0] + 2;
											gamma_seq[i*7 + 2]  = gamma_seq[i*7 + 1] + 2;
											gamma_seq[i*7 + 3]  = gamma_seq[i*7 + 2] + 1;
											gamma_seq[i*7 + 4]  = gamma_seq[i*7 + 3] + 2;
											gamma_seq[i*7 + 5]  = gamma_seq[i*7 + 4] + 2;
											gamma_seq[i*7 + 6]  = gamma_seq[i*7 + 5] + 2;
										//	gamma_seq[i*8 + 7]  = gamma_seq[i*8 + 6] + 1;
								
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 0] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 1] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 2] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 3] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 4] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 5] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 6] >79){break;}
									//		if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 7] >79){break;}
							  
										}
										
								break;
								case 1: // Min
										for(i = 0; i < 8; i++)
										{
							
											gamma_seq[i*7]  = j + i*12;
											gamma_seq[i*7 + 1]  = gamma_seq[i*7 + 0] + 2;
											gamma_seq[i*7 + 2]  = gamma_seq[i*7 + 1] + 1;
											gamma_seq[i*7 + 3]  = gamma_seq[i*7 + 2] + 2;
											gamma_seq[i*7 + 4]  = gamma_seq[i*7 + 3] + 1;
											gamma_seq[i*7 + 5]  = gamma_seq[i*7 + 4] + 2;
											gamma_seq[i*7 + 6]  = gamma_seq[i*7 + 5] + 2;
										//	gamma_seq[i*8 + 7]  = gamma_seq[i*8 + 6] + 1;
								
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 0] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 1] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 2] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 3] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 4] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 5] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 6] >79){break;}
									//		if(gamma_seq[i*7 + 7] >79){break;}
							  
										}
										
								break;
								
								case 2: // chroma
										for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
										{
							
											gamma_seq[i*12]  = j + i*12;
											gamma_seq[i*12 + 1]  = gamma_seq[i*12 + 0] + 1;
											gamma_seq[i*12 + 2]  = gamma_seq[i*12 + 1] + 1;
											gamma_seq[i*12 + 3]  = gamma_seq[i*12 + 2] + 1;
											gamma_seq[i*12 + 4]  = gamma_seq[i*12 + 3] + 1;
											gamma_seq[i*12 + 5]  = gamma_seq[i*12 + 4] + 1;
											gamma_seq[i*12 + 6]  = gamma_seq[i*12 + 5] + 1;
											gamma_seq[i*12 + 7]  = gamma_seq[i*12 + 6] + 1;
											gamma_seq[i*12 + 8]  = gamma_seq[i*12 + 7] + 1; 
											gamma_seq[i*12 + 9]  = gamma_seq[i*12 + 8] + 1; 
											gamma_seq[i*12 + 10]  = gamma_seq[i*12 + 9] + 1; 
											gamma_seq[i*12 + 11]  = gamma_seq[i*12 + 10] + 1; 
								
											if(gamma_seq[i*12 + 0] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*12 + 1] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*12 + 2] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*12 + 3] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*12 + 4] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*12 + 5] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*12 + 6] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*12 + 7] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*12 + 8] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*12 + 9] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*12 + 10] >79){break;}
											if(gamma_seq[i*12 + 11] >79){break;}
							  
										}
										
								break;
					
							}
						}
					}
					 

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK fill_x (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int x_strt,y_strt,x_end,y_end,voice_n,param_n;
	int i,j,k,val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI,&x_strt);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI,&y_strt);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI_END,&x_end);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI_END,&y_end);
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,&voice_n);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_PARAMETERS,&val);    
			
			k = 0;
			for(i = x_strt; i < x_end +1; i++)
			{
						voice_nota[voice_n][i][y_strt] = gamma_seq[k];	
							
						voice_vol[voice_n][i][y_strt] = val;	
							
						k++;
			}
			
			code_surface();
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK fill_y (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int x_strt,y_strt,x_end,y_end,voice_n,param_n;
	int i,j,k,val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI,&x_strt);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI,&y_strt);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI_END,&x_end);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI_END,&y_end);
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,&voice_n);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_PARAMETERS,&val); 
			
			k = 0;
			for(j = y_strt; j < y_end +1; j++)
			{
						voice_nota[voice_n][x_strt][j] = gamma_seq[k];	
							
						voice_vol[voice_n][x_strt][j] = val;	
							
						k++;
			}
			
			code_surface();
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK copy_region (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int x_strt,y_strt,x_end,y_end,voice_n,param_n;
	int i,j,k,val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI,&x_strt);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI,&y_strt);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_XI_END,&x_end);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_YI_END,&y_end);
			
			copied_region_w = x_end -  x_strt;
			copied_region_h = y_end -  y_strt; 
			
			for(k = 0; k < Max_voice_num; k++)
			{
			
				for(i = x_strt; i < x_end; i++)
				{
					for(j = y_strt; j < y_end; j++)
					{
						copied_region[k][i - x_strt][j - y_strt] = surface_int[k][i][j];
					
					}
				
				
				
				}
			}
			
			 
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK paste_region (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int x_strt,y_strt,x_end,y_end,voice_n,param_n;
	int i,j,k,val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DXI,&x_strt);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_DYI,&y_strt);
			for(k = 0; k < Max_voice_num; k++)
			{
			
				for(i = 0; i < copied_region_w ; i++)
				{
					for(j = 0; j < copied_region_h; j++)
					{
						surface_int[k][i + x_strt][j + y_strt] = copied_region[k][i][j];
					
					}
				
				
				
				}
			}
			
			
			decode_surface();
			update_tab();
			code_surface(); 
		//	send_sell(xc,yc);
			show_voice(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RING_VOICE,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK invert_seq (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,k;
	int temp[NumString];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			k = 0;
			
			for(i = 0; i < NumString; i++)
			{
				if(gamma_seq[NumString - i - 1] !=0)
				{
				temp[k] = gamma_seq[NumString - i - 1];
				k++;
				}
			}
			for(i = 0; i < k; i++)
			{
				gamma_seq[i] = temp[i];
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

	switch (event)

			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_PEDAL_INPUT,&pedal_input);

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_sdvig (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
	//		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_97,&val);
			send_sdvig_slave(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_98, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			Delay(0.01);
			
	//		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_98,&val);
			send_sdvig_in(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_97, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK trim_fir (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,val;
	double plotbuff[2][3*Fir_length];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_6,&val);   
			
					for( i = val; i < 3*Fir_length; i++ )
					{
						
						impulse_resp_Left[i] =   impulse_resp_Left[i]*exp(-0.001*(double)(i - val));
						impulse_resp_Right[i] = impulse_resp_Right[i]*exp(-0.001*(double)(i - val));      
				
					}
					
					for( i = 0; i < 3*Fir_length - val/2; i++ )
					{
						plotbuff[0][i] = impulse_resp_Left[i + val/2];
						
						plotbuff[1][i] = impulse_resp_Right[i+ val/2];
					}
					
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
				
				                                                                                     						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff[0],  3*Fir_length - val/2, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE); 
						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff[1],  3*Fir_length - val/2, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);  
						
					
				
			
			
				prepare_FIR();
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Play_seq (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0};    
	int i,vol;
	double delay;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,&vol);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_9,&delay); 
			
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
					sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
					sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
					sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
					sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
					sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
					sendBuff[7]	 = i; 
					sendBuff[8]	 = vol; 
			
		
		
					sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
			//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);
					UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
					
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,i);    
					
					Delay(delay);
					
					sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
					sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
					sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
					sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
					sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
					sendBuff[7]	 = i; 
					sendBuff[8]	 = 0; 
			
		
		
					sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
			//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);
					UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
					
					
					
					
					
					
					
			}
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

void CALLBACK waveInProc(HWAVEIN   hwi,UINT      uMsg,DWORD_PTR dwInstance,DWORD_PTR dwParam1,DWORD_PTR dwParam2)
{

double temp_0[44100],temp_1[44100],temp_2[44100];

double frequency;

double thres07;
double maxValue, minValue,rrr,df; 
int i_res_R[44100],i_res_L[44100];
unsigned int tmp0,tmp1,tmp2,tmp3,tmp4,tmp5,tmp6,tmp7;
char sendBuff[20000] = {0}; 
double a,b;
int ccc,eq;
int val;
double dt,freqPeak,powerPeak;
double Auto_Spec[100000]; 
WindowConst windowConstants; 
int elements;
	
int maxIndex ,minIndex;



switch(adj_mode)
{
	case 5:
		if(num_channels == 1)
		{
		 if(freq < stop_freq)     
		 {	
			ccc = buffer_length/4;
			
			

			for(i = 0; i < buffer_length;i+=4)
			{
				tmp0 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +0]& 0x000000ff; 
				tmp1 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +1]& 0x000000ff; 
				tmp2 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +2]& 0x000000ff;
				tmp3 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +3]& 0x000000ff;
		

				i_res_R[i/4] =  (tmp3<<24) + (tmp2<<16) + (tmp1<<8) + tmp0; 

		
			}
		
		
	
			for(i = 0; i < buffer_length/4;i++)
			{
				temp_0[i] = -0.0000001*(double)i_res_R[i];
				
				
	/*		
				if(osc_array_index == 0)
				{
			
					if((temp_0[i] > audio_threshold_level)||(temp_0[i] < -audio_threshold_level))
			//		if(temp_0[i] > audio_threshold_level)
					{
						osc_array_index = i;
						threshold_start = 1;
						audio_buff_counter = 0;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if(threshold_start == 1)
					{
					osc_array[i - osc_array_index + audio_buff_counter*512] = temp_0[i];
					}
				}
		
*/			
			
			}
			
				RMS (temp_0, buffer_length/4, &rms);
					SetCtrlVal(	panelHandle, PANEL_RMS,rms);
					
					
					ach[0][num_ach_freq] = freq;
					ach[1][num_ach_freq] = rms;
					num_ach_freq++;
					
				freq = freq*1.002;
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3,freq);
			
				send_gen_frq(panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
	 
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
		
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, temp_0,  512, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
									VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
						
							PlotLine(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3,0.0,audio_threshold_level,512.0,audio_threshold_level,VAL_RED);
	
//			audio_buff_counter++;
			}
			
			
		
  /*			
			
		if(audio_buff_counter == num_blocks_analys)
		{
		  
				 if(osc_array_index > 0)
				{
				//	 
					DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
		
					PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, osc_array,  512*num_blocks_analys - osc_array_index, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
		
					audio_buff_counter = 0;
				osc_array_index = 0;
			
				;
				
				RMS (osc_array, 512*(num_blocks_analys -1) -osc_array_index, &rms);
					SetCtrlVal(	panelHandle, PANEL_RMS,rms);
					
					 
	
					ach[0][num_ach_freq] = freq;
					ach[1][num_ach_freq] = rms;
					num_ach_freq++;
					
				freq = freq*1.002;
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3,freq);
			
				send_gen_frq(panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			
				
			
				}
				
						
				for(i = 0; i < audio_buff_counter*512;i++)
				{
					osc_array[i] = 0;	
				}
				
						
		}
	
*/
		 
		 else
		 {
			 SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_11,num_ach_freq);
			 
			 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
			 PlotXY(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,ach[0],ach[1],num_ach_freq,VAL_DOUBLE,VAL_DOUBLE,
				 VAL_THIN_LINE,VAL_SOLID_SQUARE,VAL_SOLID,VAL_CONNECTED_POINTS , VAL_YELLOW);
			 
			 ArrayToFile("ach_frq.txt", ach[0], VAL_DOUBLE, num_ach_freq, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 8, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE ); 
			 
			 ArrayToFile("ach_amp.txt", ach[1], VAL_DOUBLE, num_ach_freq, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 8, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE ); 
			 
		 }
		}
		break;
	
	case 4:

 		ccc = buffer_length/(4*num_channels);
		
		switch(num_channels)
		{
			
			case 1:

			for(i = 0; i < buffer_length;i+=4*num_channels)
			{
				tmp0 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +0]& 0x000000ff; 
				tmp1 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +1]& 0x000000ff; 
				tmp2 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +2]& 0x000000ff;
				tmp3 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +3]& 0x000000ff;
		

				i_res_R[i/(4*num_channels)] =  (tmp3<<24) + (tmp2<<16) + (tmp1<<8) + tmp0;
		
			}
		
	
			for(i = 0; i < buffer_length/4;i++)
			{
				temp_0[i] = -0.0000001*(double)i_res_R[i];
		
			
			}
			
				RMS (temp_0, buffer_length/4, &rms);
					SetCtrlVal(	panelHandle, PANEL_RMS,rms);
	
/*	
			for(i = 0; i < buffer_length/4;i++)
			{
			
				osc_array[i + audio_buff_counter*512] = temp_0[i];
			}	
		
	
*/	
		 
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
		
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, temp_0,  buffer_length/4, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
					
/*						
						PlotLine(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3,0.0,audio_threshold_level,512.0,audio_threshold_level,VAL_RED);
	
					audio_buff_counter++;
					if(audio_buff_counter == num_blocks_analys)
					{
						 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
		
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, osc_array,  512*num_blocks_analys, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
						
						audio_buff_counter = 0;
						audio_flag_start = 2; 
					}
					
*/
					break;
					
					
				case 2:
					for(i = 0; i < buffer_length;i+=4*num_channels)
					{
						tmp0 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +0]& 0x000000ff; 
						tmp1 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +1]& 0x000000ff; 
						tmp2 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +2]& 0x000000ff;
						tmp3 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +3]& 0x000000ff;
		

						i_res_R[i/(4*num_channels)] =  (tmp3<<24) + (tmp2<<16) + (tmp1<<8) + tmp0;
		
					
						tmp0 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +4]& 0x000000ff; 
						tmp1 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +5]& 0x000000ff; 
						tmp2 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +6]& 0x000000ff;
						tmp3 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +7]& 0x000000ff;
		

						i_res_L[i/(4*num_channels)] =  (tmp3<<24) + (tmp2<<16) + (tmp1<<8) + tmp0;
		
					}
					
					for(i = 0; i < buffer_length/(4*num_channels);i++)
					{
						temp_0[i] = -1.0*(double)i_res_R[i];
						temp_1[i] = -1.0*(double)i_res_L[i];
		
			
					}
					for(i = 0; i < buffer_length/(4*num_channels);i++)
					{
			
						osc_array[i + audio_buff_counter*buffer_length/(4*num_channels)] = temp_0[i];
						osc_array_1[i + audio_buff_counter*buffer_length/(4*num_channels)] = temp_1[i]; 
					}	
					
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
		
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, temp_0,  512, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
					
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, temp_1,  512, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);
						
						
						audio_buff_counter++;
					if(audio_buff_counter == num_blocks_analys)
					{
						
						MaxMin1D(osc_array, audio_buff_counter*buffer_length/(4*num_channels),
							&maxValue, &maxIndex, &minValue, &minIndex);
						
						if( maxValue < -1.0*minValue)
						{
							maxValue = -1.0*minValue;	
						}

					SetCtrlVal(	panelHandle, PANEL_RMS,maxValue);
					
						MaxMin1D(osc_array_1, audio_buff_counter*buffer_length/(4*num_channels),
							&maxValue, &maxIndex, &minValue, &minIndex);
						
						if( maxValue < -1.0*minValue)
						{
							maxValue = -1.0*minValue;	
						}

					SetCtrlVal(	panelHandle, PANEL_RMS_REF,maxValue);	
					
						for(i = 0; i< audio_buff_counter*buffer_length/(4*num_channels); i++)
						{  
							if((osc_array[i]<=0)&& (osc_array[i+1]>=0))
							{
								for(j = 0; j<  1*buffer_length/(4*num_channels);j++)
								{
									
									temp_0[j]= osc_array[i +j] ;
									temp_1[j]= osc_array_1[i +j] ; 
									
								}
								
								break;
							}
							
							
							
						}
						 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
		
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, temp_0,  buffer_length/(4*num_channels), VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, temp_1,  buffer_length/(4*num_channels), VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);
						
						audio_buff_counter = 0;
						
					}
					
				//		PlotLine(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3,0.0,audio_threshold_level,512.0,audio_threshold_level,VAL_RED);
	
						
					break;
						
		}			
	   
   break;
///////////////////////////////////////////////////   
   	case 0:

		ccc = buffer_length/4;

		for(i = 0; i < buffer_length;i+=4*num_channels)
		{
			tmp0 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +0]& 0x000000ff; 
			tmp1 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +1]& 0x000000ff; 
			tmp2 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +2]& 0x000000ff;
			tmp3 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +3]& 0x000000ff;
		

			i_res_R[i/(4*num_channels)] =  (tmp3<<24) + (tmp2<<16) + (tmp1<<8) + tmp0;
			if(num_channels == 2)
			{
				tmp0 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +4]& 0x000000ff; 
				tmp1 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +5]& 0x000000ff; 
				tmp2 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +6]& 0x000000ff;
				tmp3 =  (int)WaveInput[4*num_channels*i +7]& 0x000000ff;
		

				i_res_L[i/(4*num_channels)] =  (tmp3<<24) + (tmp2<<16) + (tmp1<<8) + tmp0;
				
				
			}

		
		}
		
		
	
		for(i = 0; i < buffer_length/4;i++)
		{
			temp_0[i] = -0.0000001*(double)i_res_R[i];
			if( num_channels == 2)
			{
			temp_1[i] = -0.0000001*(double)i_res_L[i];
			}
			
			if(osc_array_index == 0)
			{
			
				if((temp_0[i] > audio_threshold_level)||(temp_0[i] < -audio_threshold_level))
		//		if(temp_0[i] > audio_threshold_level)
				{
					osc_array_index = i;
					threshold_start = 1;
					audio_buff_counter = 0;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if(threshold_start == 1)
				{
				osc_array[i - osc_array_index + audio_buff_counter*512] = temp_0[i];
					if(num_channels == 2)
					{
						osc_array_1[i - osc_array_index + audio_buff_counter*512] = temp_1[i]; 
					}
				}
			}
		
		}
	 
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
		
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, temp_0,  512, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
						
						if(num_channels == 2)
						{
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, temp_1,  512, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);
							
						}
						
						PlotLine(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3,0.0,audio_threshold_level,512.0,audio_threshold_level,VAL_RED);
	
	audio_buff_counter++;
	
	////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
	if(audio_buff_counter == num_blocks_analys)
	{
		  
			if(osc_array_index > 0)
			{
			//	 
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
		
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, osc_array,  512*num_blocks_analys - osc_array_index, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
				if(num_channels == 2)
				{
				
					PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, osc_array_1,  512*num_blocks_analys - osc_array_index, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);
				}
		
				RMS (osc_array, 512*(num_blocks_analys -1) -osc_array_index, &rms);
				SetCtrlVal(	panelHandle, PANEL_RMS,rms);
				
				dt = 1.0/(double)SampleFrequency;
				
				elements =  512*(num_blocks_analys -1) -osc_array_index;
				
					AutoPowerSpectrum (osc_array,elements , dt, Auto_Spec, &df);
			
					ScaledWindowEx (osc_array, elements,1,0,&windowConstants);
			
					PowerFrequencyEstimate (Auto_Spec, elements/2, -1,windowConstants,df, 7, &freqPeak,&powerPeak);
			
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_99,freqPeak); 
					
					DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
		
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, Auto_Spec,  elements/8, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
					VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
				
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_13,&eq); 
				if(eq == 1)
				{
					GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
					GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_9,&b);
					GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_VOLUME,&val); 
					
					rms_notes[nota] = rms; 
					
					nota++;
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,nota);
					Delay(0.01);
					send_stacatto(nota,val,b); 
					
					
					
				}
				if(nota == 88){break;}
			}
						
					
						
						
						
			for(i = 0; i < audio_buff_counter*512;i++)
			{
				osc_array[i] = 0;	
			}
						
			audio_buff_counter = 0;
			osc_array_index = 0;
			
			
		  
		//audio_flag_start = 1; 
	}
						
						
	   
   break;
}	
		 waveInUnprepareHeader(InputHandle, &WaveHeader, sizeof(WAVEHDR));
	 
		 waveInPrepareHeader(InputHandle, &WaveHeader, sizeof(WAVEHDR));
	
         waveInAddBuffer(InputHandle,&WaveHeader, sizeof(WAVEHDR)); 
		 

}

int CVICALLBACK stop_audio (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			waveInStop(InputHandle);
			
			waveInClose(InputHandle);  
	//		SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3,VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,-32000.0,32000.0);
			
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_START_AUDIO,ATTR_DIMMED,0);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_STOP_AUDIO,ATTR_DIMMED,1); 
		//	SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_42,ATTR_DIMMED,1); 
			
		//		SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_STRIPCHART,ATTR_VISIBLE ,0);     
		   flag_audio = 0;
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK change_force_okt (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	int i,j;
	double dx;
	double ex,d,ex1,d1,ex2,d2;
	double temp[256],temp1[256],temp2[256],temp_circle[256],temp_circle_1[256];
	double result[256];
	double sign,sign1;
	double gain;
	int sign_int,sign1_int;
	double integr_0,integr_1,integr_2;
	int start_i_circle,end_i_circle,start_i_circle_1,end_i_circle_1;
	double ratio,diff,diff1,diff0,A,G,A_1,G_1;
	double x_circle,radius,x_circle_1,radius_1;
	int dd;

	double plotbuff[256];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal( panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_OKT_NUM,&okt_num);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&val);
			
				
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E0,fcnstr[val][okt_num].e0);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E1,fcnstr[val][okt_num].e1); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E2,fcnstr[val][okt_num].e2); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E3,fcnstr[val][okt_num].e3); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E4,fcnstr[val][okt_num].e4); 
			
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X0,fcnstr[val][okt_num].x0);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X1,fcnstr[val][okt_num].x1); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X2,fcnstr[val][okt_num].x2); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X3,fcnstr[val][okt_num].x3); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X4,fcnstr[val][okt_num].x4); 
			
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A0,fcnstr[val][okt_num].A0);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A1,fcnstr[val][okt_num].A1);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A2,fcnstr[val][okt_num].A2);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A3,fcnstr[val][okt_num].A3);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A4,fcnstr[val][okt_num].A4);
/////////////////////////////

			
			
			x_circle  = fcnstr[val][okt_num].x0;
			x_circle_1 = fcnstr[val][okt_num].x1;
			
			radius	   = fcnstr[val][okt_num].e0;
			radius_1   = fcnstr[val][okt_num].e1; 
			
			A		   = fcnstr[val][okt_num].A0; 
			A_1		   = fcnstr[val][okt_num].A1; 
			
			ex			= fcnstr[val][okt_num].e2; 
			ex1			= fcnstr[val][okt_num].e3; 
			ex2			= fcnstr[val][okt_num].e4; 
			
			
			
			d		   = fcnstr[val][okt_num].x2;
			d1		   = fcnstr[val][okt_num].x3;
			d2		   = fcnstr[val][okt_num].x4;
			
			///////circle
			
			for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
			{
				temp_circle[i] =0;
				temp_circle_1[i] =0;
				temp[i] =0;
				temp1[i] =0;
				temp2[i] =0;
			}
			
			start_i_circle = (int)(x_circle -  radius);
			if(start_i_circle < 0){start_i_circle = 0;}
			
			end_i_circle = (int)(x_circle +  radius);
			if(end_i_circle > 255 ){end_i_circle = 255;} 
			
			
			for(i = start_i_circle; i < x_circle; i++)
			{
				
				dx = (double)(i - start_i_circle)/(radius);
				
				if(dx > 1){ dx = 1; }
				
				temp_circle[i + (int)radius] =  A*sqrt((1 - dx*dx));
				temp_circle[i] =  A*sqrt((1 - (1-dx)*(1-dx)));
				
			}
			
			
			
			
			//////////////////////////
			
			
			start_i_circle_1 = (int)(x_circle_1 -  radius_1);
			if(start_i_circle_1 < 0){start_i_circle_1 = 0;}
			
			end_i_circle_1 = (int)(x_circle_1 +  radius_1);
			if(end_i_circle_1 > 255 ){end_i_circle_1 = 255;} 
			
			
			for(i = start_i_circle_1; i < x_circle_1; i++)
			{
				
				dx = (double)(i - start_i_circle_1)/(radius_1);
				
				if(dx > 1){ dx = 1; }
				
				temp_circle_1[i + (int)radius_1] =   A_1*sqrt((1 - dx*dx));
				temp_circle_1[i] =  A_1*sqrt((1 - (1-dx)*(1-dx)));
				
			}
			
			
			dd = (int)ex;
			
			for(i = 0; i < dd; i++)
			{
				
		//		dx = 2*Pi()*i/ex; 
		//		temp[i + (int)d] = 1 - cos(dx);
				
				dx = Pi()*i/ex;
				temp[i + (int)d] = sin(dx);
				
	
			}
			
			
			
			dd = (int)ex1;  
			
			for(i = 0; i < dd; i++)
			{
			 
			//	dx = 2*Pi()*i/ex1; 
			//	temp1[i + (int)d1] = 1 - cos(dx);
				dx = Pi()*i/ex1;
				temp1[i + (int)d1] = sin(dx);
				
	
			
			}
			
			dd = (int)ex2;  
			for(i = 0; i < dd; i++)
			{
				
		//		dx = 2*Pi()*i/ex2; 
		//		temp2[i + (int)d2] = 1 - cos(dx);
				dx = Pi()*i/ex2;
				temp2[i + (int)d2] = sin(dx);
				

				
			}
			
			
			
				for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
				{
					result[i] = fcnstr[val][okt_num].A2*temp[i] + fcnstr[val][okt_num].A3*temp1[i] + 
						fcnstr[val][okt_num].A4*temp2[i]  + temp_circle[i] + temp_circle_1[i] ;
				
				}
		
			
			
			
			
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,255.0);
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
			
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Force_vozb[val][okt_num],  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);  
			
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, result,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_FAT_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);  
						
				

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Save_fcnstr (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,k;
	double temp[6600];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			k = 0;
			
			for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
			{
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
					temp[k] 	= exp_e[0][j][i];
					temp[k + 1] = exp_e[1][j][i]; 
					temp[k + 2] = exp_e[2][j][i]; 
					temp[k + 3] = exp_e[3][j][i]; 
					temp[k + 4] = exp_e[4][j][i];

					temp[k + 8] = exp_d[3][j][i];
					temp[k + 9] = exp_d[4][j][i];
					
					temp[k + 10] = exp_a[0][j][i];
					temp[k + 11] = exp_a[1][j][i];
					temp[k + 12] = exp_a[2][j][i];
					temp[k + 13] = exp_a[3][j][i];
					temp[k + 14] = exp_a[4][j][i];
					
					k = k + 15;
					
				}
				
				
			}
			
				ArrayToFile("exp_all.txt", temp , VAL_DOUBLE, 6600, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
/*

//					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
//					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
			for(k = 0; k < 5; k++)
			{
				for (j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
					for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
					{
						temp[i + j*88 +k*440] = exp_e[k][j][i];
					} 
				}
			}
 			ArrayToFile("exp_e.txt", temp , VAL_DOUBLE, 2200, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
			for(k = 0; k < 5; k++)
			{
				for (j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
					for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
					{
						temp[i + j*88 +k*440] = exp_d[k][j][i];
					} 
				}
			}
 			ArrayToFile("exp_d.txt", temp , VAL_DOUBLE, 2200, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );

			for(k = 0; k < 5; k++)
			{
				for (j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
					for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
					{
						temp[i + j*88 +k*440] = exp_a[k][j][i];
					} 
				}
			}
 			ArrayToFile("exp_a.txt", temp , VAL_DOUBLE, 2200, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
					
					
	*/
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK copy_fcnstr (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E0,&fcnstr_temp.e0);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E1,&fcnstr_temp.e1); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E2,&fcnstr_temp.e2); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E3,&fcnstr_temp.e3); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E4,&fcnstr_temp.e4); 
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X0,&fcnstr_temp.x0);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X1,&fcnstr_temp.x1); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X2,&fcnstr_temp.x2); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X3,&fcnstr_temp.x3); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X4,&fcnstr_temp.x4); 
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A0,&fcnstr_temp.A0);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A1,&fcnstr_temp.A1);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A2,&fcnstr_temp.A2);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A3,&fcnstr_temp.A3);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A4,&fcnstr_temp.A4);
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK paste_fcnstr (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E0,fcnstr_temp.e0);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E1,fcnstr_temp.e1); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E2,fcnstr_temp.e2); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E3,fcnstr_temp.e3); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E4,fcnstr_temp.e4); 
			
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X0,fcnstr_temp.x0);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X1,fcnstr_temp.x1); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X2,fcnstr_temp.x2); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X3,fcnstr_temp.x3); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X4,fcnstr_temp.x4); 
			
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A0,fcnstr_temp.A0);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A1,fcnstr_temp.A1);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A2,fcnstr_temp.A2);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A3,fcnstr_temp.A3);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A4,fcnstr_temp.A4);
			
			Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Save_force_vozb (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,k;
	double temp[10240]; //5*8*256
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			k = 0;
			
			for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
			{
				
				for(i = 0; i < 8; i++)
				{
					for(k = 0; k < 256; k++)
					{
					
						temp[k + j*2048 + i*256] 	= Force_vozb[j][i][k];
					}
					
				}
				
				
			}
			
 			ArrayToFile("Force_vozb.txt", temp , VAL_DOUBLE, 10240, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
			
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Load_force_vozb (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,k;
	double temp[10240];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			 	FileToArray ("Force_vozb.txt", temp, VAL_DOUBLE, 10240, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);

			k = 0;
			
			for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
			{
				
				for(i = 0; i < 8; i++)
				{
					for(k = 0; k < 256; k++)
					{
						
						Force_vozb[j][i][k] = temp[k + j*2048 + i*256];
						
					}
				}
				
				
			}
			
			 
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK prep_force_0_1 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,k;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
				
				for(i = 0; i < 8; i++)
				{
					for(k = 0; k < 256; k++)
					{
						
						Force_vozb[0][i][k] = Force_graph[k];
						Force_vozb[1][i][k] = Force_graph_1[k];
						Force_vozb[1][i][k] = Force_graph_1[k];
						
					}
				}
				
							   

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK import_force_old (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,k;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			FileToArray("Force_graph.txt", Force_graph, VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
			
			FileToArray("Force_graph_1.txt", Force_graph_1, VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
			
			FileToArray("Force_graph_2.txt", Force_graph_2, VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
			
			FileToArray("Force_graph_3.txt", Force_graph_3, VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
			
			FileToArray("Force_graph_4.txt", Force_graph_4, VAL_DOUBLE, harm_num, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
			
				for(i = 0; i < 8; i++)
				{
					for(k = 0; k < 256; k++)
					{
						
						Force_vozb[0][i][k] = Force_graph[k];
						Force_vozb[1][i][k] = Force_graph_1[k];
						Force_vozb[2][i][k] = Force_graph_2[k];
						
					}
				}
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
				
				ind_mult_0[i] =  ind_mult_0[i]*57.032279;
				ind_mult_1[i] =  ind_mult_1[i]*57.032279; 
				ind_mult_2[i] =  ind_mult_2[i]*57.032279; 
			
			    }

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK adj_vol (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0};  
double maxValue, minValue; 
int maxIndex ,minIndex;
	
	int i,vol,val;
	double delay;
	double temp[88],plotbuff[88];
	double temp_array[44100];
	int a;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			flag_adj_vol = 1;
			
		//	audio_buff_counter = 0;
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,&vol);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_9,&delay); 
		//	GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_10,&flag_adj_vol);  
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&val);
			
	//		for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			
			audio_flag_start = 0; 
			
			for(i = 0; i < num_blocks_analys*512; i++)           
			{
				osc_array[i] = 0;	
			}
			
			
			for(nota = 0;nota < 88; nota++)
			{
		//		start_audio(panelHandle, PANEL_START_AUDIO,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				
					sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
					sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
					sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
					sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
					sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
					sendBuff[7]	 = nota; 
					sendBuff[8]	 = vol; 
			
		
		
					sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
			//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);
					UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
					
					
					
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,nota); 
					Delay(delay);
					audio_flag_start = 1; 
					
					
						 
					sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
					sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_midi;
					sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
					sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
					sendBuff[6]	 = 0x02;
		
					sendBuff[7]	 = nota; 
					sendBuff[8]	 = 0; 
			
		
		
					sendBuff[9] = 0x23;
	
			//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);
					UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);
					
					
					while(audio_flag_start !=2){};
					
						audio_flag_start = 0;
				//		stop_audio(panelHandle, PANEL_STOP_AUDIO,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
						
						for(i = 0; i < 512*num_blocks_analys;i++)
						{
							if((osc_array[i] > 10000000.0)||(osc_array[i] < -10000000.0))
							{
							  a = i;
							  break;
							}
							
							a = 0;
						}
						
						for(i = 0; i < 512*num_blocks_analys - a; i++)
						{
						  temp_array[i] = osc_array[i + a];
						}
						RMS (temp_array, 512*(num_blocks_analys -1), &rms);
						
						adj_vol_array[nota] = rms;
						
						SetCtrlVal(	panelHandle, PANEL_RMS,rms);   
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, temp_array,  512*(num_blocks_analys -1), VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
					
				
				
					
			}
			
			
			
	//		MaxMin1D( adj_vol_array, 88, &maxValue, &maxIndex, &minValue, &minIndex); 
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				switch (val)
				{
					case 0:
						
						ind_vol_0[i] = ind_vol_0[i]*(adj_vol_array[46]/adj_vol_array[i]);
						plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_0[i]; 
					break;
					case 1:								   
						
						
						ind_vol_1[i] = ind_vol_1[i]*(adj_vol_array[46]/adj_vol_array[i]);
						plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_1[i]; 
					break;
					
					case 2:
						
						ind_vol_2[i] = ind_vol_2[i]*(adj_vol_array[46]/adj_vol_array[i]);
						plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_2[i]; 
					break;
					
					case 3:
						
						ind_vol_3[i] = ind_vol_3[i]*(adj_vol_array[46]/adj_vol_array[i]);
						plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_3[i]; 
					break;
					
					case 4:
						 
						ind_vol_4[i] = ind_vol_4[i]*(adj_vol_array[46]/adj_vol_array[i]);
						plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_4[i]; 
					
					break;
					
				
				}
			}
/*			
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				switch (val)
				{
					case 0:
						
						plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_0[i];
						temp[i] = equal_loud[i]*ind_vol_0[i]*minValue/adj_vol_array[i]; 
						ind_vol_0[i] = temp[i];
					break;
					case 1:								   
						
						plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_1[i];
						temp[i] = equal_loud[i]*ind_vol_1[i]*minValue/adj_vol_array[i];
						ind_vol_1[i] = temp[i]; 
					break;
					
					case 2:
						
						plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_2[i];
						temp[i] = equal_loud[i]*ind_vol_2[i]*minValue/adj_vol_array[i];
						ind_vol_2[i] = temp[i]; 
					break;
					
					case 3:
						
						plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_3[i];
						temp[i] = equal_loud[i]*ind_vol_3[i]*minValue/adj_vol_array[i];
						ind_vol_3[i] = temp[i]; 
					break;
					
					case 4:
						
						plotbuff[i] = ind_vol_4[i];
						temp[i] = equal_loud[i]*ind_vol_4[i]*minValue/adj_vol_array[i];
						ind_vol_4[i] = temp[i]; 
					
					break;
					
				
				}
			}
			
*/			
			
			
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, temp,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
						
			
			
										   
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Equal_loud_curve (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int ringslide2,i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&ringslide2);   
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
				
			switch(ringslide2)
				 {
			 		case 0:
						
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
							ind_vol_0[i] = ind_vol_0[i]*sqrt(rms_notes[47]/rms_notes[i]);	
						}
						                                                                                          						
				    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, rms_notes,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
					break;
					case 1:
						
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
							ind_vol_1[i] = ind_vol_1[i]*sqrt(rms_notes[47]/rms_notes[i]);	
						}
						                                                                                          						
				    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, rms_notes,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
					break;	
					case 2:
						
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
							ind_vol_2[i] = ind_vol_2[i]*sqrt(rms_notes[47]/rms_notes[i]);	
						}
						                                                                                          						
				    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, rms_notes,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
					break;	
					case 3:
						
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
							ind_vol_3[i] = ind_vol_3[i]*sqrt(rms_notes[47]/rms_notes[i]);	
						}
						                                                                                          						
				    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, rms_notes,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
					break;	
					case 4:
						
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
							ind_vol_4[i] = ind_vol_4[i]*sqrt(rms_notes[47]/rms_notes[i]);	
						}
						                                                                                          						
				    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, rms_notes,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
				
					break;
				 }
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
 		   GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_11,&num_ach_freq);

		//			VAL_CONST_WIDTH, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );

	double shtg[2][44];
	double maxValue, minValue; 
	int maxIndex ,minIndex;	
	int i;
	double correction[44];
	double result[44];
		
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			shtg[0][0] = 1137;
			shtg[1][0] = 92;
			
			shtg[0][1] = 1119;
			shtg[1][1] = 91;

			shtg[0][20] = 381;

			shtg[0][21] = 351;
			shtg[1][21] = 85;

			shtg[0][23] = 279;
			shtg[1][23] = 70;

			shtg[1][24] = 67;

			shtg[0][30] = 167;
			shtg[1][30] = 52;

			shtg[1][31] = 62;
			
			shtg[0][32] = 154;
			shtg[1][32] = 62;
			
			shtg[0][33] = 141;
			shtg[1][33] = 56;
			
			shtg[0][34] = 132;
			shtg[1][34] = 51;
			
			shtg[0][35] = 133;
			shtg[1][35] = 60;
			
			shtg[0][36] = 123;
			shtg[1][36] = 57;
			
			shtg[0][37] = 111;
			shtg[1][37] = 53;
			
			shtg[0][38] = 101;
			shtg[1][38] = 46;
			
			shtg[0][39] = 94;
			shtg[1][39] = 43;
			
			
			
			shtg[0][40] = 86;
			shtg[1][40] = 40;
			
			shtg[0][41] = 93;
			shtg[1][41] = 50;
			
			shtg[0][42] = 87;
			shtg[1][42] = 49;
			
			shtg[0][43] = 79;
			shtg[1][43] = 44;
			
			
			
			MaxMin1D( shtg[0], 44, &maxValue, &maxIndex, &minValue, &minIndex); 
			
			for(i = 0; i < 44; i++)
			{
				correction[i] = 255./shtg[0][i];
				result[i] =  255. - correction[i]*shtg[1][i];
				
			}
			
			
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, shtg[0],  44, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);  
			
			
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, shtg[1],  44, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);  
			
			
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, result,  44, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_FAT_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);  
			
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
			
			PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, result,  44, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);  
			
			
			
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_compress_thres (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result; 
	
	int nsamples;
	
	double decimat,step_up,step_down;
	int i,k,j;

	int res;
	
	double thr,att,rel,rat; 
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			decimat = 1073741824;
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_THRESHOLD, &thr); 
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_ATTACK, &att); 
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_RELEASE, &rel); 
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN, &rat); 
			
			rel = rel*400000;
			att = att*400000;
			
			step_down =   (1 - rat)/rel;

			step_up = (rat - 1)/att;
			
			
			Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)thr; 
			
			res = Float_Result.int_val; 
			
		//	res = (int)thr;
			
			
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_transmit_sel; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  	
		
			Delay(0.001); 
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////			
			res = (int)(decimat*rat); 
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_transmit_sel; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  	
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////		
			Delay(0.001); 		
			
			res = (int)(decimat*step_down);  
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_transmit_sel; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x02; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  	
		
			Delay(0.001); 		
			
			res = (int)(decimat*step_up);  
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_transmit_sel; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x03; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  	
		
			Delay(0.001); 		
			
//	   send_sdvig(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_27, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
	}
	return 0;
	
}

int CVICALLBACK mult_ci_out (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:   
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_137, &val);	
			for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
			{
//				Ci_str_out[i] = Ci_str_out[i]*val;
				Ci_str_1_out[i] = Ci_str_1_out[i]*val;
			}
	//		send_ci_out (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_6,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK sweep (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j,c;
	int start,stop,step;
	double tt[100000];
	
	double strt,stp;
	double rms_Y,rms_Y_prev;
	double temp[2][100000];
	double ttemp[2][10000];

	int res;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STOP_SWEEP, &stp); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_START_SWEEP, &strt); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STEP_SWEEP, &step); 
			
			start = (int)(690*strt);
			stop = (int)(690*stp);
			
			
			
			rms_Y_prev = 0;			
			k = 0;
			for(i = start; i < stop;i+=step)
			{
				
				
					res = i;
			
					sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
					sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
					sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_transmit_sel; 
					sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
					sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
					sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
					sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	
					UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  
					Delay(0.001);
					GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RMS, &rms_Y);
					
					
					if(	rms_Y_prev != rms_Y)
					
						{
			
							temp[0][k] = (double)i/690.0;
							temp[1][k] =  rms_Y;  					   
							k++;
							
							rms_Y_prev = rms_Y;
						}
		
					
				
					}
			
			for(i = 0; i < k; i++)
			{
				tt[2*i] =  temp[0][i];
				tt[2*i +1] =  temp[1][i]; 
				
				
			}
					 
			
			
			SetAxisScalingMode (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0,temp[0][k -1]);

			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
			
						PlotXY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, temp[0],temp[1],k,  VAL_DOUBLE,VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
					
						
			ArrayToFile("Deka_frq_resp.txt", tt , VAL_DOUBLE, 2*k, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
		
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_gen_vol (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;   
	int i,k,j;
	double rat;

	int res;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_2, &rat); 
			
			Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)rat/1000.0; 
			
			res = Float_Result.int_val; 
			
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_transmit_sel; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  	
		
			Delay(0.001); 
				

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_gen_frq (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;   
	int i,k,j;
	double frq,step,ratio;

	int res;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3, &frq); 
//			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_15, &step);  
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TEST, &ratio);
			
		//	Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)rat; 
			
		//	res = Float_Result.int_val; 
			
			res = (int)(frq*690.0*ratio)*0.97;
			
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd_graph_m; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  	
		
			Delay(0.001); 
				

			break;
			
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK: 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3,&frq);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2,&i);
			omega_coef[i] = frq;
			
		break; 	
			
		case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3,&frq);  
		GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_15, &step);   
		GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TEST, &ratio); 
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				frq = frq + (double)eventData2*step;
				
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				frq = frq - (double)eventData2*step;
			
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3,frq);
			
					
					res = (int)(frq*690.0*ratio)*0.97;
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd_graph_m; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  	
			

	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK make_preset_array (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,k,nn, num_of_elements;
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result; 
	int res;
	double temp,aa,ratio;
	double decimat_23 = 65000.0; 
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_72,&v_0); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_71,&v_1); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_70,&v_2); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_69,&v_3);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_107,&v_4); 
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_55,&pp); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_56,&p); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_57,&mf); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_58,&f); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_68,&ff);  
		
			
			num_of_elements = 0;
			for(i = 0; i < 60000; i++)
			{
				preset_array[i] = 0;	
			}
			
			
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				preset_array[i + ttn_offs] = (int)ttn[i];
				num_of_elements++;
			}
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				preset_array[i + dt_offs] = (int)dt[i];
				num_of_elements++;
			}
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				preset_array[i + dec_op_offs] = (int)decr_op[i];
				num_of_elements++;
			}
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				preset_array[i + dec_cl_offs] = (int)decr_cl[i];
				num_of_elements++;
			}
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				preset_array[i + disp_offs] = (int)disp[i];
				num_of_elements++;
			}
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				preset_array[i + decr_disp_offs] = (int)decr_disp[i];
				num_of_elements++;
			}
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				preset_array[i + shteg_offs] = (int)shteg[i];
				num_of_elements++;
			}
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				preset_array[i + damping_offs] = (int)damping[i];
				num_of_elements++;
			}
			
			for(j = 0; j < 88; j++)
			{
				for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
				{
					preset_array[i + shape_offs + j*256] = (int)shape_256[j][i];	
					
					num_of_elements++;
				}
			}
			
			/////////////force_vozb
		;	
			for(k = 0; k< 5; k++)
			{
					switch (k)
					{
						case 0:
							aa = pp*4194304;
						break;
						case 1:
							aa = p*4194304;
						break;
					
						case 2:
							aa = mf*4194304;
						break;
					
						case 3:
							aa = f*4194304;
						break;
					
						case 4:
							aa = ff*4194304;
						break; 
					}
				
					for(j = 0; j < 8; j++)
					{
			
			
						for(i = 0; i < 256; i ++)
						{
							preset_array[i + force_vozb_offs + k*8*256 + j*256]  = (int)(aa*Force_vozb[k][j][i]);
						
							if(i == 0)	{ preset_array[i + force_vozb_offs + k*8*256 + j*256]  = 0;}
							if(i == 255){ preset_array[i + force_vozb_offs + k*8*256 + j*256]  = 0;} 
							num_of_elements++; 
						}
		
				
					}
			}
			
			;
			
		///////////////////////////////// ind_mult
			for(k = 0; k < 5; k++)
			{
			
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
					switch(k)
					{
						case 0:
						preset_array[i + ind_mult_offs + k*88] = (int)(decimat_23*ind_mult_0[i]); 
						break;
						case 1:
						preset_array[i + ind_mult_offs + k*88] = (int)(decimat_23*ind_mult_1[i]);  
						break;
						
						case 2:
						preset_array[i + ind_mult_offs + k*88] = (int)(decimat_23*ind_mult_2[i]);  
						break;
						
						case 3:
						preset_array[i + ind_mult_offs + k*88] = (int)(decimat_23*ind_mult_3[i]);  
						break;
						
						case 4:
						preset_array[i + ind_mult_offs + k*88] = (int)(decimat_23*ind_mult_4[i]);  
						break;
						
							
					}
				   	num_of_elements++; 
				}
			}

		;	
		//////////////////ind_vol
			
			for(k = 0; k < 5; k++)
			{
			
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
					switch(k)
					{
						case 0:
						preset_array[i + ind_vol_offs + k*88] = (int)(v_0*ind_vol_0[i]*8388607.5);   
						break;
						case 1:
						preset_array[i + ind_vol_offs + k*88] = (int)(v_1*ind_vol_1[i]*8388607.5);  
						break;
						
						case 2:
						preset_array[i + ind_vol_offs + k*88] = (int)(v_2*ind_vol_2[i]*8388607.5); 
						break;
						
						case 3:
						preset_array[i + ind_vol_offs + k*88] = (int)(v_3*ind_vol_3[i]*8388607.5);  
						break;
						
						case 4:
						preset_array[i + ind_vol_offs + k*88] = (int)(v_4*ind_vol_4[i]*8388607.5);  
						break;
						
							
					}
				   	num_of_elements++; 
				}
			}
			;

		////////////////////////////////////ci_out
			for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
			{
				Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)Ci_str_out[i];
				preset_array[i + ci_out_offs] = Float_Result.int_val;
				num_of_elements++;  
			}
			
			;
				GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_87, &ratio);    
			
			for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
			{
				
				preset_array[i + omega_offs] = (int)(omega_coef[i]*omega_coef[i]*ratio); 
				num_of_elements++;  
			}
			
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_79, &ratio);    
			for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
			{
				
				preset_array[i + Q_offs] = Q_coeff[i]*ratio;  
				num_of_elements++;  
			}
			;
			///////////////ci_coef_cos
			
			for(nn = 0; nn < 44; nn++)
			{
			//	nn = (int)k/2;
				
				for(i = 0; i < 256 ; i ++) 
				{
					Float_Result.fl_val  = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i];
					preset_array[i + Ci_cos_offs + nn*256] = Float_Result.int_val;
					
					
					num_of_elements++;
				}
			}
			
			;
			for(nn = 0; nn < 44; nn++)
			{
			//	nn = (int)k/2;
				
				for(i = 0; i < 256 ; i ++) 
				{
					Float_Result.fl_val = Ci_coeff_str[nn][i]; 
					preset_array[i + ci_str_offs + nn*256]  = Float_Result.int_val;  
				
					num_of_elements++;
				}
			}
			
			
			ArrayToFile("preset_array.txt", preset_array , VAL_INTEGER, num_of_elements, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 14, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			
		
			 
			
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Generator_on_off (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
	int err;
	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLGEN, &val);
			
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_listen; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[7]	 = val & 0x000000FF; 
			sendBuff[8]  = 0x00;
			sendBuff[9]  = 0x23;
		
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);  	
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

			QQ[3][62] =  977;
			QQ[3][63] =  981;
			QQ[3][64] =  999;
			
			
		//	GetFileSize("40_100.txt ",&fileSize) ;
			
			 
			
			 	FileToArray ("ach_frq.txt", ach[0], VAL_DOUBLE,num_ach_freq, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				FileToArray ("ach_amp.txt",  ach[1], VAL_DOUBLE,num_ach_freq, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				for(i = 0; i < num_ach_freq; i++)
				{
				//	ach[0][i] = temp[2*i];
				//	ach[1][i] = temp[2*i +1];
					derr[i] = 0;
					peaks[0][i] =0;
					peaks[1][i] =0; 
					
				}
				
/*				
				FileToArray ("ach_0", ach[0], VAL_DOUBLE,num_ach_freq, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				FileToArray ("ach_1", ach[1], VAL_DOUBLE,num_ach_freq, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
*/				
				
				for(i = 1; i < num_ach_freq -1; i++)
				{
					
					derr[i] = ach[1][i ] - ach[1][i - 1];
				}
				
				
				for(i = 1; i < num_ach_freq -1; i++)
				{
					
					peaks[0][i] = ach[0][i];
					
					if(derr[i - 1]*derr[i] <=0)
					{
						if(derr[i - 1] >0)
						{
						
						peaks[1][i - 1]	 =  ach[1][i -1];
						}
					}
					else
					{
						peaks[1][i]	 =  0;  
					}
				
						
				}
				
				peaks[0][0]  =  ach[0][0];
				peaks[0][num_ach_freq -1] = 0; 
				
				k = 0;   
				for(i = 1; i < num_ach_freq -1; i++)
				{
					
					if(peaks[1][i] >0)
					{
						QQ[0][k] = peaks[0][i];
						QQ[1][k] = peaks[1][i];
					
						k++;
						
					}
					
				}
/*				
				for(i = 0; i < 65; i++)
				{
					QQ[4][i] = QQ[0][i]/(QQ[3][i] - QQ[2][i]);
					
					temp[5*i] = QQ[0][i];

					temp[5*i +2] = QQ[2][i]; 
					temp[5*i +3] = QQ[3][i]; 
					temp[5*i +4] = QQ[4][i]; 
					
				}
*/				
				
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,(double)num_ach_freq);   	
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
			
						PlotXY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, ach[0],ach[1],num_ach_freq -1,  VAL_DOUBLE,VAL_DOUBLE,

			//			PlotXY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, ach[0],peaks[1],num_ach_freq -1,  VAL_DOUBLE,VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			//			VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED); 
						
						
						
						//////////////////
						
	//					PlotXY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, QQ[0],QQ[4],k,  VAL_DOUBLE,VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
	//					VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_BLUE); 
						
				
						
	//					PlotXY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, QQ[0],QQ[3],k,  VAL_DOUBLE,VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
	//					VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN); 
						
	//					PlotXY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, QQ[0],QQ[4],k,  VAL_DOUBLE,VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
	//					VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN); 
						
				
	//		SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_4,k);	
						
	//					DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );   
	//					PlotXY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, QQ[0],QQ[4],k,  VAL_DOUBLE,VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
	//					VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
						
						
	//			   	ArrayToFile("QQ_OMEGA.txt", temp , VAL_DOUBLE, 65*5, 1 ,

int CVICALLBACK make_responce (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j;
	double tt;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			for(j = 0; j < 4410; j++)
				{
					
					tt = j*2*Pi()/(double)num_ach_freq;
					
					responce[j] = 0;
					
					
					for( i = 0; i < num_ach_freq; i++ )
					{
						
						responce[j] = responce[j] + ach[1][i]*sin(ach[0][i]*(double)j/4410);
						
					}
				}
			
			
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
				
				PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_GRAPH,responce,4410, VAL_DOUBLE,
	    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);

			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK save_responce (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
 			ArrayToFile("responce.txt", responce, VAL_DOUBLE, 4410, 1 ,
					VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,
					VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 10, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE );
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x1;
		
			sendBuff[7]	 = 0;
		
			sendBuff[8] = 0x23;
	
      //  	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 9);	
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 9);

			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}
