int CVICALLBACK copy_coeff_c_out (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	double plotbuff[128];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		
			for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
			{
				Ci_str_out[i]  = Ci_str_1_out[i];//*omega_coef[i]*omega_coef[i];
			
			}
				for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
							{                                                                        			
							  plotbuff[i] = Ci_str_out[i]; 

							}
				//			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS   ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,1.0); 
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
			
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK e_e0 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			exp_num = 0;
			
			active_cntrl = PANEL_FCONSTR_E0;
			
			param_num = 501;
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&level);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E0,&exp_e[0][level][nota]); 
			
			send_exp_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_61,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
	//		send_exp_coef(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_58,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			 
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, exp_e[0][level],  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
			
			

			break;
			
			case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS: 
			active_cntrl = PANEL_FCONSTR_E0;
			focus = 1; 
			r_focus = 0;	
			exp_num = 0;
			param_num = 501;
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&level);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E0,&exp_e[0][level][nota]); 
			Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, exp_e[0][level],  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
			

			break;
			
			case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL: 
					scroll_wheel(eventData1,0);
					if(r_focus == 0)
					{
						Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
					    send_exp_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_61,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
					//	send_exp_coef(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_58,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
					}
				
				
			break; 
			
		
			case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E0,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E0,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E0,exp_e[0][level][nota]);
			Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;

	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK e_e1 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			exp_num = 1;
			param_num = 502;
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&level);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E1,&exp_e[1][level][nota]);
			
			send_exp_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_61,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			
			
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                              						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, exp_e[1][level],  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
			break;
			
			case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			focus = 1; 
			r_focus = 0;					
			active_cntrl = PANEL_FCONSTR_E1;
			exp_num = 1;
			param_num = 502;
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&level);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E1,&exp_e[1][level][nota]); 
			Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, exp_e[1][level],  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
			break;
			case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL: 
					scroll_wheel(eventData1,0);
					if(r_focus == 0)
					{
						Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
					//	send_exp_coef(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_58,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
					    send_exp_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_61,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
					}				
				
			break;
			
		
			case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E1,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E1,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_E1,exp_e[1][level][nota]);
			Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, exp_e[2][level],  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);

			break;
			
			case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			focus = 1; 
			r_focus = 0;					
			active_cntrl = PANEL_FCONSTR_E2; 
			param_num = 503;
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&level);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);

			break;
			case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL: 
					scroll_wheel(eventData1,0);
					if(r_focus == 0)
					{
						Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);

			break;

			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A0,&exp_a[0][level][nota]); 
			exp_a_tmp[0][level][nota] = exp_a[0][level][nota];  
		    send_exp_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_61,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
			Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, exp_a[0][level],  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE, 
				
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);


			break;
			
			case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			focus = 1; 
			r_focus = 0;	
			active_cntrl = PANEL_FCONSTR_A0; 
			param_num = 511;
			exp_num = 0;
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&level); 
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A0,&exp_a[0][level][nota]); 
			exp_a_tmp[0][level][nota] = exp_a[0][level][nota];  
			Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
	    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, exp_a[0][level],  NumString, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);


			break;
			case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL: 
					scroll_wheel(eventData1,2);
					if(r_focus == 0)
					{
						Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
					    send_exp_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_61,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
					}				
				
			break; 
			case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A0,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A0,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_A0,exp_a[0][level][nota]);
			exp_a_tmp[0][level][nota] = exp_a[0][level][nota]; 
			Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_exp_coef (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
			///
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j,res;
	int temp[88];
	double decimat_24 = 16777215.0;
	int decim_32 = 2147000000;
	double decimat_23 = 4096.0;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			for(k = 0; k < 4; k++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{
						temp[i] = (int)(decimat_24*exp_e[k][j][i]);
						if(k < 2)
						{
							exp_2_write_flash[0][0][i + j*88 + k*440] = temp[i];  	
						}
						else							  
						{
							exp_2_write_flash[0][1][i + j*88 + (k - 2)*440] = temp[i];	
						}
						
					} 
					
					 send_udp_88( cmd0_force_graph, j+ k*5, temp);
					 Delay(0.01);
												  
				}
			
			}	
			
			for(k = 0; k < 4; k++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{
						temp[i] = (int)(decim_32*exp_d[k][j][i]);
						if(k < 2)
						{
							exp_2_write_flash[1][0][i + j*88 + k*440] = temp[i];  	
						}
						else							  
						{
							exp_2_write_flash[1][1][i + j*88 + (k-2)*440] = temp[i];	
						}
						
					}
					send_udp_88( cmd0_force_graph, 30 + j + 5*k, temp);
					Delay(0.01);
				}  
			
			}	
	
			for(k = 0; k < 4; k++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{
						temp[i] = (int)(decim_32*exp_a[k][j][i]);
						if(k < 2)
						{
							exp_2_write_flash[2][0][i + j*88 + k*440] = temp[i];  	
						}
						else							  
						{
							exp_2_write_flash[2][1][i + j*88 + (k-2)*440] = temp[i];	
						}
						
					}
					
					send_udp_88( cmd0_force_graph, 60 + j + 5*k, temp);
					Delay(0.01);
				}
			
			
			}	

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_exp_single (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j,res;
	int temp[88];
	double decimat_24 = 16777215.0;
	int decim_32 = 2147000000;
	double decimat_23 = 4096.0;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&level); 
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);
			
			for(k = 0; k < 4;k++)
			{
				
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_omega_test; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = level + 5*k; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
	
				res = (int)(decimat_24*exp_e[k][level][nota]);

			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;

			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);
			Delay(0.001);
				
			}
			//////////////////////////////////////
			
			for(k = 0; k < 4;k++)
			{
				
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_omega_test; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = level + 30 + 5*k; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
	
				res = (int)(decim_32*exp_d[k][level][nota]);

			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;

			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);
			Delay(0.001);
				
			}
			///////////////////////////
						
			for(k = 0; k < 4;k++)
			{
				
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_omega_test; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = level + 60 + 5*k; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
	
				res = (int)(decim_32*exp_a[k][level][nota]);

			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;

			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);
			Delay(0.001);
				
			}
			
		
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK copy_exp_nota (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,k;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			
			for(k = 0; k< 5;k++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
					
					exp_temp[0][k][j] = exp_e[k][j][nota]; 
					exp_temp[1][k][j] = exp_d[k][j][nota];
					exp_temp[2][k][j] = exp_a[k][j][nota];
					
				}
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK paste_exp_nota (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,k;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			
			for(k = 0; k< 5;k++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
					
					exp_e[k][j][nota] = exp_temp[0][k][j]; 
					exp_d[k][j][nota] = exp_temp[1][k][j];
					exp_a[k][j][nota] = exp_temp[2][k][j];
					
				}
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_wheel_scroll_step (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double d0,d1,d2,d3;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_5,&scroll_step);  
		break;

		case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_5,&scroll_step);
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X0,&d0);
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X1,&d1);
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X2,&d2);
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X3,&d3);
				
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				d0 = d0 + scroll_step;
				d1 = d1 + scroll_step;
				d2 = d2 + scroll_step;
				d3 = d3 + scroll_step;
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				d0 = d0 - scroll_step;
				d1 = d1 - scroll_step;
				d2 = d2 - scroll_step;
				d3 = d3 - scroll_step;	
			}
				
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X0,d0);
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X1,d1);
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X2,d2);
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FCONSTR_X3,d3);
				
				Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				send_exp_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_61,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);    
			break;
	}		
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK copy_level (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,ccc,level_to_copy;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&level_to_copy);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RING_14,&ccc);
			switch(ccc)
			{
			case 0: //A0
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
				{
					exp_temp_2_copy[i] = exp_a[0][level_to_copy][i]; 
				}
			break;	
			case 1: //A1
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
				{
					exp_temp_2_copy[i] = exp_a[1][level_to_copy][i]; 
				}
			break;					
			case 2: //A2
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
				{
					exp_temp_2_copy[i] = exp_a[2][level_to_copy][i]; 
				}
			break;
			case 3: //A3
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
				{
					exp_temp_2_copy[i] = exp_a[3][level_to_copy][i]; 
				}
			break;
			case 4: //D0
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
				{
					exp_temp_2_copy[i] = exp_d[0][level_to_copy][i]; 
				}
			break; 
			case 5: //D1
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
				{
					exp_temp_2_copy[i] = exp_d[1][level_to_copy][i]; 
				}
			break;				
			case 6: //D2
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
				{
					exp_temp_2_copy[i] = exp_d[2][level_to_copy][i]; 
				}
			break;
			case 7: //D3
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
				{
					exp_temp_2_copy[i] = exp_d[3][level_to_copy][i]; 
				}
			break;			
			case 8: //E0
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
				{
					exp_temp_2_copy[i] = exp_e[0][level_to_copy][i]; 
				}
				
			break;
			case 9: //E1
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
				{
					exp_temp_2_copy[i] = exp_e[1][level_to_copy][i]; 
				}
			break;				
			case 10: //E2
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
				{
					exp_temp_2_copy[i] = exp_e[2][level_to_copy][i]; 
				}
			break;
			case 11: //E3
				
				
				for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
				{
					exp_temp_2_copy[i] = exp_e[3][level_to_copy][i]; 
				}
			break;				
			
			
			}
			
			
		

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Ci_cos_xW (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,nn,ni;
	double plotbuff[128],plotbuff_1[128];
	double mult,range;
	double max,min;
	int imax,imin;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			range = 0.01;
			
	for(j = 0; j < 128; j++)
	{
							
		for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
					nn = (int)(i/2);
					ni = i - nn*2;
					plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256]; 
					
				}
								
				MaxMin1D (plotbuff, 88,&max, &imax, &min, &imin);
				if( max < -1.0*min)
				{
					mult = -1.0/min;	
				}
				else
				{
					mult = 1.0/max;	
				}
				
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
					plotbuff_1[i] = mult*plotbuff[i];
					
					if(plotbuff_1[i]>=0)
					{
						if(plotbuff_1[i] < range){plotbuff_1[i] = range;}	
						
					}
					else
					{
						if(plotbuff_1[i] > -1.0*range){plotbuff_1[i] = -1.0*range;}	
					}
					
					
					
				}
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
					nn = (int)(i/2);
					ni = i - nn*2;
					Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256] = plotbuff_1[i]; 
					
				}
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);		
				
		}
							
					

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK ci_out_mult_W (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,ni,nn;
	double plotbuff[128][88];
	double a;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			for(j = 0;j  < 128; j++)
				{
					
					a =  omega_coef[j]/omega_coef[0];
			
		
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{
				
						nn = (int)(i/2);
						ni = i - nn*2;
						Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256]*a;
						plotbuff[j][i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256];
					
					}
				
			
				//			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS   ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,1.0); 
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
			
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff[j],  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
						}
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

				for(j = 0; j < 5;j++)

						exp_e[j][level][i] = exp_e[j][level_to_copy][i];
						exp_d[j][level][i] = exp_d[j][level_to_copy][i];

int CVICALLBACK load_fFIR_1 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	int i,j;
	double delta_250, delta_48;
	double cc;
	int work_frq;
	int khz_96_on_off;
	
	double xmax,xmin;
	int imax,imin;
	double a,b;
	
	double first_derr[16384],second_derr[16384];
	double result_L[16384];	   
	double result_R[16384];
	double integral_R,integral_L,multiplier;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			if(dir_selected_flag_fir !=0)
			{
			
				DirSelectPopup (dir_name_fir, "choose dir", 1, 0, dir_name_fir);
				letter_fir = dir_name[0] - 97;
			
				SetDrive (letter_fir);
				SetDir (dir_name_fir);
				
			}
			else
			{
				DirSelectPopup ("", "choose dir", 1, 0, dir_name_fir);
				letter_fir = dir_name[0] - 97;
			
				SetDrive (letter_fir);
				SetDir (dir_name_fir);
				
			}
			dir_selected_flag_fir = 1;
			
			 SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_2, " ");

             SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_2,dir_name_fir);
			 
			 
			 	FileToArray ("impulse_resp_L.txt", impulse_resp_Left_1, VAL_DOUBLE,3*Fir_length, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
				
				FileToArray ("impulse_resp_R.txt", impulse_resp_Right_1, VAL_DOUBLE, 3*Fir_length, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);

				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,128.0); 
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,0.0,128.0); 
				
				
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
				
				PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_GRAPH,impulse_resp_Left_1,3*Fir_length, VAL_DOUBLE,
	    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);
				
				PlotY (PANEL, PANEL_GRAPH,impulse_resp_Right_1,3*Fir_length, VAL_DOUBLE,
	    		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
		
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK marry_fir (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double mix_0,mix_1;
	int i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_MIX_FIR,&mix_0);
				mix_1 = 1.0 - mix_0;
				
				for(i = 0; i < 3*Fir_length; i++)
				{
				
					impulse_resp_Left[i] =   mix_0*impulse_resp_Left_tmp[i]  + mix_1*impulse_resp_Left_1[i]; 
					impulse_resp_Right[i] =   mix_0*impulse_resp_Right_tmp[i]  + mix_1*impulse_resp_Right_1[i]; 
				
				
				}
				
				 prepare_FIR();

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

			if(val == 1)
			{

							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
			}
			else
			{
				
				
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Ci_str_out,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);			}

	}

}

int CVICALLBACK erase (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 

	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_PRESET_NUM,&val); 
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_erase; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = val; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[7]	 = val & 0x000000FF; 
			sendBuff[8]  = 0x00;
			sendBuff[9]  = 0x23;
		
			
			
		//	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10); 

			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK write_flash (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int i,k,j,cc,sector_num,nn;
	int coef;
	int res;
	int temp[1056];
	int Str,Harm; 
	double decimat_100 =  100;
	double ratio;

	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result; 
 
	double fb_mult;
	double decimat_23 = 65000.0;
	double decim_24 = 16777215.0;
	int decim_32 = 2147000000;
	double decimat_31 =	2147483648.0;
	float fir_vol;
	double val;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
		
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				array_to_send[i + 88*0] = ttn[i];
				array_to_send[i + 88*1] = dt[i]; 
				array_to_send[i + 88*2] = decr_op[i];
				array_to_send[i + 88*3] = decr_cl[i];
				array_to_send[i + 88*4] = disp[i];
				array_to_send[i + 88*5] = decr_disp[i];
				array_to_send[i + 88*6] = shteg[i];
				array_to_send[i + 88*7] = damping[i];
				array_to_send[i + 88*8] = 50;  //point noise
				array_to_send[i + 88*9] = Quan_NL[i];  //point noise  
			}
			for(i = 88*10; i < 1024; i++)
			{
				array_to_send[i] = 0;
				
			}
			
		     sector_num = 0;
			
			write_sector(sector_num); 
				
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
				
				//write ind_mult, ind_vol
				
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_72,&v_0); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_71,&v_1); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_70,&v_2); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_69,&v_3);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_107,&v_4);
			
		
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				array_to_send[i + 88*0] = (int)(ind_mult_0[i]*decimat_23);
				array_to_send[i + 88*1] = (int)(ind_mult_1[i]*decimat_23); 
				array_to_send[i + 88*2] = (int)(ind_mult_2[i]*decimat_23);
				array_to_send[i + 88*3] = (int)(ind_mult_3[i]*decimat_23);
				array_to_send[i + 88*4] = (int)(ind_mult_4[i]*decimat_23);
				array_to_send[i + 88*5] = (int)(v_0*ind_vol_0[i]*8388607.5);
				array_to_send[i + 88*6] = (int)(v_1*ind_vol_1[i]*8388607.5);
				array_to_send[i + 88*7] = (int)(v_2*ind_vol_2[i]*8388607.5);
				array_to_send[i + 88*8] = (int)(v_3*ind_vol_3[i]*8388607.5); 
				array_to_send[i + 88*9] = (int)(v_4*ind_vol_4[i]*8388607.5);
			}
			for(i = 88*10;i <1024;i++)
			{
				array_to_send[i] = 0;	
			}
			sector_num = 1; 
			write_sector(sector_num);
////////////////////////////////////////////////Gauss			
			
			for(k = 0; k < 2; k++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
							array_to_send[i + 88*j + k*440] = exp_2_write_flash[0][0][i + j*88 + k*440];	
						}
				}
				
			}
				sector_num  = 2;   //2
			  	write_sector(sector_num);
						
			for(k = 0; k < 2; k++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
							array_to_send[i + 88*j + k*440] = exp_2_write_flash[0][1][i + j*88 + k*440];	
						}
				}
				
			}
				sector_num  = 3;   //3
			  	write_sector(sector_num);
			
			for(k = 0; k < 2; k++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
							array_to_send[i + 88*j + k*440] = exp_2_write_flash[1][0][i + j*88 + k*440];	
						}
				}
				
			}
				sector_num  = 4;   //4
			  	write_sector(sector_num);
			
			for(k = 0; k < 2; k++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
							array_to_send[i + 88*j + k*440] = exp_2_write_flash[1][1][i + j*88 + k*440];	
						}
				}
				
			}
				sector_num  = 5;   //5
			  	write_sector(sector_num);
			
			for(k = 0; k < 2; k++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
							array_to_send[i + 88*j + k*440] = exp_2_write_flash[2][0][i + j*88 + k*440];	
						}
				}
				
			}
				sector_num  = 6;   //6
			  	write_sector(sector_num);
			for(k = 0; k < 2; k++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{
							array_to_send[i + 88*j + k*440] = exp_2_write_flash[2][1][i + j*88 + k*440];	
						}
				}
				
			}
				sector_num  = 7;   //7
			  	write_sector(sector_num);
				
				
				/////////////////Ci
			
				for(j = 0; j < 22;j++)
				{
					for(k = 0; k < 4; k++)
					{
					
						nn = j + k/2;
						
					
					
						for(i = 0; i < 256;i++)
						{
							Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i]; 
							array_to_send[i + k*256] = Float_Result.int_val; 
						}
					} 
					sector_num ++;
				  	write_sector(sector_num);
				}
				
				
				
			//////////////////////////////////////	Ci_coeff_str
				
				
				
				
				
				for(j = 0; j < 22;j++)
				{
					for(k = 0; k < 4; k++)
					{
						nn = j + k/2;
					
						for(i = 0; i < 256;i++)
						{
							Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)Ci_coeff_str[nn][i]; 
							array_to_send[i + k*256] = Float_Result.int_val; 
						}
					} 
					sector_num ++;
				  	write_sector(sector_num);		//sector_num 51
				}
				
			
				
				//////////////Omega, Q, Ci_out
				
				GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_87, &ratio); 
				for(i = 0; i < 256;i++)
				{
					array_to_send[i] = (int)(omega_coef[i]*omega_coef[i]*ratio);
				}
				
				GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_79, &ratio); 
				for(i = 0; i < 256;i++)
				{
					array_to_send[i + 256] = (int)(Q_coeff[i]*ratio); 
				}
				
				for(i = 0; i < 256;i++)
				{
					Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)Ci_str_out[i];
			 
					array_to_send[i + 2*256] = Float_Result.int_val; 
				}

				for(i = 768; i < 1024;i++)
				{
					array_to_send[i] = 0; 
				}
				
				sector_num ++;			   //52
				write_sector(sector_num);
				
					
					///////////////shape
					
				for(j = 0; j < 57 ; j++)
				{
					for(i = 0; i < 512; i++) 
					{ 
						array_to_send[i] = (int)(shape_to_flash[j][i]); 
					//	array_to_send[i + 512] = shape_512[j +1][i]; 
					}
					
					sector_num ++;				//53
					write_sector(sector_num);	 
					
				}
					
				for(i = 0; i < 256;i++)  // Mass
				{
					array_to_send[i] = (int)(Mass[i]*decimat_31/(omega_coef[i]*omega_coef[i]));;
				}
					sector_num ++;				//110
					write_sector(sector_num);	 
					
			/////deka_coeff
					
					for(j = 0; j < 4; j++)
					{
						for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
						{
							array_to_send[i + j*256] = decka_coeff_vol[j][i];	
						} 
					}
					sector_num ++;				  //111
					write_sector(sector_num);
					
					for(j = 4; j < 8; j++)
					{
						for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
						{
							array_to_send[i + (j - 4)*256] = decka_coeff_vol[j][i];	
						} 
					}
					sector_num ++; //112
					write_sector(sector_num);
					
					for(j = 8; j < 12; j++)
					{
						for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
						{
							array_to_send[i + (j - 8)*256] = decka_coeff_vol[j][i];	
						} 
					}
					sector_num ++;
					write_sector(sector_num);	 
					
					for(j = 12; j < 16; j++)
					{
						for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
						{
							array_to_send[i + (j - 12)*256] = decka_coeff_vol[j][i];	
						} 
					}
					sector_num ++;
					write_sector(sector_num);	 
					
					
					
					//////////////Fb,out_vol
				
					GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_3, &val);
		
					Float_Result.fl_val  = -1.0*(float)val; 
					
					array_to_send[0] =  Float_Result.int_val;		  //fb
					
					GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11, &val);   //vol
					Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)val;		
					array_to_send[1] =  Float_Result.int_val; 
					
					
					
					for(i = 2;i< 1024;i++)
					{
						array_to_send[i] = 0;	
					}
					sector_num ++;
					write_sector(sector_num);
					
					
				
				///////////Pitch  
				
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{
						for(j = 0; j < 3; j++)
						{
							for(k = 0;k < 4; k++)
							{    
								
								temp[12*i + 4*j +k] = Pitch[i][j][k];

							}
						}
				
					}
					
					for(i = 0; i < 1024; i++) 
					{
						
						array_to_send[i] =  temp[i];
						
					}
					
					sector_num ++;	//	sector num 116
					write_sector(sector_num);
					
					for(i = 0; i < 32; i++) 
					{
						
						array_to_send[i] =  temp[i + 1024];
						
					}
					for(i = 32; i < 1024; i++) 
					{
						
						array_to_send[i] =  0;
						
					}
					sector_num ++;
					write_sector(sector_num);	 //117
					
					
					
					
					
					
				
			break;
	}
	return 0;

}

int CVICALLBACK Set_shteg (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val,res;
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_RING_12, &val);
		
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_transmit_sel; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[7]	 = val & 0x000000FF; 
			sendBuff[8]  = 0x00;
			sendBuff[9]  = 0x23;
		
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);  	
		
	
			Delay(0.001); 

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK switch_border (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};    
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12, &val);  
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_transmit_sel; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[7]	 = val & 0x000000FF; 
			sendBuff[8]  = 0x00;
			sendBuff[9]  = 0x23;
		
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);  	

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  128 - offs_scale, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
	}	
*/
			break;
			
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK Switch_Led_8 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8,&val);
			if(val == 1)
			{
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,0);
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_zero_single_point (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int LED;
	int n,nn,ni,i,xint;
	double plotbuff[128];
	double x,y;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		//	GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8, &LED);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			
			
			nn = (int)(nota/2);
			ni = nota - nn*2;
			
			for(i = 0; i< 128;i++)
			{
				plotbuff[i] = 0;
			}
			
				
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4,1,&x,&y);
			
				xint=(int)x + offs_scale;;                                                                   
				if(xint < 0) xint = 0; 
				if(xint > 127) xint = 127;
			
				  
				if(LED == 1)
				{
					
				  
		
					  Ci_coeff_str[nn][xint + ni*256] = 0;
					  Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][xint + ni*256] = 0; 
				
                                               			
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256];
						  
						}
						
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
					
				}			


			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Zero_note_fb (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int LED;
	int n,nn,ni,i,xint;
	double plotbuff[128];
	double x,y;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		//	GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8, &LED);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_OFFS,&offs_scale); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			
			
			nn = (int)(nota/2);
			ni = nota - nn*2;
			
			for(i = 0; i< 128;i++)
			{
				plotbuff[i] = 0;
			}
			
				
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4,1,&x,&y);
			
				xint=(int)x + offs_scale;;                                                                   
				if(xint < 0) xint = 0; 
				if(xint > 127) xint = 127;
			
				  
				if(LED == 1)
				{
					
				  
				 for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
				 {
					  Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256] = 0;
					  Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][i + ni*256] = 0; 
				
                 }                             			
						for(i = offs_scale; i < 128; i++)                                                 			
						{                                                                        			
						  plotbuff[i - offs_scale] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][i + ni*256];
						  
						}
						
						DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN); 
						send_ci_coef_single(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_97,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);   
					
				}			


			break;
	}
	return 0;
}
int CVICALLBACK test_3d (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	 VARIANT vArray;
	
	int i,j,k,nn,ni;
	double dat_array[100][100];
	double x,y,dx,dy,gamm;
	int num,transd_num;
	
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_12,&gamm);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2,&num);
		//	GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER,&transd_num);
		//	gamm = 60;
			
			
		//	gamm = 0.01;
			
			
			
				deck[0][0] =  0.25;
			    deck[0][1] =  0.25;
				deck[0][2] =  decka_coeff[0][num];
			
				deck[1][0] =  0.5;
			    deck[1][1] =  0.25;
				deck[1][2] =  decka_coeff[1][num];  
				
			
				deck[2][0] =  0.75;
			    deck[2][1] =  0.25;
				deck[2][2] =  decka_coeff[2][num];
				
				deck[3][0] =  0.25;
			    deck[3][1] =  0.5;
				deck[3][2] =  decka_coeff[3][num];  
				
				deck[4][0] =  0.5;
			    deck[4][1] =  0.5;
				deck[4][2] =  decka_coeff[4][num];
				
				deck[5][0] =  0.75;
			    deck[5][1] =  0.5;
				deck[5][2] =  decka_coeff[5][num];   
				
				deck[6][0] =  0.25;
			    deck[6][1] =  0.75;
				deck[6][2] =  decka_coeff[6][num];  
				
				deck[7][0] =  0.5;
			    deck[7][1] =  0.75;
				deck[7][2] =  decka_coeff[7][num];  
				
				deck[8][0] =  0.75;
			    deck[8][1] =  0.75;
				deck[8][2] =  decka_coeff[8][num];  
				
					/*		
							for(j = 0; j < 88;j++)
							{
								nn = (int)(j/2);
								ni = j - nn*2;
												   
								for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
								
								{
									dat_array[i][j] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256];
								
								
								}
							}
							
					*/		
							
							
						
			
			
			
			
			for(i = 0; i < 100; i++)
			{
				dx = (double)i/100.0;
				for(j = 0; j < 100;j++)
				{
					dy = (double)j/100.0;
					dat_array[i][j]  = 0;
					
					for(k = 0; k < 9; k++)
					{
					
				 	dat_array[i][j]  = dat_array[i][j] + deck[k][2]*exp(-1.0*gamm*(
						(deck[k][0] - dx)*(deck[k][0] - dx) + (deck[k][1] - dy)*(deck[k][1] - dy)));	
				
					}
				}
			}
			
			
			
			
/*			
            //Put 2D array in VARIANT and plot surface
            CA_VariantSet2DArray (&vArray, CAVT_DOUBLE, 100, 100, &dat_array);
            CW3DGraphLib_CWPlot3DPlot3DSimpleSurface (plotHandle, NULL, vArray, CA_DEFAULT_VAL);
		
            CA_VariantClear (&vArray);
			
*/
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_deka_coef_0 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,transd_num;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
	//		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER,&transd_num);
		for(transd_num = 0; transd_num < 16; transd_num++)
			{	
				for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
				{
					decka_coeff[transd_num][i] = 0;	
					decka_coeff_temp[transd_num][i] = 0;
				
				}
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

			send_gen_frq(panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			
			
				for(i = 0; i < 16; i++)

				}
					
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );

						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);   
	//			test_3d(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_61,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
/*			
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;

			sendBuff[8] = 0x23;

			break;
	}

int CVICALLBACK Set_Deka_m (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int transd_num;
	double x,y;
	
	double dval;
	static int xint; 
	double plotbuff[4096];
	double plotbuff_1[4096];
	
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	
	 
	int i,j,k,led,pan,res;
	int val, LED_ADJ;
	int mode_num_d;
	int left_right;
	double  sc_val,out_vol,d,d0;
	int up_lo;
	int spline_mode;
	int okt_num; 
	int tr_led[4]; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;    
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2,&mode_num_d);
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2,1,&x,&y);
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,&out_vol); 
				xint=(int)x;                                                                   
				if(xint<0) xint=0; 
				
				decka_coeff[xint][mode_num_d]= y;
				decka_coeff_temp[xint][mode_num_d]= y; 
					
				for(i = 0; i < 16; i++)                                                 			
				{                                                                        			
					 plotbuff[i] = decka_coeff[i][mode_num_d]; 
				}
				
				plotbuff[16] = 0;
					
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					    
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2, plotbuff,  16, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);    
				
		//			test_3d(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_61,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
				send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 

			break;
		case EVENT_VAL_CHANGED:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2,&mode_num_d);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,&out_vol);  
			
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2,1,&x,&y);
			
				xint=(int)x;                                                                   
				if(xint<0) xint=0; 
				
				decka_coeff[xint][mode_num_d]= y;
			
				decka_coeff_temp[xint][mode_num_d]= y;		
				for(i = 0; i < 16; i++)                                                 			
				{                                                                        			
					 plotbuff[i] = decka_coeff[i][mode_num_d]; 
				}
				plotbuff[16] = 0;	
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					    
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2, plotbuff,  16, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);    
				
	//				test_3d(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_61,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
				send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break; 
					
			
			case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
			
		 		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2,&mode_num_d); 
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER,&transd_num); 
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2,1,&x,&y); 
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,&out_vol);
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_ADJ,&LED_ADJ);  
				xint=(int)x;                                                                   
				if(xint<0) xint=0;
				
				
				if(LED_ADJ == 0)
				{
				
					if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
					{
						
				 		d = y*(1+ 0.03*(double)eventData2);
				
					}
					if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
					{
						d = y*(1 - 0.03*(double)eventData2);
			
					}
					
					decka_coeff[xint][mode_num_d]= d;
				
					decka_coeff_temp[xint][mode_num_d]= d;
							
								                                                			
								for(i = 0; i < 16; i++)                                                 			
								{                                                                        			
								  plotbuff[i] = decka_coeff[i][mode_num_d];                                               			
								}
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2, plotbuff,  16, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
								send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
								out_vol_out(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
								
				}
				else
					{
						if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
						{
						
					 		d = y*(1+ 0.03*(double)eventData2);
				
						}
						if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
						{
							d = y*(1 - 0.03*(double)eventData2);
			
						}
						
						if(d >= 1){d = 1;}
						if(d <= -1){d = -1;}
					d0 = sqrt(1 - d*d);
					decka_coeff[xint][mode_num_d]= d;
					
					decka_coeff_temp[xint][mode_num_d]= d;
					
					decka_coeff[0][mode_num_d]= d0;
					
					decka_coeff_temp[0][mode_num_d]= d0;
					
					for(i = 0; i < 16; i++)                                                 			
								{                                                                        			
								  plotbuff[i] = decka_coeff[i][mode_num_d];                                               			
								}
					
					DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2, plotbuff,  16, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
								send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
								out_vol_out(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
						
					}
	
				break;
			case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK_UP:
				
					GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_0,&tr_led[0]); 
					GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_1,&tr_led[1]); 
					GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_2,&tr_led[2]); 
					GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_3,&tr_led[3]); 
				
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2,&mode_num_d);
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,&out_vol);  
			
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA_2,1,&x,&y);
				xint=(int)x;
			
			temp_transduser_array[xint] = -1.0*y/decka_coeff[0][mode_num_d];
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					    
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA, temp_transduser_array,  16, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED); 
			break;
			
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK_UP:
			
			
			send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			out_vol_out(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;	

	break;
			
	}
	return 0;
}
int CVICALLBACK Set_Deka_coeff (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int transd_num;
	double x,y;
	
	double dval;
	static int xint; 
	double plotbuff[4096];
	double plotbuff_1[4096];
	
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	
	 
	int i,j,k,led,pan,res;

	int num_shape;
	int left_right;
	double  sc_val;
	int up_lo;

	switch (event)
	{

	//				test_3d(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_61,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
					Set_deka_mode_num(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);

			break;
		case EVENT_VAL_CHANGED:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER,&transd_num);
				GetGraphCursor (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH_DEKA,1,&x,&y);
			
				xint=(int)x;                                                                   
				if(xint<0) xint=0; 
				
				decka_coeff[transd_num][xint]= y;
						
				for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)

				}

		case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK_UP: 
			
			send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			
 /*

int CVICALLBACK send_deka_coeff (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int transd_num;  
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi;
	
	int i,k,j;
	int coef;
	int res;
	double temp[256],temp_out[256];
	int Str,Harm; 
	double decimat_100 =  100;
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;
	double out_vol;
	int sdvig_kostyl = 98;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,&out_vol);
       for(j = 0; j < 16 ; j ++) 
		{
			transd_num = j;
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_transmit_sel; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = transd_num;
	
			k = 0;
			
			for(i = 0; i < 256 ; i ++) 
			{
				temp_out[i] = decka_coeff[transd_num][i]*out_vol*Ci_str_1_out[i];
				
				 	
		//		Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)(decka_coeff[transd_num][i]*out_vol);
				Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)temp_out[i]*(double)tr_led[transd_num]; 
				res = Float_Result.int_val;
				decka_coeff_vol[transd_num][i] = res;
		//		res = (int)decka_coeff[transd_num][i];
					
			
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8); 
			Delay(0.01); 
		}
	   Delay(0.05); 
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
		//	FlushOutQ (comport);

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK init_DAC (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
		char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	int init_dac[14];
	int res,i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_27,&res);
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_FB_test; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
			

			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 12);
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  
			
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK mult_deka_coef (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,num_tr;
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER,&num_tr);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_21,&val);
			
			
				for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
				{
					decka_coeff[num_tr][i] = val*decka_coeff[num_tr][i];//decka_coeff[j][i]*10.0;	
				}
			
		

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)

	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
	int err;
	int val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RING_13,&val);
			
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_nl_sl; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x03; 
						   
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x1;
		
			sendBuff[7]	 = val;
		
			sendBuff[8] = 0x23;
	
      //  	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 9);	
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 9);

			
			break;
	}

}

int CVICALLBACK start_gen (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double start_frq,stop_frq;
	char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;   
	int i,k,j;
	double frq,step;

	int res;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_16, &start_frq); 
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_134, &stop_frq);
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_15, &step); 
			
			
		//	Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)rat; 
			
		//	res = Float_Result.int_val; 
			
			frq = start_frq;
			while(frq < stop_frq)
			{
			
				res = (int)(frq*690);
			
			
				sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
				sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
				sendBuff[3]	 = cmd_graph_m; 
				sendBuff[4]	 = 0x00; 
				sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
				sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
								lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
								mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
								hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
								hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

							sendBuff[7] = lo;
							sendBuff[8] = mid;
							sendBuff[9] = hi;
							sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
				sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	
				UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  	
		
				Delay(0.001); 
				frq = frq + step;
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_sigmoid (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
		char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result; 
	int i,k,j;

	int res;
	int LED; 
	double decimat_20 = 1048576.0;
	
	double decimat_24 = 16777215.0;
	double decimat_25 = 16777215.0*2.0; 
	double decimat_28 = 268435456.0; 
	double decimat_30 = 1073741824.0; 
	double decimat_32 = 4294967296.0; 
	double decimat_31 = 2147483648.0; 
	
	double val; 
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICSLIDE_2, &val); 
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12, &LED); 
			if (LED == 0)
			{
			Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)val; 
			
		//	res = (int)(val*decimat_31);
			res = Float_Result.int_val;  
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_fir_gain; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
			
			

			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	//	ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 12);
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  	
		//	FlushOutQ (comport);
			Delay(0.001);
			
			
		break;
		}

	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_deka_vol (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
		char sendBuff[20000] = {0};
	unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
	union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;   
	int i,k,j;
	double vol,step;

	int res;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_DEKA_VOL, &vol); 
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_15, &step);  
			
			
			Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)vol; 
			
			res = Float_Result.int_val; 
			
		//	res = (int)(frq*690.0*1.025);
			
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd_graph_m; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  	
		
			Delay(0.001); 
				

			break;
			
			
		case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_DEKA_VOL, &vol);  
		GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_15, &step);   
	
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				vol = vol + (double)eventData2*step;
				
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				vol = vol - (double)eventData2*step;
			
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_DEKA_VOL,vol);
			
			send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);     
			
			Float_Result.fl_val  = (float)vol; 
			
			res = Float_Result.int_val; 
			
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd_graph_m; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x04; 
			
							lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
							mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
							hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

						sendBuff[7] = lo;
						sendBuff[8] = mid;
						sendBuff[9] = hi;
						sendBuff[10] = hi_hi;
		
			sendBuff[11]  = 0x23;
	
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 12);  	
			

	}
	return 0;
}

		for(i = 0; i < 128; i++)
		{
		      decka_coeff[transd_num][i] = 0;	
					decka_coeff_temp[transd_num][i] = 0;
				
					
		}
				
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK normal_modes (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,val,num_mode;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_NORMAL, &val);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_MUTE, 0);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_SOLO, 0);
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NUM_MODE, &num_mode); 
			
			for(j = 0; j < 44;j++)
			{
				for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
				{
									  
				  Ci_coeff_cos[j][i] = Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[j][i];
				  Ci_coeff_str[j][i] = Ci_coeff_str_tmp[j][i];
				}
			}
			send_ci_zeros(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_101,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK mute_mode (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
		int i,j,val,num_mode,nn,ni;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_MUTE, &val);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_NORMAL, 0);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_SOLO, 0);
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NUM_MODE, &num_mode); 
			
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
			{
					nn = (int)(i/2);
					ni = i - nn*2;
				
									  
				  Ci_coeff_cos[nn][num_mode + ni*256] = 0;
				  Ci_coeff_str[nn][num_mode + ni*256] = 0;
				
			}
			send_ci_zeros(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_101,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK solo_mode (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
		int i,j,val,num_mode,nn,ni;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_SOLO, &val);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_NORMAL, 0);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_MUTE, 0);
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NUM_MODE, &num_mode); 
			zero_ci_str_all(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_100,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
			{
					nn = (int)(i/2);
					ni = i - nn*2;
				
									  
				  Ci_coeff_cos[nn][num_mode + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][num_mode + ni*256];
				  Ci_coeff_str[nn][num_mode + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][num_mode + ni*256];
				
			}
			send_ci_zeros(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_101,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			
			 

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK send_nl_modes (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
 double val;
 int decimat32 =  2147483648;
 int res,led,i,k;
 char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 
 unsigned char lo,mid,hi,hi_hi; 
 union {unsigned int int_val;  float fl_val;}Float_Result;    

	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
		
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NL, &val);
			
			
			val = val*2147483648.0;
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = CMD_sdvig_f; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0;
	
			k = 0;
		
			for(i = 0; i < 128 ; i ++) 
			{
				 	
				res  = (int)(val*NL[i]);
			
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);  
	
			Delay(0.001);	
			
		

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_edit_mode (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int mode_index;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_EDIT_MODE, &mode_index);
			switch(mode_index)
			{
				case 0:   //string
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,ATTR_PLOT_BGCOLOR,VAL_BLACK);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_6,0);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8,1);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_DISPLAY,1);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_DISPLAY,1);//strings
					MUTE_mode = 0;
					
				break;
				case 1:   //moda
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,ATTR_PLOT_BGCOLOR,VAL_BLACK);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_6,1);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8,0);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_DISPLAY,1);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_DISPLAY,2);//moda	
					scale88(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_139,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
					SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_4, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS ,VAL_AUTOSCALE,-0.001,0.001); 
					MUTE_mode = 0;
					
				break;
				case 2:   //amps
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,ATTR_PLOT_BGCOLOR,VAL_BLACK);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_6,0);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8,0);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_DISPLAY,0);
					displ_modes_amps (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_28, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
					MUTE_mode = 0;
					
				break;
				case 3: //mute
					SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2,ATTR_PLOT_BGCOLOR,VAL_DK_RED);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_6,0);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_8,0);
					SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_DISPLAY,0); 
					displ_modes_amps (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_28, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
					MUTE_mode = 1;
					
				break;
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK mute_all (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
			{
				mute_amps[i] =  -1.0;	
				
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK unmute_all (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
int i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
			{
				mute_amps[i] = 1.0;	
				
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK moda_2_fb (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,nn,ni,val;
	double plotbuff[88];
	double alpha;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_NUMERIC_36,&val);
			
			alpha = 1.0/(ttn[0]);
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
				
					nn = (int)(i/2);
					ni = i - nn*2;
					Ci_coeff_str[nn][val + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][val + ni*256]*ttn[i]*alpha;
					plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_str[nn][val + ni*256];
					
				}
			
			displ_modes_amps (panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_28, EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Invert_Mode (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,ni,nn,mode_num;
	double plotbuff[88];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36,&mode_num); 
			
						for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
						{ 
							nn = (int)(i/2);
							ni = i - nn*2;
							Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_num + ni*256] = -1.0*Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_num + ni*256]; 
							plotbuff[i] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][mode_num + ni*256]; 
					
						}
						
				update_modes_amp(plotbuff,mode_num,0);
				send_ci_zeros(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_101,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, plotbuff,  72, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK moda_solo (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,mode_num,val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36,&mode_num); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_MODA_SOLO,&val); 
			if(val == 1)
			{	
				for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
				{
					mute_amps_temp[i] = mute_amps[i];	
					mute_amps[i] = -1.0;	
				}
			
				mute_amps[mode_num] = 1.0;
			}
			else
			{
				for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
				{
					mute_amps[i] = mute_amps_temp[i];	
						
				}
				
			}
//			update_modes_amp(plotbuff,mode_num,0);
			send_ci_zeros(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_101,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_mute_modes (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
				{
					mute_amps_set[i] = mute_amps[i];	
						
				}
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK extract_mute_modes (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
				{
					mute_amps[i] = mute_amps_set[i];	
						
				}
			
			send_ci_zeros(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_101,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}


int CVICALLBACK scale_gr_mode_2 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT :
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SCALE_GR_MODE_2,&val);
			val = val*val*val;
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS   ,VAL_MANUAL,-val,val);

		break;
		case EVENT_VAL_CHANGED :
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SCALE_GR_MODE_2,&val);
			val = val*val*val;
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS   ,VAL_MANUAL,-val,val);

		break;
		case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL :
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SCALE_GR_MODE_2,&val);
			
				
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				val = val + 0.005;
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SCALE_GR_MODE_2,val); 
				val = val*val*val;
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS   ,VAL_MANUAL,-val,val);
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				val = val - 0.005;
				if(val < 0.01) val = 0.01;
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_SCALE_GR_MODE_2,val); 
				val = val*val*val;
				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE_2, VAL_LEFT_YAXIS   ,VAL_MANUAL,-val,val);
			}
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK FIR_off (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			for(i = 0; i< 3*Fir_length; i++)
			{
				impulse_resp_Left[i]  = 0;
				impulse_resp_Right[i]  = 0; 
				
			}
			
				for(i = 0; i < Fir_length; i++)
				{
					
					impulse_resp_R[i] = 0;
					
					impulse_resp_R1[i] = 0;
					
					impulse_resp_R2[i] = 0; 
					
					impulse_resp_L[i] = 0;
					
					impulse_resp_L1[i] = 0;
					
					impulse_resp_L2[i] = 0;
				}
			
			impulse_resp_R[0] =  1.0;
			impulse_resp_L[0]  = 1.0; 

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

//	double decimat_24 = 4294967296.0 ;

		break;

							ind_tail_4[nota] = ind_tail_0[nota];

						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_tail_0[nota] = ind_tail_1[nota];
							ind_tail_2[nota] = ind_tail_1[nota];
							ind_tail_3[nota] = ind_tail_1[nota];
							ind_tail_4[nota] = ind_tail_1[nota];
							
						 }
						 
						 
						 
						 c1 = 6;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_3,ind_tail_1[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_tail_1,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 2:
				
						 ind_tail_2[nota] = ind_tail_2[nota] - eventData2*step[19];
						 
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_tail_1[nota] = ind_tail_2[nota];
							ind_tail_2[nota] = ind_tail_2[nota];
							ind_tail_3[nota] = ind_tail_2[nota];
							ind_tail_4[nota] = ind_tail_2[nota]; 
						
						 }
						 
						 
						 
						 c1 = 7;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_3,ind_tail_2[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_tail_2,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 3:
				
						 ind_tail_3[nota] = ind_tail_3[nota] - eventData2*step[19];
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_tail_0[nota] = ind_tail_3[nota];
							ind_tail_1[nota] = ind_tail_3[nota];
							ind_tail_2[nota] = ind_tail_3[nota];
							ind_tail_4[nota] = ind_tail_3[nota];
							
						 }
						 
						 
						 c1 = 8;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_3,ind_tail_3[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_tail_3,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_DK_CYAN);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
						case 4:
				
						 ind_tail_4[nota] = ind_tail_4[nota] - eventData2*step[19];
						 
						 if(led == 1)
						 {
							ind_tail_0[nota] = ind_tail_4[nota];
							ind_tail_1[nota] = ind_tail_4[nota];
							ind_tail_2[nota] = ind_tail_4[nota];
							ind_tail_3[nota] = ind_tail_4[nota];
							 
						 }
						 
						 
						 c1 = 9;
				 
						 	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_3,ind_tail_4[nota]); 
						    DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                                                                         						
					    	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, ind_tail_4,  88, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_RED);
						
							count = 88;
						break;
				
					}
			
			}
			
		
			
/////////////////////////////////////
		Construct_force_shape(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_130,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);      	
		
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd_total_vol; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = c1;
	
			k = 0;
			for(i = 0; i < count; i ++)
			{
 				if(c1 >= 5)
				
				{
					switch (ringslide2)
					{
						case 0:   
							res =   (int)(ind_tail_0[i]*decimat_24);
						 
						break;
						
						case 1:   
							res =   (int)(ind_tail_1[i]*decimat_24); 
						break;
						case 2:   
							res =   (int)(ind_tail_2[i]*decimat_24); 
						break;
						case 3:   
							res =   (int)(ind_tail_3[i]*decimat_24); 
						break;
						case 4:   
							res =   (int)(ind_tail_4[i]*decimat_24); 
						break;
					}
				}
				
				//	res =   100; //(int)(0.00001*16777216);  
					
					lo    = (res)      & 0x000000ff;
					mid   = (res >>8)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi    = (res>>16)  & 0x000000ff; 
					hi_hi = (res>>24)  & 0x000000ff; 

				sendBuff[7+k] = lo;
				sendBuff[7+k+1] = mid;
				sendBuff[7+k+2] = hi;
				sendBuff[7+k+3] = hi_hi;
				k+=4;

			}
		
			Length232 = k;
			sendBuff[5]	 =( Length232>>8) & 0x0000FF;
			sendBuff[6]	 = Length232 & 0x000000FF; 
		
			sendBuff[Length232 + 7] = 0x23;
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);     
	
//			ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, Length232 + 8);
/////////////////////////////////////////////////////////			
			
	//		mode_addr_shift =  6;
	//		Send_param_pack4(disp[nota],disp[nota],disp[nota]);
		 }
		 
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[19] = step[19]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[19] = step[19]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_3,step[19]);  
			 
		 }
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_3,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ_3,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Set_vol_level_exp (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,k,length;
	double dx,dt,dd;
	double decimat_23 = 65500.0;
	double ex0,ex1,ex2,ex3,ex4;
	double dist0,dist1,dist2,dist3,dist4;
	double amp0,amp1,amp2,amp3,amp4;
	double d1,d2;
	double temp0[1000000];
	int V;
	
	double exp_aa[5][88],exp_dd[5][88],exp_ee[5][88];
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_17,&V); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_2,&level);  
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota); 
			
				for(j = 0; j < 5;j++)
				{
					for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
					{
						exp_aa[j][i] = exp_a[j][0][i] + (double)V*(exp_a[j][4][i] - exp_a[j][0][i])/127.0;
						exp_dd[j][i] = exp_d[j][0][i] + (double)V*(exp_d[j][4][i] - exp_d[j][0][i])/127.0; 
						exp_ee[j][i] = exp_e[j][0][i] + (double)V*(exp_e[j][4][i] - exp_e[j][0][i])/127.0; 
						
					
					
					}
					
				}
				
			
					
			
				for(j = 0; j < 5;j++)
				{
					for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
					{
						exp_e[j][level][i] = exp_ee[j][i];
						exp_d[j][level][i] = exp_dd[j][i]; 
						exp_a[j][level][i] = exp_aa[j][i]; 
					
					}
				}

 /*
			k = 0;
			i = 0;
			dt = ind_mult_2[nota];
			dd = 0.5*(int)(dt*decimat_23);					
			while(k < 1000000000)
			{   
				i++;
				length = i;
	//			if(i == 1000000){ break;} 
				k = k + dd;	
				
			}
			
			dx = (double)length/(double)k;
			
			
			for(i = 0; i < length; i++)
			{	
			
	 		    temp0[i] =  exp_a[0][level][i]*exp(-1.0*exp_e[0][level][i]*((double)i*dt - exp_d[0][level][i])*((double)i*dt - exp_d[0][level][i])) +
							exp_a[1][level][i]*exp(-1.0*exp_e[1][level][i]*((double)i*dt - exp_d[1][level][i])*((double)i*dt - exp_d[1][level][i])) + 
							exp_a[2][level][i]*exp(-1.0*exp_e[2][level][i]*((double)i*dt - exp_d[2][level][i])*((double)i*dt - exp_d[2][level][i])) + 
							exp_a[3][level][i]*exp(-1.0*exp_e[3][level][i]*((double)i*dt - exp_d[3][level][i])*((double)i*dt - exp_d[3][level][i])) ; 
				
			
			
			}
			
		
			
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,(double)length);
			DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
			
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, temp0,  length, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);  
			
*/		 
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK sw_pedal (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0};
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_24,&val);
			
			val = val*127;
			
							sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
							sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
							sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_pedal;
							sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01;
		
							sendBuff[5]	 = 0x0;
							sendBuff[6]	 = 0x01;
		
							sendBuff[7]	 = val; 
		
							sendBuff[8] = 0x23;
																				 
							UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 9);

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}
