int CVICALLBACK load_ref_ci (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			if(dir_selected_flag_fir !=0)
			{
			
				DirSelectPopup (dir_name_fir, "choose dir", 1, 0, dir_name_fir);
				letter_fir = dir_name[0] - 97;
			
				SetDrive (letter_fir);
				SetDir (dir_name_fir);
				
			}
			else
			{
				DirSelectPopup ("", "choose dir", 1, 0, dir_name_fir);
				letter_fir = dir_name[0] - 97;
			
				SetDrive (letter_fir);
				SetDir (dir_name_fir);
				
			}
			dir_selected_flag_fir = 1;
			
			 SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_2, " ");

             SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_2,dir_name_fir);
			 
			 				
				FileToArray("Ci_coef_cos.txt", Ci_coeff_cos_ref, VAL_DOUBLE, 44*harm_num, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
				
				FileToArray("Ci_coef_str.txt", Ci_coeff_str_ref, VAL_DOUBLE, 44*harm_num, 1, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_ASCII);
			 

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK copy_coeff_ref (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
		int i,j,numstr;
	
	int nn,ni;
	
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_STRING_8, &numstr); 
			
			nn = (int)numstr/2;
			ni = numstr - nn*2;
			for(j = 0; j < 128; j++)
			{
				 Copy_ci_cos[j] = Ci_coeff_cos_ref[nn][j + ni*256];
				 Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_cos[j];
				
				 Copy_ci_str[j] = Ci_coeff_str_ref[nn][j + ni*256];   
				 Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][j + ni*256] = Copy_ci_str[j] ; 
			}
		

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}
int CVICALLBACK copy_ref_mode (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
		int i,j,nummod;
	
	int nn,ni;
	
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36, &nummod); 
			
			for( i= 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				nn = (int)i/2;
				ni = i - nn*2;
				
				 Ci_coeff_cos[nn][nummod + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_cos_ref[nn][nummod + ni*256];
				 Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][nummod + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_cos_ref[nn][nummod + ni*256];
				
				 Ci_coeff_str[nn][nummod + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_str_ref[nn][nummod + ni*256];   
				 Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][nummod + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_str_ref[nn][nummod + ni*256];
				
			}
		
			;
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK shift_coeff (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,nn,ni,num_start,shift;
	double plotbuff[128],plotbuff_1[128];
	
	int numstr; 
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
		//	GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_100,&num);
			
			num_start = 47;
			shift = 10;
			
			for(i = 0; i < 128 ; i ++) 
			{
				 	
				plotbuff[i] = Ci_str_out[i];
			}
			for(i = num_start; i < 128 ; i ++) 
			{
				 	
				Ci_str_out[i] = plotbuff[i - shift] ;
			}
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
			{
				nn = (int)i/2;
				ni = i - nn*2;
				for(j = num_start; j < 128; j++)
				{
					  Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][j - shift + ni*256];  
				
					 Ci_coeff_str[nn][j + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][j - shift + ni*256]; 
			
					
				}
			
			}
			  for(i = 0; i < 88;i++)
			{
				nn = (int)i/2;
				ni = i - nn*2;
				for(j = 0; j < 128; j++)
				{
					 
					  Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][j + ni*256] = Ci_coeff_cos[nn][j + ni*256];   
				
					 	Ci_coeff_str_tmp[nn][j + ni*256] =  Ci_coeff_str[nn][j + ni*256] ;
				}
			
			}
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_transduser (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int mode_num_d;
	int i,j;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
//		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2,&mode_num_d); 
//		temp_transduser_array[0] = 1.0;;
		
		for(i = 0; i < 16; i++)
		{
			for(j = 0; j < 128; j++)
			{
			 decka_coeff_temp[i][j]= 1.0;	
			 decka_coeff[i][j]= 1.0;
			}
		 
		}
		send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			out_vol_out(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Deka_On_Off (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		int val;
		
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			amp_sp = 2.8;
			amp_deka = 0.043;
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_25,&val);
			if(val == 0)//speakers
			{
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12,1); 
				
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,0.0);
				show_hide_deka(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
				out_vol_out(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
				
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12,0);
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,amp_sp);
				out_vol_out(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			}
			else
			{
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12,0); 
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,0.0);
				out_vol_out(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
				
				
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12,1); 
				show_hide_deka(panelHandle, PANEL_LED_12,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,amp_deka);   
			//	send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				
			//	init_DAC(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_143,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				
				
				out_vol_out(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERICTHERM_11,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK mute_deca_ciout (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val,i,num_m;
	double plotbuff[256];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_26,&val);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2,&num_m);  
			if(val == 0)
			{
				for(i = 0;i < 256; i++)
				{
					Ci_str_1_out[i] = Ci_str_1_out_tmp[i];	
				}
				
					for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
							{                                                                        			
							  plotbuff[i ] = Ci_str_1_out[i];
							  
							}
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
			}
			if(val == 1)
			{
				for(i = 0;i < 256; i++)
				{
					Ci_str_1_out[i] = 0;	
				}
				
				Ci_str_1_out[num_m] = Ci_str_1_out_tmp[num_m];
				
					for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)                                                 			
							{                                                                        			
							  plotbuff[i ] = Ci_str_1_out[i];
							  
							}
							DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
							PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, plotbuff,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
							VAL_BASE_ZERO_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);						
						
			}
				send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{

				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_6,ATTR_DIMMED ,1);
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_7,ATTR_DIMMED ,1);
				SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_8,ATTR_DIMMED ,1);

			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_1,&tr_led[1]); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_2,&tr_led[2]); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_3,&tr_led[3]); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_4,&tr_led[4]); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_5,&tr_led[5]); 
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TR_LED_6,&tr_led[6]);

	}

int CVICALLBACK create_export_force (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val,Vol,v,dv;
	int i,j,k;
	double dx,dt,dd;
	double ex0,ex1,ex2,ex3,ex4;
	double dist0,dist1,dist2,dist3,dist4;
	double amp0,amp1,amp2,amp3,amp4;
	double d1,d2;
	double decimat_23 = 8388607.0; 
	
	int length;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
		GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,&nota);
		
//		for(nota = 0; nota < 1; nota++)
		{
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_VOL,0);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,nota);
			set_nota (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_NOTA,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			for(Vol = 0; Vol < 127;Vol++)
			{
				v = (int)(Vol/32);
				dv = Vol - v*32;
			
				switch(v)
				{
					case 0:
					
					dt = ind_mult_0[nota] +  dv*(ind_mult_1[nota] - ind_mult_0[nota])/31;
				
					ex0 =   exp_e[0][0][nota] + dv*(exp_e[0][1][nota] - exp_e[0][0][nota] )/31;
					amp0 =  exp_a[0][0][nota] + dv*(exp_a[0][1][nota] - exp_a[0][0][nota] )/31;
					dist0 = exp_d[0][0][nota] + dv*(exp_d[0][1][nota] - exp_d[0][0][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex1 =   exp_e[1][0][nota] + dv*(exp_e[1][1][nota] - exp_e[1][0][nota] )/31;
					amp1 =  exp_a[1][0][nota] + dv*(exp_a[1][1][nota] - exp_a[1][0][nota] )/31;
					dist1 = exp_d[1][0][nota] + dv*(exp_d[1][1][nota] - exp_d[1][0][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex2 =   exp_e[2][0][nota] + dv*(exp_e[2][1][nota] - exp_e[2][0][nota] )/31;
					amp2 =  exp_a[2][0][nota] + dv*(exp_a[2][1][nota] - exp_a[2][0][nota] )/31;
					dist2 = exp_d[2][0][nota] + dv*(exp_d[2][1][nota] - exp_d[2][0][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex3 =   exp_e[3][0][nota] + dv*(exp_e[3][1][nota] - exp_e[3][0][nota] )/31;
					amp3 =  exp_a[3][0][nota] + dv*(exp_a[3][1][nota] - exp_a[3][0][nota] )/31;
					dist3 = exp_d[3][0][nota] + dv*(exp_d[3][1][nota] - exp_d[3][0][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex4 =   exp_e[4][0][nota] + dv*(exp_e[4][1][nota] - exp_e[4][0][nota] )/31;
					amp4 =  exp_a[4][0][nota] + dv*(exp_a[4][1][nota] - exp_a[4][0][nota] )/31;
					dist4 = exp_d[4][0][nota] + dv*(exp_d[4][1][nota] - exp_d[4][0][nota] )/31; 

					
					break;
					case 1:
					
					dt =  ind_mult_1[nota]    +  dv*(ind_mult_2[nota] - ind_mult_1[nota])/31;
					ex0 =   exp_e[0][1][nota] + dv*(exp_e[0][2][nota] - exp_e[0][1][nota] )/31;
					amp0 =  exp_a[0][1][nota] + dv*(exp_a[0][2][nota] - exp_a[0][1][nota] )/31;
					dist0 = exp_d[0][1][nota] + dv*(exp_d[0][2][nota] - exp_d[0][1][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex1 =   exp_e[1][1][nota] + dv*(exp_e[1][2][nota] - exp_e[1][1][nota] )/31;
					amp1 =  exp_a[1][1][nota] + dv*(exp_a[1][2][nota] - exp_a[1][1][nota] )/31;
					dist1 = exp_d[1][1][nota] + dv*(exp_d[1][2][nota] - exp_d[1][1][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex2 =   exp_e[2][1][nota] + dv*(exp_e[2][2][nota] - exp_e[2][1][nota] )/31;
					amp2 =  exp_a[2][1][nota] + dv*(exp_a[2][2][nota] - exp_a[2][1][nota] )/31;
					dist2 = exp_d[2][1][nota] + dv*(exp_d[2][2][nota] - exp_d[2][1][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex3 =   exp_e[3][1][nota] + dv*(exp_e[3][2][nota] - exp_e[3][1][nota] )/31;
					amp3 =  exp_a[3][1][nota] + dv*(exp_a[3][2][nota] - exp_a[3][1][nota] )/31;
					dist3 = exp_d[3][1][nota] + dv*(exp_d[3][2][nota] - exp_d[3][1][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex4 =   exp_e[4][1][nota] + dv*(exp_e[4][2][nota] - exp_e[4][1][nota] )/31;
					amp4 =  exp_a[4][1][nota] + dv*(exp_a[4][2][nota] - exp_a[4][1][nota] )/31;
					dist4 = exp_d[4][1][nota] + dv*(exp_d[4][2][nota] - exp_d[4][1][nota] )/31; 
					
					break;
					case 2:
					
					dt =  ind_mult_2[nota] +  dv*(ind_mult_3[nota] - ind_mult_2[nota])/31;
					
				
					ex0 =   exp_e[0][2][nota] + dv*(exp_e[0][3][nota] - exp_e[0][2][nota] )/31;
					amp0 =  exp_a[0][2][nota] + dv*(exp_a[0][3][nota] - exp_a[0][2][nota] )/31;
					dist0 = exp_d[0][2][nota] + dv*(exp_d[0][3][nota] - exp_d[0][2][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex1 =   exp_e[1][2][nota] + dv*(exp_e[1][3][nota] - exp_e[1][2][nota] )/31;
					amp1 =  exp_a[1][2][nota] + dv*(exp_a[1][3][nota] - exp_a[1][2][nota] )/31;
					dist1 = exp_d[1][2][nota] + dv*(exp_d[1][3][nota] - exp_d[1][2][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex2 =   exp_e[2][2][nota] + dv*(exp_e[2][3][nota] - exp_e[2][2][nota] )/31;
					amp2 =  exp_a[2][2][nota] + dv*(exp_a[2][3][nota] - exp_a[2][2][nota] )/31;
					dist2 = exp_d[2][2][nota] + dv*(exp_d[2][3][nota] - exp_d[2][2][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex3 =   exp_e[3][2][nota] + dv*(exp_e[3][3][nota] - exp_e[3][2][nota] )/31;
					amp3 =  exp_a[3][2][nota] + dv*(exp_a[3][3][nota] - exp_a[3][2][nota] )/31;
					dist3 = exp_d[3][2][nota] + dv*(exp_d[3][3][nota] - exp_d[3][2][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex4 =   exp_e[4][2][nota] + dv*(exp_e[4][3][nota] - exp_e[4][2][nota] )/31;
					amp4 =  exp_a[4][2][nota] + dv*(exp_a[4][3][nota] - exp_a[4][2][nota] )/31;
					dist4 = exp_d[4][2][nota] + dv*(exp_d[4][3][nota] - exp_d[4][2][nota] )/31; 
					
					break;
					case 3:
					
					dt =  ind_mult_3[nota] +  dv*(ind_mult_4[nota] - ind_mult_3[nota])/31;
					ex0 =   exp_e[0][3][nota] + dv*(exp_e[0][4][nota] - exp_e[0][3][nota] )/31;
					amp0 =  exp_a[0][3][nota] + dv*(exp_a[0][4][nota] - exp_a[0][3][nota] )/31;
					dist0 = exp_d[0][3][nota] + dv*(exp_d[0][4][nota] - exp_d[0][3][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex1 =   exp_e[1][3][nota] + dv*(exp_e[1][4][nota] - exp_e[1][3][nota] )/31;
					amp1 =  exp_a[1][3][nota] + dv*(exp_a[1][4][nota] - exp_a[1][3][nota] )/31;
					dist1 = exp_d[1][3][nota] + dv*(exp_d[1][4][nota] - exp_d[1][3][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex2 =   exp_e[2][3][nota] + dv*(exp_e[2][4][nota] - exp_e[2][3][nota] )/31;
					amp2 =  exp_a[2][3][nota] + dv*(exp_a[2][4][nota] - exp_a[2][3][nota] )/31;
					dist2 = exp_d[2][3][nota] + dv*(exp_d[2][4][nota] - exp_d[2][3][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex3 =   exp_e[3][3][nota] + dv*(exp_e[3][4][nota] - exp_e[3][3][nota] )/31;
					amp3 =  exp_a[3][3][nota] + dv*(exp_a[3][4][nota] - exp_a[3][3][nota] )/31;
					dist3 = exp_d[3][3][nota] + dv*(exp_d[3][4][nota] - exp_d[3][3][nota] )/31; 
				
					ex4 =   exp_e[4][3][nota] + dv*(exp_e[4][4][nota] - exp_e[4][3][nota] )/31;
					amp4 =  exp_a[4][3][nota] + dv*(exp_a[4][4][nota] - exp_a[4][3][nota] )/31;
					dist4 = exp_d[4][3][nota] + dv*(exp_d[4][4][nota] - exp_d[4][3][nota] )/31; 
					
					break;
			
				}
				decimat_23 = 65500.0; 
			
				dd = 0.5*(int)(dt*decimat_23);
			
			
				decimat_23 = 65000.0; 
			
				dist0 = dist0*decimat_23;
				dist1 = dist1*decimat_23;
				dist2 = dist2*decimat_23;
				dist3 = dist3*decimat_23;
				dist4 = dist4*decimat_23;
			
			
				decimat_23 = 2*1048576.0;
			
			
			
				ex0 = ex0/decimat_23;
				ex1 = ex1/decimat_23;
				ex2 = ex2/decimat_23;
				ex3 = ex3/decimat_23;
				ex4 = ex4/decimat_23;
			
				k = 0;
				i = 0;
			
			
//			if(dd == 0){dd = 0.0001;}
			
			while(k < 1000000000)
			{   
				i++;
				length = i;
	//			if(i == 1000000){ break;} 
				k = k + dd;	
				
			}
			
				dx = (double)length/(double)k;
			
				dt = dt*10; 
			
				for(i = 0; i < length; i++)
			{	
			
	 		    Foorce[Vol][i] =  amp0*exp(-1.0*ex0*((double)i*dt - dist0)*((double)i*dt - dist0)) +
							amp1*exp(-1.0*ex1*((double)i*dt - dist1)*((double)i*dt - dist1)) + 
							amp2*exp(-1.0*ex2*((double)i*dt - dist2)*((double)i*dt - dist2)) + 
							amp3*exp(-1.0*ex3*((double)i*dt - dist3)*((double)i*dt - dist3)) ; 
				
			
			   
			}
			

				SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,(double)5000);    
			//	SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,(double)length/10.0);
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
				
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Foorce[Vol],  50000, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);  
			
			
			//	PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Foorce[Vol],  length/10.0, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
			//	VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);  
			
			
	
			}
			
			
		//		SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_MODE, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,(double)length/10.0);
		//		DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 
			
		//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Foorce[127],  length/10.0, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
		//		VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW); 
			
		}
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK save_export_vozb (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			ArrayToFile("vozb_01.txt", Foorce, VAL_DOUBLE, 128*10000, 128, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_COLUMNS,VAL_SEP_BY_TAB, 8, VAL_ASCII, VAL_TRUNCATE ); 		
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK test_export_vozb (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,nn,ni;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		for(j = 0; j <88; j++)
			{	
				nn = (int)j/2;
				ni = j - nn*2;
				for(i = 0; i < 256; i++)
				{
					  Ci_coeff_cos[nn][i + ni*256] = 0.01; 
					  Ci_coeff_cos_tmp[nn][i + ni*256] = 0.01; 
				
				
				}
			}
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, Foorce[val], 10000, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
				VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_YELLOW);
								 

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK scroll_omega (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,m_num;
	double frq,step,ratio;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2, &m_num); 
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_SCROLL_OMEGA,omega_coef[m_num]);

			break;
			case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER_2, &m_num);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3,&frq);  
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_15, &step);   
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TEST, &ratio); 
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				omega_coef[m_num] = omega_coef[m_num] + (double)eventData2*step;
				
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				omega_coef[m_num] = omega_coef[m_num] - (double)eventData2*step;
			
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_99,omega_coef[m_num]);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_SCROLL_OMEGA,omega_coef[m_num]); 
			Send_Omega(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_5,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);  
										
								elements = 128;					
								
								DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
								PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, omega_coef,  elements, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_VERTICAL_BAR, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
								Delay(0.01);
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK set_pedal_curve (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	double plotbuff[128];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			param_num = 560;
			SetAxisScalingMode(panelHandle,PANEL_GRAPH, VAL_BOTTOM_XAXIS  ,VAL_MANUAL,0.0,127);
			
			for(i = 0; i < 128;i++)
			{
			plotbuff[i] = (double)pedal_out[i];	
			}
			
			
				DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );  
				
				                                                                                     						
						PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, plotbuff,128, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
						VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_WHITE);  
						

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK Construct_Pitch (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int temp[88][12];
	int i,j, k;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			////////////		
					FileToArray("D:/CVI_PIANOID_FANERA/Pitch.txt", temp, VAL_INTEGER, 88*12, 88, VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER,
				VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS ,VAL_ASCII);
					
					
					// Pitch [nota][strr_num]//[length]
											 //[mashinka ]
											 //[num in mashinka]]
											 //[start point]
					for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
					{
						for(k = 0;k < 4; k++)
						{  
							 Pitch[i][0][k] = temp[i][k];
							 Pitch[i][1][k] = temp[i][k +4];
							 Pitch[i][2][k] = temp[i][k +8];
						}
				
					}
			;			
			
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				for(j = 0; j < 512; j++)
				{
					shape_512[i][j] = 0;	
				}
			}
	/*

			Pitch[0][1][3] = 464; //length

			Pitch[0][2][1] = 0; //num_str

			Pitch[1][1][2] = 0; //start_point

			Pitch[1][2][1] = 0; //num_str
			Pitch[1][2][2] = 0; //start_point

	*/
					
			break;

int CVICALLBACK GenShape_4 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
		double tt[1000];
		double out_array[512];
	double tt_sdv[256];
	double ww;
	double ddel;
	double ddd,dx;
	double a,b,c, D;
	double X[1000],Y[1000], x_val;
	double secondDerivatives[1000];
	int i,j,k,colour;
	int val;
	int length,start_point;
	int numberOfElements;
	double xmax,xmin,ceil;
	int imax,imin;
	int mute,shift;
	int m0,m1,m2;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_15, &val);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_102, &ceil);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_22, &mute);
			

			ww = width[39];
			ddel = del[39];
			numberOfElements  = 1000;
			dx = 1.0/256.0;
		
		
		
	//		length = 340;
				
				for (i = 0; i < numberOfElements; i++)
				{
					
					c = 0.5 - (double)i/(double)numberOfElements;
					
					a = c*c - 2*ddel*c + ddel*ddel + ww*ww - 1;
					
					D = 4*ww*ww - 4*a;
					if(D < 0){D =0;}
					tt[i] = (2*ww + sqrt(D))/2;
					if(tt[i] < 0) tt[i] = 0;
					   
					
				}
			
			
		
		MaxMin1D(tt, numberOfElements, &xmax,&imax,&xmin,&imin); 
			
			if(xmax < -1.0*xmin){b = -1.0/xmin;}else{ b = 1.0/xmax; } 
				
			
						for(i = 0; i < numberOfElements; i ++)
						{
							 
							tt[i] = tt[i]*b; 
					
						}
						
						for(i = 0; i < numberOfElements; i ++)
						{
						
						
							if(tt[i] > ceil) tt[i] = ceil;
							
						}
			
  
		  
		 // shape_512
		  
//	for(i = Pitch[nota][0][3]; i < Pitch[nota][0][3] + Pitch[nota][0][0]; i++)
//	{
//		shape_512[Pitch[nota][0][1]][i] = out_array[i - Pitch[nota][0][3]];	
		
//	} 
//	Send_shape_512( Pitch[nota][0][1],shape_512[Pitch[nota][0][1]]); 
	

	
for(nota = 0; nota < 88; nota++)
{
 for(k = 0; k < 3; k++)
 {	  
	 	if(	Pitch[nota][k][0] < 0){break;}
		
		length =  Pitch[nota][0][0];   //length mashinka 0
		start_point = Pitch[nota][0][3]; 
		m0 =  Pitch[nota][0][1];  
		
		 dx = (double)numberOfElements/(double)length;
		  
		  for(i = 0; i < length; i++)
		  {
			  out_array[i] = tt[(int)((double)i*dx)];
	  
		  }
		  
		  DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );
		  PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE, out_array,  length, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
								VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_GREEN);
		  
	
	

	for(i = Pitch[nota][k][3]; i < Pitch[nota][k][3] + Pitch[nota][k][0]; i++)
	{
		shape_512[Pitch[nota][k][1]][i] = out_array[i - Pitch[nota][k][3]];	
		
	} 
	
//	Send_shape_512( Pitch[nota][k][1],shape_512[Pitch[nota][k][1]]); 
	DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 	
	DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW ); 	     
	DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );	
	switch (k)
		 {
			case 0:
			
			
				colour = VAL_YELLOW;
		//		PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, shape_512[Pitch[nota][k][1]],  512, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
		//							VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, colour);
				
				for(i = 0; i < 3;i++)
				{
		//		PlotLine(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_3, (double)shteg_pack4[i][nota], 0, (double)shteg_pack4[i][nota],1.0,colour);
				}
				break;
			
			case 1:
				colour = VAL_RED;
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, shape_512[Pitch[nota][k][1]],  512, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
									VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, colour);
				for(i = 0; i < 3;i++)
				{
		//		PlotLine(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_2, (double)shteg_pack4[i][nota], 0, (double)shteg_pack4[i][nota],1.0,colour);
				}
				break;
			case 2:
				colour = VAL_GREEN;
				PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, shape_512[Pitch[nota][k][1]],  512, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
									VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, colour);
				for(i = 0; i < 3;i++)
				{
		//		PlotLine(panelHandle, PANEL_MODE_4, (double)shteg_pack4[i][nota], 0, (double)shteg_pack4[i][nota],1.0,colour);
				}				
				break;
		 }
	
	
		 
 
		
  
					
}		
			///////////spline interp
}

			break;
	}
	return 0;

}

int CVICALLBACK gen_shteg (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,j,k,length,start_point,p;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			for(nota = 0; nota < 88; nota++)
			{
			   for(k = 0; k < 3;k++)
			   {
				if(	Pitch[nota][k][0] < 0){break;}
				
				shteg_pack4[k][nota] = Pitch[nota][k][3] + Pitch[nota][k][0] - (int)(Pitch[nota][k][0]/11);

			   }
			}
			Pitch_2_mashinka();
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK mass_adj (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	
	char *char_val;
	
	double val;
	
	
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_GOT_FOCUS:
			focus = 1;
			r_focus = 0; 
		//	char_val = StrDup ("Tn");
			param_num = 570;
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_36,&Harmonic);
			
			draw_params(panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH,EVENT_VAL_CHANGED,0,0,0);
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_MASS,Mass[Harmonic]);
		//	SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STRING_PARAM,char_val);
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MASS,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
		
		break;
		
	case EVENT_LOST_FOCUS:
		
		focus = 0;
		r_focus = 0;
		
		
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MASS,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_WHITE);
		break;
		
	case EVENT_MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL:
		
		if(focus == 1)
		{
			
		 if(r_focus == 0)
		 {
			
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				Mass[Harmonic] = Mass[Harmonic] + eventData2*step[12];
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				Mass[Harmonic] = Mass[Harmonic] - eventData2*step[12];		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_MASS,Mass[Harmonic]);
			
			
			
			send_mass();
			
		
			Delay(0.1);
			
		 }
		 
		 if(r_focus == 1)
		 {
			 if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_UP)
			{
				step[12] = step[12]*10;	
			}
			if(eventData1 ==  MOUSE_WHEEL_SCROLL_DOWN)
			{
				step[12] = step[12]*0.1;		
			}
			
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_MASS,step[12]);  
			 
		 }
		 
	
		 
		 DeleteGraphPlot (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, -1, VAL_IMMEDIATE_DRAW );                     						
					                                                                                           						
		 PlotY (panelHandle, PANEL_GRAPH, Mass,  256, VAL_DOUBLE,                           						
		 VAL_THIN_LINE, VAL_SOLID_DIAMOND, VAL_SOLID, 1, VAL_CYAN);
		 
		 
		 
		}
		
		break;
		
		case EVENT_RIGHT_CLICK:
			if(focus == 1)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MASS,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_RED); 
			r_focus = 1; 
			}
		
		break;
		
			case EVENT_LEFT_CLICK:
			SetCtrlAttribute(panelHandle, PANEL_MASS,ATTR_TEXT_BGCOLOR,VAL_CYAN); 
			r_focus = 0; 
		
		break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK ch_gain (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,tr_num;
		double val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_TRANSDUCER,&tr_num);
			GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_NUMERIC_22,&val); 
			
			for(i =0;i<128;i++)
			{
			
				decka_coeff[tr_num][i] =  val*decka_coeff_temp[tr_num][i]; 
			}
			
				send_deka_coeff(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_62,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK force_string_osc (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0}; 

	switch (event)
		
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
		
			GetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_RING_15, &val);
			Gain_FB[0] = (double)val;
	
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_notes_off; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[7]	 = val & 0x000000FF; 
			sendBuff[8]  = 0x00;
			sendBuff[9]  = 0x23;
		
		UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10);  	
			
	//		ComWrt (comport, sendBuff, 10);	
	
			others[6] = val;
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK sweep_frq (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
		double frq,start_frq,end_frq,step;
	
	switch (event)
	{
		
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			//	GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_FRQ,&frq);
			
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_START_FRQ,&start_frq);
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_END_FRQ,&end_frq);
				GetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_STEP_FRQ,&step);
				
				frq = start_frq;
				
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3,start_frq);
		//		send_gen_frq(panelHandle,PANEL_FRQ,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
		//		Sleep(300);
				
			
			while(frq < end_frq)
			{
			
				
				frq = frq*step;
				SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3,frq);
				send_gen_frq(panelHandle,PANEL_COMPRESS_GAIN_3,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
				Sleep(20);
				
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[7]	 = split_nota & 0x000000FF; 
			sendBuff[8]  = 0x00;
			sendBuff[9]  = 0x23;
		
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10); 
	
		
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK toggle_p0 (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_3, 0) ;
			FIR_sel( panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_3,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_P,0);
			SetCtrlAttribute(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_P0,ATTR_CMD_BUTTON_COLOR ,VAL_YELLOW);
			SetCtrlAttribute(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_P1,ATTR_CMD_BUTTON_COLOR ,VAL_GRAY);
			SetCtrlAttribute(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_P2,ATTR_CMD_BUTTON_COLOR ,VAL_GRAY);
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)

		case EVENT_COMMIT:

			SetCtrlAttribute(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_P0,ATTR_CMD_BUTTON_COLOR ,VAL_GRAY);

			break;

int CVICALLBACK toggle_p2 (int panel, int control, int event,

			SetCtrlVal(panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_3, 2) ;
			FIR_sel( panelHandle, PANEL_RINGSLIDE_3,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			SetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_P,2);

			SetCtrlAttribute(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_P1,ATTR_CMD_BUTTON_COLOR ,VAL_GRAY);

int CVICALLBACK set_pedal_params (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int ped;
	char sendBuff[2000] = {0};   
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_P,&ped);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_NUMERIC_PEDAL_PARAMS,&Pedal_params[ped]);  
			
			sendBuff[0]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[1]	 = 0x21;
			sendBuff[2]	 = 0x22;
		
			sendBuff[3]	 = cmd0_fb_input; 
			sendBuff[4]	 = 0x04; 
			sendBuff[5]	 = 0x00; 
			sendBuff[6]	 = 0x01; 
			sendBuff[7]	 = Pedal_params[ped] & 0x000000FF; 
			sendBuff[8]  = 0x00;
			sendBuff[9]  = 0x23;
		
			
			UDPWrite(writerChannel, 5555, destAddr, sendBuff, 10); 
	

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK tab_noise (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
	   GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_NOISE,&val);
	   SetCtrlVal(panelHandle,PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_NOISE,val);  
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK edit_visible (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i,val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_VISIBLE,&val);
			for(i = 2; i < 80; i++)
			{
			SetCtrlAttribute(tabPanelHandle,i,	ATTR_VISIBLE ,val);
				
			}

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK load_microtune (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	char micro_path_name[MAX_PATHNAME_LEN];
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			
			FileSelectPopup ("",  "*.txt",  "*.txt", "choose microtune", VAL_SELECT_BUTTON ,0, 1, 1, 0, micro_path_name);

			 	FileToArray (micro_path_name, ttn_micro, VAL_DOUBLE, 88, 1,
				VAL_GROUPS_TOGETHER, VAL_GROUPS_AS_ROWS, VAL_ASCII);
			

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK toggle_TN (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
			{
				ttn[i] = ttn_orig[i];	
			}
			send_nl(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_8,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			SetCtrlAttribute(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_TTN,ATTR_CMD_BUTTON_COLOR ,VAL_GREEN); 
			SetCtrlAttribute(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MICRO,ATTR_CMD_BUTTON_COLOR ,VAL_GRAY);  
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK toggle_MICRO (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int i, order,split;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_SPLIT_MICRO,&split);
			GetCtrlVal(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_SPLIT_ORDER_MICRO,&order);
			if(split == 0)
			{
				for(i = 0; i < 88; i++)
				{
					ttn[i] = ttn_micro[i];	
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if(order == 0)  //lo part
				{
					for(i = 0; i < 48; i++)
					{
						ttn[i] = ttn_micro[i];	
					}
					for(i = 48; i < 88; i++)
					{
						ttn[i] = ttn_orig[i];	
					} 
				}
				else
				{
					for(i = 0; i < 48; i++)
					{
						ttn[i] = ttn_orig[i];	
					}
					for(i = 48; i < 88; i++)
					{
						ttn[i] = ttn_micro[i];	
					} 
					
				}
				
				
			}
			send_nl(panelHandle, PANEL_COMMANDBUTTON_8,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);
			SetCtrlAttribute(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_TTN,ATTR_CMD_BUTTON_COLOR ,VAL_GRAY); 
			SetCtrlAttribute(tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_MICRO,ATTR_CMD_BUTTON_COLOR ,VAL_BLUE);  
			
			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK tab_hide (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_35,0); 
			show_hide_picture (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON_35,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0); 

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}

int CVICALLBACK tab_reset (int panel, int control, int event,
		void *callbackData, int eventData1, int eventData2)
{
	int val;
	switch (event)
	{
		case EVENT_COMMIT:
			GetCtrlVal (tabPanelHandle,TABPANEL_RESET, &val); 
			SetCtrlVal (panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON, val); 
			Reset(panelHandle, PANEL_TOGGLEBUTTON,EVENT_COMMIT,0,0,0);    

			break;
	}
	return 0;
}
